# DONE

- [2026-03-04] **Module-scoped namespaces — compiler phases 1–4 (core implementation)**: File-based modules now preserve identity through the semantic analysis pipeline. (1) AST: Added `Item::Module { path, items }` variant wrapping non-entry module items. (2) Loader: `merge_modules()` wraps non-entry modules in `Item::Module` instead of flattening them, carrying the logical import path through `load_recursive`. (3) Scope: Added `ScopeKind::FileModule { path }`, `names_in_current_scope()`, and `export_non_private()` to `ScopeTable`. (4) All exhaustive `match item.node` sites updated: `resolve.rs` `collect_top_level_inner` pushes FileModule scope, defines items, exports non-private to parent, pops; `resolve_item_body` recurses into nested items so function bodies are properly resolved (critical bug fix: without this, identifiers inside module functions weren't in the resolution map, breaking the struct-literal rewrite pass and causing missing `MoveZero` GIR instructions → use-after-free in tensor tests); `rewrite.rs`, `meta.rs`, `typecheck.rs`, `borrow.rs`, `traits.rs`, `derive.rs`, `formatter/mod.rs` all handle `Item::Module`. (5) GIR lowering: `lower_module` computes a flat view of all items (flattening `Item::Module` wrappers) before the existing lowering loop, preserving all downstream behavior. Result: 660 unit tests + 482 integration tests pass (only 2 pre-existing httpserver failures remain, unrelated to this change). Phase 5 (GIR name mangling) and Phase 6 (lib prefix cleanup) remain as future work.

- [2026-03-04] **JSON body/response support for `gg.httpserver`**: Added `from gg.json import Json, json_parse, json_stringify` to httpserver.gg. Added `req.json() -> Result[Json, str]` to `equip HttpRequest` — delegates directly to `json_parse(self.body)`, content-type agnostic. Added `HttpServerResponse.of_json(Json val)` factory — calls `json_stringify(val).str()` and sets `Content-Type: application/json`. Key finding: Gorget `{}` string interpolation conflicts with JSON object literal notation `{...}`, so JSON values must be built via `Dict[str, Json]` + `json_stringify` rather than raw string literals. New fixture: `httpserver_json.gg` (valid JSON parse → correct fields; invalid JSON → Err; of_json content-type; of_json body round-trip; wire-level POST→echo→parse). 469 integration tests pass.

- [2026-03-04] **Phase 6: Server Lifecycle for `gg.httpserver`**: (6A) Per-connection max requests: added `max_requests_per_conn: int` field to `HttpServer` (0 = unlimited); added `HttpServer.with_max_requests(host, port, max_req)` factory; added `http_server_handle_conn_ka_limited` and `http_server_handle_conn_tls_ka_limited` (4-param variants with request counting and `server_wants_close` logic); updated `serve_plain` and `serve_tls` to spawn `_limited` variants passing `self.max_requests_per_conn`; kept original 3-param `http_server_handle_conn_ka`/`http_server_handle_conn_tls_ka` for backward-compatible external use. (6B) `accept_n` method: `HttpServer.accept_n(handler, n)` binds the port, accepts exactly `n` connections using `_limited` handler, waits for all to finish via `TaskGroup.join()`, then closes the server socket. New fixture: `httpserver_lifecycle.gg` (serve_n two GET requests → both 200; max_requests_per_conn=1 → server closes after 1st request on keep-alive socket). 660 unit + 468 integration tests pass.

- [2026-03-04] **Phase 5: Static File Enhancements for `gg.httpserver`**: Added `http_etag(str content) -> str` — computes SHA-1 hash of file content via `crypto_sha1(bytes_from_str(content))` and returns it as a double-quoted hex string (RFC 7232 format). Updated `http_serve_file` to: (1) compute ETag for every file read and add it to the `200 OK` response headers; (2) check `if-none-match` header — return `304 Not Modified` with ETag header when it matches; (3) parse `Range: bytes=start-end` (or `bytes=start-`) header — return `206 Partial Content` with `Content-Range` header and the requested byte slice. Added imports `bytes_from_str`, `bytes_to_hex` from `std.bytes` and `crypto_sha1` from `gg.crypto`. New fixture: `httpserver_static_enhanced.gg` (200+ETag, 304 on match, 200 on miss, range `0-3`, range `5-end`). 660 unit + 467 integration tests pass.

- [2026-03-04] **Phase 4: Body Parsers & Cookies for `gg.httpserver`**: Added `parse_form_body(str body) -> Dict[str, str]` — splits on `&`, decodes key/value pairs via `form_decode` (`+` → space, `%XX` → byte). Added `parse_cookies(str header) -> Dict[str, str]` — splits on `;`, trims, splits at first `=`, no encoding (cookie values are raw). Extended `equip HttpRequest` with `form_params() -> Dict[str, str]` (delegates to `parse_form_body(self.body)`) and `cookies() -> Dict[str, str]` (reads `self.headers["cookie"]` and delegates to `parse_cookies`, returns empty dict if header absent). New fixture: `httpserver_body_parsers.gg` (form `+` decoding, `%20` decoding, multi-cookie parsing, no-cookie empty dict, empty body empty dict). 660 unit + 466 integration tests pass.

- [2026-03-04] **Phase 3: Routing Enhancements for `gg.httpserver`**: (3A) Wildcard routes: added `wildcard_routes: Dict[str, Callable[...]]` field to `Router`; `route()` now detects `*` prefix segments and stores them there; added `http_router_match_wildcard()` module-level helper that finds the `*name` segment, verifies preceding segments match exactly, and captures all remaining path parts joined by `/` into `params["name"]`; added wildcard dispatch as step 2b in `dispatch_inner` (between param match and HEAD auto-handling); updated `allowed_methods()` to scan `wildcard_routes` in addition to `exact_routes` and `param_routes`. (3B) Prefix mounting: added `prefix_mount(&self, str prefix, Router sub)` method that copies all exact, param, and wildcard routes from `sub` into `self` with `prefix` prepended (middlewares not copied — parent controls the pipeline). New fixture: `httpserver_routing.gg` (wildcard match, wildcard non-match, prefix exact route, prefix param route, unmounted path → 404). 660 unit + 465 integration tests pass.

- [2026-03-04] **Phase 2: Pre-dispatch Middleware for `gg.httpserver`**: Added `pre_middlewares: Vector[Callable[Option[HttpServerResponse](HttpRequest)]]` field to `Router`. Added `router.before(hook)` method to register pre-dispatch hooks. Rewrote `dispatch()` to run `before` hooks first — first `Some(resp)` short-circuits (handler is skipped); post-dispatch middlewares still run on short-circuit responses for logging/CORS. Extracted `run_post_middlewares` helper to avoid code duplication. New fixture: `httpserver_before.gg`. 660 unit + 464 integration tests pass.

- [2026-03-04] **Phase 1: Protocol Correctness for `gg.httpserver`**: (1A) URL percent-decoding: `http_server_parse_request` (and TLS variant) now apply `url_decode` to path and query string after splitting; `parse_query_string` decodes each key and value individually. (1B) 405 Method Not Allowed: `dispatch_inner` now checks `allowed_methods()` before returning 404 — returns 405 with `Allow` header when the path exists under a different method. (1C) Chunked Transfer-Encoding: Added `http_server_parse_hex`, `http_server_read_body_chunked`, and TLS variant; `http_server_parse_request` now checks `Transfer-Encoding: chunked` before Content-Length. (1D) Request body size limit: Added `max_body_bytes: int = 16777216` (16MB) parameter to parse functions; added `max_body_bytes` field to `HttpServer` with `HttpServer.with_max_body()` factory. Updated `httpserver_router_extended` expected output from 404 to 405 (correct behavior). New fixtures: `httpserver_protocol.gg`, `httpserver_chunked.gg`. 660 unit + 463 integration tests pass.

- [2026-03-04] **`gg.http` + `gg.httpserver` REST completion, HEAD/OPTIONS auto-handling, large body test, and documentation**: (1) Added `http_patch` to `lib/gg/http.gg` completing the REST method set (`GET`/`POST`/`PUT`/`DELETE`/`PATCH`). (2) Added `put()`, `delete()`, `patch()` convenience methods to `Router` in `lib/gg/httpserver.gg` (all delegate to `route()`). (3) Added `HEAD` auto-handling in `dispatch_inner`: when no explicit HEAD route is registered, re-dispatches as GET and strips the response body. (4) Added `OPTIONS` auto-handling: `allowed_methods()` helper scans `exact_routes` and `param_routes` for all registered methods on a path; OPTIONS returns 200 with `Allow` header or 404 if no routes match. (5) Fixed `language-reference.md`: replaced the entirely wrong `gg.http` stub (which described a `Response`/`Client` API that doesn't exist) with correct docs for `HttpResponse`, all 5 client functions, and added a complete `gg.httpserver` section covering `HttpRequest`, `HttpServerResponse`, `Router`, middleware, TLS, static files, and connection handlers. (6) Three new integration test fixtures: `httpserver_methods.gg` (PUT/DELETE/PATCH routes + HEAD/OPTIONS auto-handling), `httpserver_large_body.gg` (8192-byte POST body via real TCP verifying `read_exact` multi-read path), `http_patch.gg` (end-to-end PATCH client+server). 660 unit + 461 integration tests pass.

- [2026-03-03] **Code review + exhaustive tests for `gg.http` and `gg.httpserver`**: Fixed 4 bugs and added 7 new integration test fixtures. **(1A) `redirect()` bug**: `resp.headers.put()` mutated a copy due to Dict value semantics — fixed by building the headers dict before passing it to the constructor. **(1B) `http_parse_url` redirect port loss**: relative redirects dropped the port number on reconstruction — fixed by including non-default port in the rebuilt URL. **(1C) Dead code removal**: Deleted unused `http_server_default_not_found` function. **(1D) DRY `get()`/`post()`**: Simplified to delegate to `route()`. **(Codegen fix — callable parameter → GorgetClosure assignment)**: Callable function parameters are lowered as `void*` in the C ABI (callers pass `(void*)(void*[2]){fn_ptr, env}`). When assigning such a parameter to a `GorgetClosure` local (`Callable[T] h = handler`), the C backend emitted `_5 = _4` which fails because `GorgetClosure` is a struct. Fixed `emit_instruction` `Assign` handler: when `dst_is_escaped_closure && src_c_type == "void"`, emit `*(GorgetClosure*)src` dereference. **(Borrow checker fix — `compute_method_call_origin`)**: Static factory calls like `HttpServerResponse.not_found()` produced `UnresolvedBorrowOrigin` errors because the type checker doesn't populate `method_resolutions` when the receiver is a struct type name (struct `DefInfo.type_id = None` → `infer_expr` returns `error_id` → `traits.resolve_method` fails). Fixed in `compute_method_call_origin`: when `method_resolutions` has no entry and the receiver resolves to `Struct`/`Enum`/`Newtype`, return `BorrowOrigin::Static` (static constructors always produce fresh values). Also added `BorrowOrigin::Static` return for callable variable calls (`DefKind::Variable`) and variant constructors (`DefKind::Variant`) in `compute_call_origin`. **(New fixtures)**: `httpserver_router.gg` (restored missing fixture), `httpserver_router_extended.gg`, `httpserver_response.gg`, `httpserver_query_string.gg`, `httpserver_parse_request.gg`, `http_urls_extended.gg`, `httpserver_e2e_extended.gg`. Updated `http_urls` integration test to include `tls` bool output. All 660 unit + 458 integration tests pass.

- [2026-03-04] **New meta/comptime builtins + `meta while`**: Extended the delayed meta system (evaluated at monomorphization time in generic bodies) with 11 new builtins and a new statement form. **Builtins**: `typeof(T)` (alias for typename), `bitwidth(T)` (8/16/32/64 for numeric types), `min_val(T)` / `max_val(T)` (integer range bounds as MetaValue::Int), `implements(T, "TraitName")` (checks both intrinsic numeric traits and user-defined equip blocks), `field_names(T)` / `field_count(T)` / `has_field(T,"name")` / `field_type(T,"name")` (struct reflection), `variant_names(T)` / `variant_count(T)` (enum reflection). **MetaValue::List**: New variant added to support list-returning builtins (`field_names`/`variant_names`); `meta for x in field_names(T):` now works, binding each field name as a MetaValue::Str usable inside nested meta if/match. **`meta while`**: New `Stmt::MetaWhile { condition, body }` AST node; parser (`parse_meta_while_stmt`), all 13 walker arms, and delayed evaluator with 100k iteration safety limit. Primary use: guard pattern where condition is false for instantiated types (0 iterations). **DelayedMetaContext** extended with `trait_registry` and `type_registry` references for reflection queries. **Test fixtures**: `meta_numeric_meta.gg`, `meta_implements.gg`, `meta_while.gg`, `meta_reflection.gg`. 660 unit + 458 integration tests pass.

- [2026-03-04] **Codegen bug: `return` inside `if` inside match arm emitted `goto if_end` instead of function return**: Root cause in `build_if_chain_expr` (`src/ir/lowering/exprs.rs`): after calling `lower_block_expr` on each branch body, it unconditionally emitted `assign(result_id, val)` + `jump(merge_bb)`, overwriting the `ret` terminator that `lower_return` had just set via `set_terminator`. Fix: guard each branch's post-body emit with `if !builder.is_terminated()`. Also fixed the related double-drop in `lower_if` (`src/ir/lowering/stmts.rs`): replaced the `block_always_returns` check (conservative, AST-level) with `builder.is_terminated()` (precise, GIR-level) for all three branch types (then/elif/else), mirroring the pattern already used in `lower_match_stmt`. This prevents `pop_scope` from emitting drop instructions that `emit_early_exit_drops` already emitted inside `lower_return`. Removed the now-dead `block_always_returns` helper. Regression test: `tests/fixtures/return_in_if_in_match.gg`. 454 integration tests pass.

- [2026-03-04] **`std.datetime`: `dt_days_from_epoch` pre-epoch bug**: The while loop `while dt_y < dt_year` in `lib/std/datetime.gg` never executed for years < 1970 (loop starts at `dt_y=1970`, condition `dt_y < dt_year` is false for e.g. 1969). Fix: split into two branches — for `dt_year >= 1970`, count days forward as before; for `dt_year < 1970`, count backward by subtracting each year's day count from the epoch, starting at `dt_year` and walking up to 1969 inclusive. Then add `day_of_year - 1` in both cases. Verified: `dt_days_from_epoch(1969, 12, 31) = -1`, `(1969, 1, 1) = -365`, `(1968, 12, 31) = -366`, `(1968, 1, 1) = -731`. `to_epoch()` and round-trip through `from_epoch_utc` also correct for pre-epoch dates. Added pre-epoch test cases to `tests/fixtures/datetime_extended.gg`.

- [2026-03-03] **DataFrame: `meta match` + TypedColumn[T] refactor + `df_cast`/`df_clip`**: Three-part improvement to the Gorget dataframe library. **Part 1 — `meta match` statement**: Added `meta match <expr>: case <val>: ... else: ...` as a cleaner alternative to chained `meta if` for compile-time multi-way type dispatch inside generic function bodies. Added `Stmt::MetaMatch` AST variant, parser dispatch, walker arms in all 13 relevant files, `meta_values_eq` helper, and evaluator arm in `evaluate_delayed_meta_block`. Test fixture: `meta_delayed_match.gg`. **Part 2 — TypedColumn[T] refactoring**: Introduced `struct TypedColumn[T]` and 6 generic inner helpers (`col_slice_inner`, `col_filter_inner`, `col_gather_inner`, `col_gather_nullable_inner`, `col_compare_inner`, `value_as_str_inner`) to eliminate ~400 lines of 4-way type-dispatch boilerplate in `lib/gg/dataframe.gg`. Root cause fix: `map_type_with_subs` in `src/ir/lowering/context.rs` had a condition `!self.type_name_subs.is_empty()` that blocked fragment substitution for generic params (e.g., `Vector[T]` resolved to UNIT_TYPE); widened to `|| !self.generic_fragment_subs.is_empty()`. **Part 3 — `df_cast`/`df_clip`**: Added `df_cast(DataFrame, str col_name, str target_type) -> Result[DataFrame, str]` (int↔float↔str↔bool conversions; null propagation; error on unknown column or type) and `df_clip(DataFrame, str col_name, float lo, float hi) -> DataFrame` (clamp numeric values; preserve nulls; unchanged for non-numeric columns). Used per-type cast helper pattern and `df_replace_col` to work around the existing compiler bug where `return` inside `if` inside `match` arm generates incorrect goto. Test fixtures: `dataframe_cast.gg`, `dataframe_clip.gg`. All 16 dataframe integration tests pass. 453 total integration tests.

- [2026-03-03] **Delayed meta: Zig-style comptime in generic bodies (Phase 1+2)**: Added `meta if` and `meta for` statement constructs inside generic function bodies, evaluated at monomorphization time (when type parameters are concrete). Previously, meta evaluated only at Phase 0 (before semantic analysis), making it impossible to branch on generic type params. **New syntax**: `meta if typename(T) == "int": ... elif typename(T) == "float": ... else: ...` and `meta for i in 0..N: ...` inside generic functions/methods. **Implementation**: Added `Stmt::MetaIf` and `Stmt::MetaFor` AST variants, parser dispatch for the `meta` keyword in function bodies (`parse_meta_stmt`/`parse_meta_if_stmt`/`parse_meta_for_stmt`), walker arms in visitor/loader/formatter/generics/stmts; fixed resolver/typechecker/borrow-checker/rewriter to skip regular name resolution/type-inference on meta conditions (they're evaluated at monomorphization time, not runtime); added `DelayedMetaContext` struct, `evaluate_delayed_meta_block` (in-place splice with `Vec::splice`), and `eval_delayed_expr` (recurses through BinaryOp/UnaryOp, resolves `typename(T)` and `sizeof(T)` via type_subs, delegates to Phase 0 evaluator for literals/constants) in `src/semantic/meta.rs`; hooked into `lower_generic_function` in `src/ir/lowering/functions.rs` to evaluate delayed meta on a cloned template before GIR lowering. **Reflection builtins**: `typename(T)` resolves generic params to concrete names ("int", "float", "str", etc.); `sizeof(T)` resolves generic params then queries primitive size table. **Test fixtures**: `meta_delayed_basic.gg` (typename dispatch → integer/float/string/other), `meta_delayed_for.gg` (meta for unrolling + nested meta if), `meta_delayed_nested.gg` (nested meta if + sizeof). Files modified: `src/parser/ast.rs`, `src/parser/stmt.rs`, `src/parser/visitor.rs`, `src/parser/expr.rs`, `src/loader.rs`, `src/formatter/mod.rs`, `src/semantic/meta.rs`, `src/semantic/resolve.rs`, `src/semantic/typecheck.rs`, `src/semantic/rewrite.rs`, `src/semantic/borrow.rs`, `src/ir/lowering/generics.rs`, `src/ir/lowering/stmts.rs`, `src/ir/lowering/functions.rs`, `tests/integration.rs`. 660 unit + 450 integration tests pass.

- [2026-03-03] **`gg.dataframe` Phase 1+2: Bugs/correctness + Tier 2 features**: Fixed 10 correctness bugs and added 15+ new features to the dataframe library. **Phase 1 (bugs):** (1.1) Replaced O(n²) bubble sort with O(n log n) bottom-up merge sort using direct typed column comparison. (1.2) Fixed `sort_by` destroying column types — now uses `col_gather` to reorder by index preserving types/nulls. (1.3) Fixed `df_inner_join` destroying column types — same `col_gather` fix. (1.4) Fixed `df_concat` destroying types/nulls — replaced string round-trip with new `col_concat` function. (1.5) Fixed `apply_int/float/str` ignoring null masks — now checks `is_null` before applying, carries forward original mask. (1.6) Fixed `apply_*/fillna_*` changing column order — replaced `drop().add_column()` with new `replace_column` method. (1.7) Fixed `filter_by` string comparison issues — now dispatches on column dtype for typed comparison. (1.8) Added missing `col_bools_null` constructor. (1.9) Fixed `GroupBy.agg` coercing everything through float — now produces typed IntColumn/FloatColumn output. (1.10) Fixed `value_counts` O(n²) → O(n) using `Dict[str, int]`. **Phase 2 (features):** Added `rename_column`, `dtypes`, `isna`/`notna`, `df_unique`/`df_nunique`, `df_median`, `df_var`, `nlargest`/`nsmallest`, `sample`/`shuffle`, `sort_by_multi` (multi-column sort), `col_add`/`col_sub`/`col_mul` (column arithmetic), `df_left_join`/`df_right_join`/`df_outer_join`/`df_cross_join`, `df_group_by_multi` (multi-column groupby), `df_from_records_typed` (type-inferring records constructor). Infrastructure: `col_gather`, `col_concat`, `col_gather_nullable`, `replace_column`, `col_compare`, `merge_sort_idxs`. 4 new test fixtures (dataframe_tier2_basic/sort_arith/joins/groupby). 660 unit + 14 dataframe integration tests pass.

- [2026-03-03] **`mod` operator (true modulo) alongside `%` (remainder)**: Added `mod` keyword operator with true modulo semantics (result sign matches divisor), keeping `%` as C-style remainder (result sign matches dividend). Renamed `BinaryOp::Mod` → `BinaryOp::Rem` throughout AST/IR/codegen for naming consistency, added new `BinaryOp::Mod` for the keyword operator. Added `Mod[Out]` trait with `mod(self, Self rhs)` method alongside existing `Rem[Out]`. Extended `Numeric` composite trait to include `Mod`. Wired through all compiler stages: lexer (`mod` keyword), parser (same precedence as `%`), type checker, semantic analysis, IR lowering, constant folding (with correct modulo formula), C backend (inline sign-correction code for int/float), simulator, formatter, and self-hosting resolver fixture. Added `mod_rem.gg` integration test covering negative operand combinations for both operators. Updated language-reference.md. 660 unit + 443 integration tests pass.

- [2026-03-03] **`Numeric` trait + `One` trait + Tensor library deduplication**: Implemented a 6-phase plan to add composite `Numeric` trait (`Add + Sub + Mul + Div + Rem + Neg + Comparable + Default + One`), `One` identity trait, and intrinsic numeric trait satisfaction for all numeric primitives. Wired `T.one()` through all backend layers (type checker, IR lowering, C backend, simulator). Fixed `T.method()` resolution in generic function bodies (pre-existing bug where `T.default()` / `T.parse()` failed inside monomorphized bodies). Added trait bound propagation so `[Numeric T]` functions can call other `[Numeric T]` functions with `T` transitively. Fixed generic collector to skip unresolved type parameters during discovery and added transitive discovery phase for nested generic calls. Fixed generic function parameter drop elaboration (used `register_local` instead of `register_param`, causing double-free of borrowed GorgetArray params). Migrated tensor library from 26 duplicated `_int`/`_float` function pairs (~1109 lines) to single generic `[Numeric T]` functions (~733 lines). Updated all 8 tensor test fixtures. 660 unit + 442 integration tests pass.

- [2026-03-03] **Targeted integration tests for `gg.csv`, `gg.xml`, `gg.http` coverage gaps**: Added 3 new fixture files (`csv_edge.gg`, `xml_roundtrip.gg`, `http_urls.gg`) and wired them into `integration.rs` (440 total). `csv_edge` covers `has_column`/`column_index` (true/false), unterminated-quote error path, tricky roundtrip with embedded delimiter and `""` double-quote escaping, and empty-input `Ok`. `xml_roundtrip` covers entity encoding in text (`&lt;` `&amp;` `&gt;`) and attributes (`&amp;` `&quot;`) across stringify→re-parse roundtrips, mismatched-closing-tag error, processing-instruction skip (`<?foo?>`), and unterminated-comment error. `http_urls` covers `http_parse_url` (https default port 443, http explicit port, no-path default `/`, port + root path) and `http_parse_hex` (lower/upper/decimal/zero). **Also fixed two bugs**: (1) `src/main.rs` linker-flag detection used `source` (user file only) instead of `concat_source` (full transitive imports) — caused `gg.http`'s transitive `std.net.tls` dependency to be missed, so `-lssl`/`-lcrypto` were not added. (2) `gorget_try_parse_int` codegen passed non-null-terminated `Str.data` pointer to `strtoll`, causing port parse to fail on substrings like `"8080/path..."`. Added `gorget_try_parse_int_n(const char* s, size_t n)` length-bounded variant to runtime, and updated codegen to use it for `Str` operands. 658/658 unit + 440/440 integration tests pass.

- [2026-03-03] **Fix `int.parse()` / `float.parse()` on non-null-terminated `Str` slices**: `gorget_try_parse_int` and `gorget_try_parse_float` used `*endptr != '\0'` as the termination check, which fails when the `Str` pointer comes from `byte_slice()` (interior pointer into a larger allocation, not null-terminated). Example: `int.parse("18086")` on the port substring of `"127.0.0.1:18086/hello"` saw the trailing "/" and returned `None`, so `http_parse_url` fell back to port 80, causing all HTTP client requests to connect to the wrong port. Fix: updated both parse functions to accept `(const char* s, size_t len)` and check `(size_t)(endptr - s) == len` instead of null-termination. Updated `bool.parse()` to use `strncmp + length check` instead of `strcmp`. Updated `extract_str_data_len` closure in the codegen to emit `_N.data, _N.len` for `Str`/`GorgetString` operands (string literals use the literal length). This was the root cause of the `httpserver_e2e` hang: the client connected to port 80 instead of 18086, causing "Connection refused" for all requests; `serve_n` then blocked in `accept()` forever waiting for connections that never arrived. Added `tests/fixtures/httpserver_e2e.gg` end-to-end test (gg.http client vs gg.httpserver over real TCP: GET/POST/404/custom headers/query strings). All 658 unit + 447 integration tests pass.

- [2026-03-03] **`gg.httpserver` V2 — Phase D (TLS HTTPS server)**: Added `GorgetTlsServerSocket` C runtime (`TLS_SERVER_RUNTIME`): struct `{int fd; SSL_CTX* ctx}`, `gorget_tls_server_bind` (SSL_CTX_new + cert/key load + bind/listen), `gorget_tls_server_accept` (accept + SSL_new + SSL_accept, returns `GorgetTlsSocket` with `ctx=NULL`), `gorget_tls_server_close`. Added `gorget_tls_set_timeout` to `TLS_SOCKET_RUNTIME`. Extended `gen_tls_socket_module()` in `stdlib.rs`: added `TlsServerSocket` opaque struct, `tls_server_bind(host, port, cert_path, key_path) → Result[TlsServerSocket, str]`, `TlsServerSocket.accept()` and `TlsServerSocket.close()`, and `TlsSocket.set_timeout(ms)`. Wired in `mod.rs`: KNOWN_MAPPINGS (`tls_server_bind`, `TlsServerSocket__accept`, `TlsServerSocket__close`, `TlsSocket__set_timeout`), `is_cstr_param_fn` (`gorget_tls_server_bind`), `runtime_type_name` + `collection_type_alias` (`TlsServerSocket → GorgetTlsServerSocket`), preamble emission (TLS_SERVER_RUNTIME after TLS_SOCKET_RUNTIME when `needs_tls_server`), `last_error_fn` (TlsServerSocket check before TlsSocket to avoid `gorget_tls_server_` prefix collision). Added `needs_tls: bool` to `GirCodegenOutput` and updated `main.rs` to add `-lssl -lcrypto` when `gir_output.needs_tls` (fixes linker errors when TLS comes from imported library modules, not user source). Added TLS connection functions to `lib/gg/httpserver.gg`: `http_server_parse_request_tls`, `http_server_write_response_tls_ka`, `http_server_handle_conn_tls_ka`. Extended `HttpServer` struct with `use_tls/cert_path/key_path`, added `HttpServer.new_tls` constructor, split `serve()` into `serve_plain()` + `serve_tls()`. Added `httpserver_tls.gg` smoke test (compile + field access, no live TLS connection). All 446 integration tests pass.

- [2026-03-03] **`gg.httpserver` V2 — Phases C4 + E (Middleware, Static files) + Vector[Callable] codegen fix**: (A) **Phase C4 (Middleware)**: Added `middlewares: Vector[Callable[HttpServerResponse(HttpRequest, HttpServerResponse)]]` field to `Router`. Added `Router.use(hook)` method and split `dispatch` into `dispatch_inner` (routing only) + `dispatch` (applies middleware pipeline after route match). Hooks receive the original request and current response, returning the (possibly modified) response. Added `httpserver_middleware.gg` fixture (3 hooks: add header, append body suffix, method-tag header). (B) **Phase E (Static files)**: Added `http_mime_type(path)` (19 extensions) and `http_serve_file(base_dir, req)` (directory traversal protection, index.html fallback for directories). Added `httpserver_static.gg` fixture (MIME detection, file serving, 404, 400 traversal block, index.html). (C) **`Vector[Callable[...]]` codegen fix**: Three-part fix: (1) `mangle_type_for_name(Type::Function)` now returns `"GorgetClosure"` instead of `"unknown"`, so `Callable[T(P)]` mangled names use `Callable__GorgetClosure` not `Callable__unknown`. (2) `extract_collection_elem_type` now returns `"GorgetClosure"` when the element rest-string starts with `"Callable__"`, `"MutCallable__"`, or `"ConsumeCallable__"`, fixing `gorget_array_new(sizeof(...))` construction. (3) `IndexLoad` preprocessing pass now falls back to the raw IR type name (via `GirType::Named`) when `base_type` is normalized to `"GorgetArray"`, recovering the element type for `type_overrides` insertion — this ensures `Vector[Callable]` element locals are correctly declared as `GorgetClosure` not `int64_t`. (D) **`Router.use()` callable ABI fix**: Callable parameters passed as `void*` must be dereferenced to `GorgetClosure` via an intermediate variable before push (same convention as `route()/get()/post()`). Added `Callable[...] h = hook` in `use()`. All 445 integration tests pass.

- [2026-03-03] **`gg.httpserver` V2 — Phase C (Router, URL params, custom headers, query strings) + borrow checker fix**: (A) **Phase C (Router)**: Added `Router` struct with exact-match (`Dict[str, Callable]`) and parameterized routes (`Dict[str, Callable]`; key = `"METHOD|/path/:param"`). Added `route()`, `get()`, `post()`, `dispatch()` equip methods with URL path parameter capture (`http_router_match_pattern`). Also added `parse_query_string`, `HttpRequest.query_params()`, `HttpServerResponse.with_header()`, `HttpServerResponse.redirect()`, and custom response headers. (B) **Borrow checker fix — `UnresolvedBorrowOrigin` on static method calls**: `HttpServerResponse.not_found()` fired a spurious borrow error because the TypeChecker fails to add static equip method calls to `method_resolutions` (struct defs have `type_id = None`, so `infer_expr(Identifier("StructName"))` returns `error_id`, and `traits.resolve_method(error_id, ...)` fails). Fixed in `compute_method_call_origin`: when `method_resolutions` has no entry and the receiver resolves to a type name (`Struct`/`Enum`/`Import`/etc.), return `BorrowOrigin::Static` — a static constructor call can never produce a dangling reference. (C) **Runtime bug fix — use-after-free in Router dispatch**: `Dict[str, Vector[str]] param_patterns` caused heap-use-after-free: pulling `Vector[str]` from the dict with `dict[key]` creates a shallow copy (same data pointer); freeing the copy at scope exit freed the dict's internal storage; subsequent calls to `dispatch()` accessed freed memory. Fix: eliminated `param_patterns` entirely. Encoded the original route path directly in the param_routes key (`"METHOD|/path/:param"`), and re-derived pattern segments in `dispatch()` via `route_path.split("/")` — each iteration creates a fresh owned `Vector[str]`, safe to free. **Root cause is general**: any `Vector`/`Dict` value pulled from a container via `dict[key]` or `vec.get(i).unwrap()` is a shallow copy; freeing it corrupts the container's data. This is a latent bug in the runtime that only manifests when the same key is accessed multiple times. Added `tests/fixtures/httpserver_router.gg` + `tests/fixtures/dict_box_callable.gg` + integration tests. All 443 integration tests pass.

- [2026-03-03] **`gg.httpserver` — HTTP/1.1 server module (V1)**: Implemented full HTTP/1.1 server stack. (1) Added `GorgetServerSocket` C runtime (`SERVER_SOCKET_RUNTIME` const) with bind/listen/accept/close. Added `GorgetServerSocket` typedef at the end of `SOCKET_RUNTIME` so the type is always available even when `SERVER_SOCKET_RUNTIME` isn't emitted. (2) Extended `gen_socket_module()` in `stdlib.rs` with `ServerSocket` opaque type, `server_socket_bind` free function, and `accept`/`close` methods. Registered `httpserver` in `is_builtin_module`, `generate_builtin_module`, `builtin_module_source`. (3) Wired `server_socket_bind` → `gorget_server_socket_bind` in `mod.rs` KNOWN_MAPPINGS, `is_cstr_param_fn`, `last_error_fn`, `runtime_type_name`, `collection_type_alias`, and updated preamble emission logic. (4) Added plain-TCP path to `lib/gg/http.gg`: `http_parse_url` now returns a 4-tuple `(str, int, str, bool)` where the 4th element is `use_tls` (true for https://, false for http://). `http_single` branches on `use_tls` — TLS path unchanged, plain path uses `socket_connect`. Redirect handling uses `._3` flag instead of port check. (5) Created `lib/gg/httpserver.gg`: `HttpRequest`, `HttpServerResponse` structs; `http_server_parse_request`, `http_server_write_response`, `http_server_handle_conn` (takes `Socket conn` by value to avoid borrow re-borrowing constraint); `HttpServer.serve` (sequential V1 loop). (6) Added `tests/fixtures/httpserver_basic.gg` (one-shot server + raw socket client, outputs `true\ndone`) and `fn httpserver_basic()` in `tests/integration.rs`. All 658 unit + 438 integration tests pass.

- [2026-03-03] **Edge-case integration tests for `std.encoding`, `std.fmt`, `gg.toml`**: Added 3 new fixture files (`encoding_edge.gg`, `fmt_edge.gg`, `toml_edge.gg`) covering ~40 previously untested code paths: empty-string URL/HTML operations, form_decode error paths (incomplete percent, invalid hex), HTML named entities (ndash, mdash, hellip, euro, pound), unknown entity pass-through, bare trailing ampersand, UTF-8 validation on empty string, utf8_char_at out-of-bounds, latin1 out-of-range error, high-byte latin1 roundtrip; pad_left/pad_right with empty fill, center odd-padding and no-op cases, str_truncate max=0 and suffix-too-long cases, repeat negative count, join with empty vector, single element, and empty separator; TOML multiline basic strings (first-newline trim), literal strings (no escape processing), multiline literal strings, inline tables (with `{{}}` escaping in Gorget strings), negative integers and floats, empty arrays, boolean values, and basic-string escape sequences. **Also fixed**: `parse_number_or_date` in `gg/toml.gg` now handles signed special floats (`-inf`, `+inf`, `-nan`, `+nan`) by checking for "inf"/"nan" after consuming the sign character. **Logged**: `dt_days_from_epoch` pre-epoch bug and inline table string-interpolation gotcha (`{{` escape required) to TODO.md. 658/658 unit + 422/422 integration tests pass.

- [2026-03-03] **Integration test fixtures — second batch (9 new fixtures)**: Added `csv_stringify.gg`, `json_pretty.gg`, `xml_query.gg`, `yaml_multi.gg`, `uuid_props.gg`, `toml_datetime.gg`, `datetime_gaps.gg`, `ecs_advanced.gg`, `dataframe_extra.gg`. Covers `csv_stringify_delim`, `csv_stringify_table_delim` (roundtrip), `json_pretty` (Dict-constructor approach avoids string interpolation), `XmlNode.find`/`find_all`/`xml_stringify`, `yaml_parse_all`/`yaml_stringify_all`/`yaml_pretty`, `UUID.equals`/`version`, `TomlValue.is_datetime`, `DateTime.from_epoch`/`from_epoch_utc`/`day_of_year`/`add_minutes`, `EntityPool.max_id`/`SparseSet.try_get`/`each`, `df_from_records`/`DataFrame.row_as_dict`/`value_counts`. Fixed formatter idempotency issue by using Dict-constructor JSON instead of string literal with `\{`. 658/658 unit + 434/434 integration tests pass.

- [2026-03-03] **Wire up dead Vector methods `first`, `last`, `reversed`, `unique`, `binary_search`**: Registered all 5 methods across the full pipeline — typechecker (`src/semantic/typecheck.rs`), GIR lowering (`src/ir/lowering/exprs.rs`), C backend (`src/backend/c/mod.rs`), and C runtime (`src/backend/c/c_runtime.rs`). Added `InlineMethod::Reversed` and `InlineMethod::Unique` variants (clone+reverse / clone+sort+dedup). Added `__INLINE_ARRAY_FIRST__` / `__INLINE_ARRAY_LAST__` as inline Option-returning handlers (same pattern as `__INLINE_ARRAY_GET__`). Added `gorget_array_binary_search()` (binary search with memcmp) and `gorget_array_dedup()` (adjacent dedup) to the C runtime. Removed dead `flatten` from the Vector path. Fixed a subtle GIR bug: `first`/`last` needed the same `ensure_option_type_registered` + fn_sig override pass that `get` uses, otherwise the local's GIR TypeId was `I64_TYPE` while `type_overrides` had the correct `Option__int64_t`. Added `tests/fixtures/vector_first_last.gg` test. 658/658 unit + 414/414 integration tests pass.

- [2026-03-03] **Fix `DataFrame.sort_by()` float comparison bug**: Added `elif str_is_float(va) and str_is_float(vb)` branch to the bubble sort in `sort_by()` (`dataframe.gg:869`), mirroring the same fix already present in `filter_by()`. Float columns previously fell through to string comparison (`"10.5"` < `"9.0"` lexicographically). Now uses `parse_float()` for numeric ordering. Added float sort test to `dataframe_filter_sort.gg` fixture (values `9.0`, `10.5`, `2.3` → sorted `2.3`, `9.0`, `10.5`). Updated `tests/integration.rs`. All integration tests pass.

- [2026-03-03] **Comprehensive allocator test coverage + `scan_stmt` bug fix**: Added 10 new fixture files and integration tests covering (1) escape tests for FBA (assign + return), FallbackAllocator (assign), PoolAllocator (assign), TrackingAllocator (return); (2) mixed nested allocator tests (`alloc_nested_mixed.gg`: TLSF wrapping Arena; `tracking_wraps_arena.gg`: Tracker wrapping Arena); (3) first Dict test (`arena_dict.gg`); (4) first multi-collection test (`arena_multi_collection.gg`: Vector[int], Vector[str], Dict[str,int] sharing one Arena). **Bug fixed**: `scan_stmt` in `src/ir/lowering/generics.rs` silently skipped `Stmt::With`, `Stmt::Loop`, `Stmt::Unsafe`, and `Stmt::Assert` — collection types declared inside `with` blocks (e.g., `Dict[str, int]`) were never pre-registered during the generic-collector discovery pass, so `resolve_var_type` returned `UNIT_TYPE` and all method calls on those variables were silently dropped. Fixed by adding the missing arms to the `match`. Total allocator tests: 26 (was 17). 658/658 unit + 396/396 integration tests pass.

- [2026-03-03] **gg.tensor + gg.dataframe coverage gaps closed**: (A) **Added `col_floats_null`** to `lib/gg/dataframe.gg` (mirrors `col_ints_null`/`col_strs_null`). (B) **Fixed `df_from_records` bug** — used column-major `col_data.get(ci).unwrap().push(val)` pattern which mutates a copy (value semantics), leaving all columns empty. Rewrote to iterate columns outer / rows inner, building each column's values directly — same approach as `df_from_csv`. (C) **Extended `tensor_extra.gg`** with 6 float comparison spot checks (`tensor_gt_int`, `tensor_lt_float`, `tensor_gt_float`, `tensor_le_float`, `tensor_ge_float`, `tensor_ne_float`). (D) **Extended `dataframe_nulls.gg`** with `fillna_float` (via new `col_floats_null`) and `fillna_str` tests. (E) **Extended `dataframe_groupby.gg`** with `agg("min")` and `agg("max")` cases. (F) **Extended `dataframe_basic.gg`** with `df_from_records` test (now correctly returns 1 row, 2 cols). (G) **Updated `tests/integration.rs`** expected strings for all 4 fixtures. 658/658 unit + 387/387 integration tests pass.

- [2026-03-03] **Fix tensor float truncation & closure return type**: Two silent numeric correctness bugs. (A) **`Tensor[float]::flat_get` truncated fractions** — `flat_get(0)` on `[0.5, 1.0, 1.5, 2.0]` returned `0.0` instead of `0.5`. Root cause: `infer_method_return_type` was missing `"at"` in the element-returning method arm, and `extract_element_type_from_collection` didn't handle the `Shared__` prefix (so `Shared__Vector__double` → `None`). Fix: added `"at"` to the match arm; added `Shared__` prefix stripping with recursion at the top of `extract_element_type_from_collection`. (B) **`apply_float` closure return type mismatch** — closure `float(float) f` was cast as `int64_t(*)(void*, double)` instead of `double(*)(void*, double)`, producing garbage values. Root cause: `lower_equip_method` never called `ctx.callable_return_types.clear()` or populated `callable_return_types` for non-generic equip methods, unlike the regular-function and generic-equip paths. Fix: added `callable_return_types.clear()` after `ctx.clear_locals()` and added `extract_callable_return_type` tracking in the param loop (mirroring lines 53/67-68 for regular functions). Defense-in-depth: `emit_indirect_callable` now prefers the `FnPtr` return type from the callable local's GIR type over the destination local type. New fixtures: `tensor_float_frac.gg`, `closure_float_ret.gg`. Updated `tensor_extra.gg` `tensor_div_float` to use fractional values. 658/658 unit + 387/387 integration tests pass.

- [2026-03-03] **gg.tensor + gg.dataframe gap-fill & coverage**: (A) **Fixed `apply_int()` bug** — was adding column twice before dropping (corrupting `col_names`). (B) **Added missing tensor functions** — `tensor_bsub_float` (broadcast float subtraction) + 8 comparison operators (`tensor_lt_float`, `tensor_gt_float`, `tensor_le_int`, `tensor_le_float`, `tensor_ge_int`, `tensor_ge_float`, `tensor_ne_int`, `tensor_ne_float`) + updated header comment. (C) **Added nullable column constructors** `col_ints_null`/`col_strs_null` to `lib/gg/dataframe.gg`. (D) **Fixed pre-existing `df_group_by` bug** — `groups.get(key).unwrap().push(i)` operated on a copy, never updating the dict. Rewrote to get-modify-set pattern. (E) **New fixture `tests/fixtures/tensor_extra.gg`** — covers `tensor_div_int`, `tensor_sub_float`, `tensor_div_float`, `tensor_neg_float`, scalar ops, `tensor_clone_int`, `tensor_permute`, `tensor_std_all`, `tensor_mean_axis`, `tensor_matmul_float`, and comparison spot checks. (F) **New fixture `tests/fixtures/dataframe_nulls.gg`** — covers BoolColumn, `col_ints_null`/`col_strs_null`, `fillna_int`, `dropna`, `column_names`, `apply_float`, `apply_str`, `display()`. (G) **Expanded 3 fixtures** — `dataframe_agg.gg` (`df_std`, `describe`, `value_counts` count), `dataframe_groupby.gg` (agg "count" and "mean"), `dataframe_filter.gg` (`!=` and `<` operators). (H) **Updated integration.rs** with 2 new tests + 3 updated expected strings. (I) **Updated stdlib.rs unit tests** with 3 new assertions. (J) **Updated `docs/language-reference.md`** — expanded comparisons table, added `col_ints_null`/`col_strs_null` to construction table. Tests: 658/658 unit + 384/384 integration.

- [2026-03-03] **`FixedBufferAllocator` and `FallbackAllocator`**: Two new allocators added to `std.alloc`. `FixedBufferAllocator(capacity)` is a zero-heap bump allocator over an inline buffer (struct + buffer in one allocation). `FallbackAllocator(primary, secondary)` is a combinator: tries primary first, overflows to secondary on NULL; tracks `primary_count`/`fallback_count`. Full pipeline integration: C runtime in `c_runtime.rs`; stdlib equip declarations in `stdlib.rs`; opaque type mapping in IR lowering (`mod.rs`, `exprs.rs`, `stmts.rs`); semantic validation in `typecheck.rs` and `borrow.rs`; C backend dispatch in `mod.rs`. **Bug found and fixed**: `GorgetArena` had `__alloc` as 3rd field (not 1st), breaking raw `GorgetAllocator*` casts from Arena pointers — moved `__alloc` to first field to match the invariant documented in all other allocator structs. Also added `FixedBufferAllocator` and `FallbackAllocator` to `COLLECTION_TYPES` in `rewrite.rs` to prevent the struct-call rewrite pass from corrupting their constructors. 4 new integration test fixtures: `fba_basic.gg`, `fba_composable.gg`, `fallback_basic.gg`, `fallback_composable.gg`. Docs updated in `§15.3` of `language-reference.md`.

- [2026-03-03] **Release-prep bug squash round 6**: Three items: (A) **Stale TODO removal** — removed "Simulator dispatch for basic File I/O" from TODO.md; all four functions (`File__create`, `File__open`, `File__read_all`, `File__write_str`) were already fully implemented in `src/sim/runtime.rs` and `src/sim/dispatch.rs`. (B) **Fix `'\0'` char literal in self-hosting `format.gg`** — `case ECharLiteral(c):` now checks `c.len() == 0` to detect the null char (since `char_to_str('\0')` → `gorget_str_from_cstr` uses `strlen` → `len = 0`) and returns the literal string `"'\\0'"` instead of concatenating the null byte into the output, which would truncate the C string. Self-hosting parser comparison improves from 234/237 → 235/237. (C) **Add `path_normalize` and `path_absolute` to `std.path`** — implemented across all layers: `gen_path_module()` declarations in `stdlib.rs`; `gorget_path_normalize` (stack-based `.`/`..` resolution via in-place null-termination tokenization) + `gorget_path_absolute` (prepend `getcwd()` then normalize) in `c_runtime.rs`; name mapping + `returns_void`/`returns_cstr` whitelists + return-type inference in `mod.rs`; Rust `path_normalize`/`path_absolute` helpers + dispatch arms in `sim/runtime.rs`. New integration test fixture `path_normalize.gg`. 658/658 unit + 383/383 integration tests pass.

- [2026-03-03] **Release-prep bug squash round 5 — warning cleanup & dead code removal**: (A) **Dead code removal** — deleted 6 dead items (`gen_fmt_module`, `ty_cstr`, `cmc!` macro, `has_float_arg`, `lower_instance_trait_method`, `extract_dict_value_suffix`) and added `#[allow(dead_code)]` to 4 intentional infrastructure items (`SimAllocState::record_free`, `TrackingState` alias, `tracking_record_alloc/realloc/free`). (B) **Unreachable match arms** — fixed 5: merged duplicate `gorget_str_byte_len`/`gorget_string_byte_len` arms in runtime.rs, removed duplicate `gorget_str_byte_at` arm, removed duplicate `Value::Null` arm in value.rs, removed unreachable `_ =>` catch-alls in exprs.rs and stmts.rs. (C) **Warning cleanup** — `cargo fix` removed ~50 unused imports/mut qualifiers; manual fixes for unused `taken` variable in meta.rs, unused `std::io::Write` import in runtime.rs, and `#[allow(dead_code)]` on `MetaContext::empty()`. Result: **0 compiler warnings**. (D) **TOML DateTime documented** — expanded `TomlValue` description in `docs/language-reference.md` to list all variants with types; noted `DateTime(str)` stores raw text only. (E) **TODO/DONE housekeeping** — rephrased TOML DateTime TODO as future enhancement. 658/658 unit + 336/336 integration tests pass.

- [2026-03-03] **Release-prep bug squash round 4**: Three items: (A) **Remove dead `List`/`Array` collection aliases** — `List[T]` and `Array[T]` had zero usage in tests, docs, examples, or library code. Removed 15 occurrences across `stdlib.rs`, `semantic/resolve.rs`, `semantic/typecheck.rs` (9 sites), `semantic/rewrite.rs`, `ir/lowering/mod.rs`, `ir/lowering/generics.rs`. Updated `generate_collections` test from 10 to 8. (B) **Add diagnostics to `build_tree` for malformed trace events** — added `eprintln!` warnings at 3 locations in `src/report.rs`: unmatched Return/StmtEnd, depth jumps with unclosed Normal frames, and unclosed Normal frames in the final drain. (C) **Document `gg.log` in language reference** — added `gg.log` section to `docs/language-reference.md §15.3` (after `gg.json`) covering `LogLevel`, `Logger`, and module-level convenience functions. Removed 3 stale TODO items (gg.log level filtering, Vector/List/Array aliases, build_tree diagnostics). 658/658 unit + 382/382 integration tests pass.

- [2026-03-03] **Release-prep bug squash round 3**: Four items: (A) **Fix 6 broken dataframe test fixtures** — `parse_int`/`parse_float` return `Result[int/float, str]` but `lib/gg/dataframe.gg` used bare `int`/`float` assignments at 16 call sites. Added `.unwrap()` to all 16 calls (all guarded by `str_is_int`/`str_is_float` checks, so unwrap is safe). All 6 dataframe tests now pass. (B) **Topological sort cycle diagnostic** — `src/backend/c/mod.rs:1092-1097` silently fell through on cycles. Added `debug_assert!` with count of remaining types. (C) **Async/await TODO cleanup** — replaced the 30-line mega-item (90% strikethrough) with a concise 5-item remaining list. Removed duplicate Low-priority cross-reference. (D) **Stale const evaluation TODO** — removed "No compile-time expression evaluation" item, which is superseded by Meta M7 (compile-time pure function evaluation). Const generics already tracked separately.

- [2026-03-03] **Release-prep bug squash round 2**: Three items: (A) **Pattern-bound variable type inference in print** — confirmed fixed by GIR migration; the GIR lowerer's `emit_pattern_bindings` in `stmts.rs` correctly propagates field types from enum TypeDef to pattern variables, and `format_for_printf` in `exprs.rs` emits correct format specifiers (`%.*s` for Str, `%lld` for int, `%f` for float). Tested with `match_option_result.gg` fixture. Removed stale TODO item. (B) **Stale TODO cleanup** — audited 9 items referencing deleted legacy codegen (`src/codegen/`); removed 7 resolved items, updated 2 with correct file references (`src/backend/c/mod.rs`). (C) **`str.contains()` GorgetString→Str coercion fix** — audited all 3 code paths for `gorget_str_*` functions: (1) self coercion in `emit_collection_method_call` ✓, (2) `FormatArgsMode::StrFn` for printf-style args ✓, (3) remaining method args — **found and fixed a real bug**: non-self GorgetString args (e.g., `s.contains(other_string)`) were passed without coercion, causing a C type mismatch. Added GorgetString→Str coercion in the remaining-args loop for `gorget_str_*` methods. Also fixed stale line reference in comment. New test fixture `string_coerce_args.gg` exercises contains/starts_with/ends_with/in/find/replace with GorgetString args.

- [2026-03-02] **`gg.tensor` + `gg.dataframe` modules**: Implemented NumPy-equivalent (`gg.tensor`, ~1100 lines) and pandas-equivalent (`gg.dataframe`, ~1420 lines) pure Gorget libraries. Tensor uses `Shared[Vector[T]]` for O(1) view operations (reshape/transpose/slice share backing buffer). Key features: Tensor — construction helpers, strided multi-index access, element-wise arithmetic, broadcasting, reductions along axis, matmul/dot, comparisons. DataFrame — typed columns (int/float/str/bool) with null masking, head/tail/select/drop/filter/sort, groupby+agg, inner join, concat, CSV I/O. **Compiler fixes**: (1) Dict `"set"` and `"has"` added as aliases for `put`/`contains` in C backend dispatch (`mod.rs`) and type checker (`typecheck.rs`). (2) `sort_by` bug fixed: inner `ia`/`ib` shadowed index variables — renamed to `va_int`/`vb_int`. **Test infrastructure**: 12 integration test fixtures (6 tensor + 6 dataframe), all registered in `tests/integration.rs`; 8 new stdlib unit tests; `gg.tensor` and `gg.dataframe` documented in `docs/language-reference.md §15.3`. 654/654 unit + 373/373 integration tests pass.

- [2026-03-02] **Self-hosting resolver: 295/323 matches (up from 0/323)**: Multiple fixes to bring the self-hosting resolver back into alignment after 83 new test fixtures and new built-in type registrations drifted the comparison. (1) Added 8 missing built-in collection types (`Future`, `Task`, `Channel`, `Shared`, `Weak`, `Mutex`, `Guard`, `TaskGroup`) to `resolve.gg`'s `collect_top_level()` — fixed the 323-fixture mass regression. (2) Fixed `with` block `as` binding: `parse_expr()` consumes `expr as name` as `EAs(expr, type)`; added `decompose_with_stmt()` helper to extract binding name from the type position (+10 fixtures). (3) Added `IStaticDecl` AST variant for module-level VarDecl; parser wraps top-level `SVarDecl` as `IStaticDecl`; resolver registers as `DkStatic()` (+3 fixtures: thread_atomic, thread_barrier, serialize_collections). (4) Whole-module import: when `ImportStmt.names` is empty, extract last path segment from `module_path` and register as Import. (5) `meta` item skipping: detect `meta` identifier at module level and skip tokens until next item — prevents meta declarations from being misidentified as statics (+5 fixtures).

- [2026-03-02] **Basic orphan rule for equip blocks**: Added `OrphanImpl` semantic error. In `process_impl()` (`traits.rs`), after the duplicate-impl check, the compiler now verifies that at least one of (self type, trait) is defined locally — i.e., has a non-dummy span and is not an import. Built-in traits (`Displayable`, `Equatable`, etc.) and primitives (`int`, `str`) count as foreign. Equipping a local type with a built-in trait: allowed. Equipping a foreign type with a local trait: allowed. Both foreign: rejected with a clear error message. Inherent impls (no trait) are not checked. 4 new unit tests. 650 unit + 367 integration tests pass.

- [2026-03-02] **ByMutRef captures in escaping closures (defense-in-depth)**: Investigation revealed the borrow checker already prevents the dangerous case — `cannot return: borrows from local variable which will be dropped`. Added C-level defense-in-depth: when heap-allocating a closure env for escaping, detect MutPtr fields (ByMutRef captures) and box them (allocate heap cell, copy value from stack pointer, redirect env field to heap). This prevents dangling pointers if the borrow checker is ever relaxed. Removed from TODO as the primary safety concern is already addressed.

- [2026-03-02] **Channel[T] RAII + Shared[T] spawn fix**: Two related correctness fixes. (1) **Channel refcount**: Added `volatile int64_t refcount` to `GorgetChannel` struct, initialized to 1 in `gorget_channel_new()`. Added `gorget_channel_retain()` (atomic increment) and `gorget_channel_release()` (atomic decrement → auto-close + free at zero). Changed Channel type registration from `(Copy, DropStrategy::None)` to `(Copy, DropStrategy::Trivial("Channel__T__drop"))`. Emitted `Channel__T__clone()` and `Channel__T__drop()` wrapper functions. (2) **Spawn arg retain**: When copying parameters into `__SpawnCtx`, the compiler now emits `Type__clone()` calls for ref-counted types (Channel, Shared, Weak) instead of plain bitwise copies. This fixes a latent use-after-free where `Shared[T]` pointers were bitwise-copied without incrementing the refcount. (3) **Function parameter drops**: Added `register_param()` to the drop elaborator — registers only Copy-semantics types with non-None drop strategy (Channel, Shared, Weak) for scope-exit release. Move types are excluded (handled by body-level drops). Applied to all four function lowering paths. New test fixture `channel_raii.gg` (producer/consumer with auto-close). 646 unit + 367 integration tests pass.

- [2026-03-02] **Task[T] join-on-drop (unawaited task leak fix)**: Spawned-but-never-awaited tasks now have RAII cleanup. Changes: (1) Removed `map_ast_type` unwrap of `Task[T]→T` (`types.rs`), so Task is a first-class type with its own TypeId. (2) Registered Task TypeDef with `CopySemantics::Move` + `DropStrategy::Trivial("Task__T__drop")` when created in the spawn path (`exprs.rs`). (3) Added `"Task"` to the lazy-generic re-inference list (`stmts.rs`) so `auto` VarDecls pick up the correct type. (4) Extended Task struct with `void (*__drop)(void*)` function pointer for polymorphic cleanup — different spawned functions have different `__SpawnCtx` sizes, so each gets a per-fn `__spawn_drop_fn(void*)` that knows the correct size for `GORGET_FREE`. (5) Emit `Task__T__drop(Task__T* self)` per unique Task type — checks `self->__task && self->__drop`, dispatches to the per-fn drop. (6) After `.await()`, emit `move_zero` + `mark_moved` on the task local to prevent double-join — the DropIfAlive handler skips zeroed locals via `memcmp`. Two new test fixtures: `spawn_join_on_drop.gg` (await + unawaited in same function), `spawn_drop_void.gg` (unawaited task with no crash/hang). 646 unit + 366 integration tests pass.

- [2026-03-02] **Three release-prep bug fixes**: (A) `gorget_crypto_hmac_sha256` phantom entry: removed `gorget_crypto_sha256` and `gorget_crypto_hmac_sha256` from `is_cstr_param_fn` (both take `GorgetArray*`, already handled by `takes_array_ptr_args`); removed dead `"crypto_hmac_sha256" => "gorget_crypto_hmac_sha256"` mapping from `map_stdlib_name` (no C function exists); removed `"crypto_hmac_sha256" | "gorget_crypto_hmac_sha256"` from `infer_gir_type_for_stdlib_fn`. (B) For-loop range bounds type: `lower_for_range` in `stmts.rs` now lowers `start` first, infers its GIR type via `infer_operand_type_full`, and uses that type for the loop variable, comparison, and increment instead of hardcoding `I64_TYPE`. Handles typed bound variables (e.g. `start: uint8`); literal `0` still infers I64 as expected. (C) Codegen panics: confirmed the specific legacy-codegen panics (string interpolation of non-primitive types, `in` operator fallthrough) no longer exist — the GIR pipeline handles both gracefully (display-method dispatch + format_specifier fallback for interpolation; `gorget_array_contains` fallback for `in`). Remaining panics are test-internal assertions or `BUG:`-prefixed invariant guards. 646/646 unit + 364/364 integration tests pass.

- [2026-03-02] **Async Housekeeping + Unbuffered Channels**: (1) Struck `ConsumeCallable single-call enforcement` from the async roadmap in TODO.md — three test fixtures already existed confirming it is done. Also struck `unbuffered channels` from the remaining items list. (2) Added two new Medium-priority TODO entries: `Channel[T] RAII (refcount-based auto-close+free)` and `Task[T] join-on-drop (unawaited task leak fix)` with full implementation notes. (3) Implemented rendezvous channel (`Channel[T](0)`): removed the `if (capacity == 0) capacity = 1` hack from `gorget_channel_new`; when `capacity == 0` a single-element transfer slot is allocated and `ch->capacity` stays 0 to signal rendezvous mode throughout all functions. `gorget_channel_send` with `capacity==0` deposits into the slot, wakes the receiver (async waker or condvar), then blocks until the receiver signals consumption (count drops to 0). `gorget_channel_recv` with `capacity==0` waits for data, copies it out, sets count=0, and signals `not_full` to ack the waiting sender. `gorget_channel_poll_send` and `gorget_channel_poll_recv` got matching rendezvous branches for async contexts. `gorget_channel_free` was fixed to dealloc `elem_size` bytes (not 0) when `capacity==0`. New test fixture `async_channel_unbuffered.gg` (producer spawns, main recvs 10/20/30) + integration test. 361/361 integration tests pass.

- [2026-03-02] **Meta M7: compile-time pure function evaluation**: Extended the meta evaluator (`src/semantic/meta.rs`) to call user-defined functions at compile time. Key additions: (1) `MetaContext` gains `items: &[Spanned<Item>]` (all module items for function lookup) and `call_depth: Cell<usize>` (recursion counter); (2) `MetaControlFlow` enum (`Continue`/`Return`/`Break`/`LoopContinue`) propagates control flow through statement-level evaluation; (3) `lookup_meta_function` scans module items, rejecting generic/async/unsafe functions and unsupported param/return types; (4) `eval_meta_fn_call`/`eval_meta_fn_body`/`eval_meta_block`/`eval_meta_stmt` interpret function bodies supporting: VarDecl, Assign, CompoundAssign, Return, If/elif/else, While, Loop, For (integer ranges), Break, Continue, Assert, and expression-body functions; (5) `eval_expr` Call fallback now tries `lookup_meta_function` before reporting unknown function; (6) `Expr::If` (expression-position if) added to `eval_expr`; (7) `evaluate_meta_consts` scopes the Phase 1 ctx borrow before Phase 1.5 mutation. Limits: MAX_META_RECURSION=256, MAX_META_ITERATIONS=100,000. 3 new integration tests: `meta_fn_basic` (expression-body + block), `meta_fn_recursive` (factorial+fib), `meta_fn_loops` (while-loop summation). 646/646 unit + 363/363 integration tests pass.

- [2026-03-02] **Three Stdlib Gap Closures for Release (Tasks A/B/C/D)**: (A) **GorgetString read-only method dispatch**: Added ~23 read-only method entries to the `GorgetString__*` block in `try_rewrite_collection_method()` (`src/backend/c/mod.rs`) — `contains`, `starts_with`, `ends_with`, `replace`, `find`, `index_of`, `count`, `split`, `join`, `repeat`, `pad_left`, `pad_right`, `trim`, `removeprefix`, `removesuffix`, `byte_slice`, `bytes`, `codepoints`, `chars`, `byte_len`, `strip`/`lstrip`/`rstrip` (with ws_variant). All delegate to `gorget_str_*` functions; the GorgetString→Str coercion at line 7591 handles self-argument coercion. (B) **`parse_int`/`parse_float` → `Result[T, str]`**: Changed `std.conv` declarations in `stdlib.rs`; added thread-local error `__gorget_parse_last_error` and non-crashing implementations in `c_runtime.rs`; added `gorget_parse_last_error` to `last_error_fn()` in `mod.rs`; changed `sim/runtime.rs` to return `Value::Enum` Result values; updated `conv_stdlib.gg` fixture + integration test; updated all callers in `lib/gg/json.gg`, `yaml.gg`, `toml.gg`, `ssh.gg`, `xml.gg`, `tests/fixtures/char_methods.gg`, `tests/fixtures/self_host_lexer/lexer.gg` to use `.unwrap_or()`. Also removed `parse_int`/`parse_float` from `infer_runtime_return_type` (was incorrectly overriding the correct `Result__*` GIR type); fixed `try_emit_result_wrapped_call` to use `gorget_str_to_cstr` instead of `.data` for `Str` arguments (ensures null termination for `strtoll`). (C) **DateTime arithmetic test coverage**: Added `add_minutes(dt_n)` method to `lib/std/datetime.gg`; added 5 deterministic arithmetic tests to `datetime_basic.gg` (add_days, add_hours, add_seconds, add_days(-1), add_minutes); updated integration test expected output. (D) **Deferred audit items**: Added 7 new TODO entries (path_normalize/path_absolute, gorget_crypto_hmac_sha256 phantom entry, uuid_parse, gg.log level filtering, Heap[T] max-heap, Simulator File I/O, str.contains() type dispatch audit). 646/646 unit + 360/360 integration tests pass.

- [2026-03-02] **ECS Release Polish: new(), each(), try_get(), query2**: Four ECS API improvements. (1) `SparseSet[T].new()` static factory — eliminates 4-arg boilerplate; required new `Expr::Index` static method dispatch and `has_self` guard in generic equip lowering. (2) `SparseSet[T].each(Callable[void(Entity, T)] fn)` — callback iteration over (entity, component) pairs; required Fix A (callable_return_types in generic equip param loop) and void-return callable call fix. (3) `SparseSet[T].try_get(self, Entity e) -> Option[T]` — safe accessor returning None if missing; required Fix B (map_ast_type_mut for generic equip return types so Option__T is registered). (4) `query2[A, B](SparseSet[A], SparseSet[B]) -> Vector[Entity]` — multi-component intersection iterating the smaller set. Updated call sites in examples/ecs/main.gg, examples/breakout/world.gg, tests/fixtures/ecs_basics.gg. 646 unit + 359 integration tests pass.

- [2026-03-02] **Fix `const char*` coercion for 5 missing network/hot-reload functions**: Added `gorget_socket_write_str`, `gorget_tls_write_str`, `gorget_udp_join_multicast`, `gorget_udp_leave_multicast`, and `gorget_hot_load` to `is_cstr_param_fn()` in `src/backend/c/mod.rs`. These functions take `const char*` parameters but were missing from the coercion list, causing "incompatible type for argument" C compiler errors. Fixes `examples/http_get.gg` type mismatch. 646 unit + 360 integration tests pass.

- [2026-03-02] **Stdlib Gap Closure for Release (4 phases)**: Closed key stdlib gaps ahead of release. (1) **Phase 1 — Simulator dispatch for RWLock + Thread**: Added `thread_next_id` to `Interpreter`; `__gorget_thread_spawn_FN` eagerly calls the function and stores result+tid as a `Value::Struct` heap entry; `gorget_current_thread_id` returns 1 (main thread); full dispatch for RWLock (new/read/write/drop), ReadGuard/WriteGuard (get/set/drop), Thread (join/id/drop), AtomicInt (new/load/store/add/sub/compare_exchange), AtomicBool (new/load/store/swap/compare_exchange), Barrier (new/wait=no-op), CondVar (new/notify_one/notify_all/wait=no-op). Verified: `sync_rwlock.gg`, `thread_basic.gg`, `sync_atomics.gg`, `sync_barrier.gg` all pass in sim. (2) **Phase 4 — std.fmt module**: Created `lib/std/fmt.gg` as a file-based module with 6 utilities: `pad_left`, `pad_right`, `center`, `repeat`, `str_truncate` (renamed from `truncate` to avoid POSIX conflict), `join`. Updated `src/stdlib.rs` to serve it via `include_str!`. New fixture `tests/fixtures/fmt_basic.gg` + integration test. (3) **Phase 3 — bytes_to_str UTF-8 validation**: Added `gorget_bytes_utf8_valid` C function to `c_runtime.rs` with proper leading-byte + continuation-byte + codepoint-range validation. Added `gorget_bytes_utf8_valid` to `src/sim/runtime.rs` using `std::str::from_utf8`. Updated `lib/std/bytes.gg` to call the validator before returning `Ok`. (4) **Phase 2 — Missing examples**: Created 4 examples: `examples/http_get.gg`, `examples/toml_config.gg`, `examples/uuid_gen.gg`, `examples/cli_app.gg`. 646/646 unit + 354/354 integration tests pass.

- [2026-03-02] **Async I/O Reactor (timerfd/kqueue) + `std.async.async_sleep`**: Added a full I/O reactor backed by timerfd+epoll (Linux) and kqueue EVFILT_TIMER (macOS) with `clock_nanosleep` fallback. The reactor uses `pthread_once` lazy init, a dedicated reactor thread running `epoll_wait`/`kevent`, and the existing `GorgetWaker` protocol (condition-variable based) to wake sleeping tasks. `REACTOR_RUNTIME` C constant added to `c_runtime.rs`. `std.async` module added to `stdlib.rs` with `async_sleep(ms: int)` → `gorget_reactor_sleep_ms()`. C backend: `async_sleep` → `gorget_reactor_sleep_ms` in `KNOWN_FUNCTION_MAPPINGS`; reactor emission triggered when `async_sleep` calls are detected in the module. Simulator: `gorget_reactor_sleep_ms` handled as real `std::thread::sleep`. Two test fixtures (`async_reactor_sleep.gg`, `async_timer_loop.gg`) + two integration tests. 646/646 unit + 358/358 integration tests pass.

- [2026-03-02] **Shared[T] V2: Atomic ref-counting + Drop + Weak[T]**: Replaced the stub Shared[T] implementation with full Arc/Weak pattern. (1) **C runtime** (`SHARED_RUNTIME`): `GorgetShared` struct with `volatile int64_t strong` + `weak` fields; `gorget_shared_new/clone/drop/get_ptr/strong_count/downgrade`; `gorget_weak_clone/drop/upgrade` (CAS-based, lock-free). (2) **Semantic analysis**: Added `strong_count()` and `downgrade()` to Shared methods; added `Weak` type with `clone()` and `upgrade()` methods; added "Weak" to built-in type list (resolve.rs) and Copy-type matches (borrow.rs). (3) **IR lowering**: `Shared` and `Weak` now use `DropStrategy::Trivial` (atomic decrement at scope exit); `Weak[T]` in lazy-generic type re-inference list (stmts.rs); added Shared `downgrade/strong_count` and Weak `clone/upgrade` dispatch in `exprs.rs`; `ensure_weak_type_def` function; `weak_types: Vec<String>` field on Module. (4) **C backend**: `emit_shared_weak_typedefs()` emits typedefs before type defs (prevents forward-ref errors); `emit_shared_defs()` emits `clone/drop/get/strong_count/downgrade` wrappers; `emit_weak_defs()` emits `clone/drop/upgrade` wrappers; fix to `!c_name.ends_with("__upgrade")` condition so `upgrade()` isn't wrapped in integer-sentinel Option pattern. (5) **Simulator**: `shared_refcounts: HashMap<usize, (i64, i64)>` for per-handle strong/weak tracking; `Shared` handles as `Value::Ptr`, `Weak` handles as `Value::Ref`; `run_drop_value` split for Shared/Weak/Box. Three test fixtures (`shared_refcount.gg`, `shared_weak.gg`, `shared_struct.gg`) + integration tests. 646/646 unit + 358/358 integration tests pass.

- [2026-03-02] **Document `std.alloc` in language reference**: Added comprehensive `std.alloc` module documentation to §15.3 of `docs/language-reference.md`. Covers all four allocators (Arena, PoolAllocator, TlsfAllocator, TrackingAllocator) with API tables, scoped `with` binding, composable `alloc=` parameter, escape analysis (with negative examples), nesting, and a "when to use which" reference table. Added cross-reference from §6.14 (with statement) to the new section. All method names verified against `src/stdlib.rs`. All code examples match existing integration test fixtures. Evaluated additional allocator types (stack, buddy, slab, ring) — none needed; the four existing allocators cover all major use-case categories.

- [2026-03-02] **TLSF (Two-Level Segregate Fit) Allocator**: Fourth allocator type providing O(1) worst-case alloc/free with low fragmentation and individual deallocation. (1) C runtime (`c_runtime.rs`): ~300 lines — block header with size+flags in low bits, segregated free-list matrix indexed by (fl, sl) bitmaps, `__builtin_clz`/`__builtin_ctz` bit ops, sentinel blocks at region boundaries for safe coalescing, overflow regions from parent allocator, 16-byte alignment. (2) stdlib.rs: `TlsfAllocator` struct + equip block (bytes_used, peak_bytes, pool_size, reset, destroy). (3) Semantic analysis: added to builtin constructor list, allocator type validation, is_allocator_type, is_copy_type, COLLECTION_TYPES. (4) IR lowering: constructor (`gorget_tlsf_new`), destroy dispatch, opaque type registration. (5) C backend: method mapping (5 methods), type alias, return type inference. (6) Simulator: `Tlsf` variant in `SimAllocState` with bytes_used/peak_bytes tracking. (7) **Bug fix: `with` block drop ordering** — moved `ctx.drops.pop_scope()` BEFORE allocator pop+destroy in `lower_with()`, fixing a use-after-free where collections allocated within the scope tried to dealloc via the already-destroyed allocator. This was a latent bug affecting all allocator types (Arena, Pool, Tracking, TLSF). (8) Three test fixtures: `tlsf_basic.gg` (with binding + stats + reset), `tlsf_composable.gg` (alloc= parameter), `tlsf_escape.gg` (escape detection negative test). ASan clean. 638/638 unit + 341/341 integration tests pass.

- [2026-03-02] **Hot-reload: inotify file watching for Linux**: Replaced the ~20-line Linux stub in `src/backend/c/c_runtime.rs` with a real inotify-based watcher. `gorget_hot_watch_init` calls `inotify_init1(IN_NONBLOCK)` and adds per-file watches with `IN_MODIFY | IN_MOVE_SELF | IN_DELETE_SELF`. `gorget_hot_watch_check` does a non-blocking `read()` (returns `true` if any events pending). `gorget_hot_watch_close` removes watches and closes the inotify fd. Hot-reload (`directive hot-reload`) now works on Linux.

- [2026-03-02] **ECS generational entity IDs (ABA problem + double-destroy)**: Replaced raw `int` entity handles with `Entity { int id, int gen }`. `EntityPool` gained `Vector[int] generations` and `Vector[bool] alive`; `create()` now returns `Entity`, `destroy(Entity)` rejects stale/already-dead handles and bumps generation on recycle, and `is_alive(Entity)` detects stale handles. `SparseSet[T]` field `entity_ids` changed from `Vector[int]` to `Vector[Entity]`; all methods (`insert`, `remove`, `has`, `get`, `set`, `entity_at`) take/return `Entity` (using `.id` internally for sparse indexing). `SparseSetIter` and `Iterable` trait updated to yield `Entity`. Added `equip Entity with Displayable`. Updated `examples/ecs/world.gg`, `examples/ecs/main.gg`, `examples/breakout/world.gg`, `tests/fixtures/ecs_basics.gg` (added generational safety tests), `docs/stdlib-ecs.md`. Stdlib test updated for new equip count (5). 646 unit + 346 integration tests pass.

- [2026-03-02] **Cross-primitive integration: module-level vars, CondVar, 4 fixtures, docs**: Completed the full cross-primitive integration plan. (1) **eval_static_init fix**: `AtomicInt(0)` is parsed as `Expr::StructLiteral` not `Expr::Call`; added StructLiteral arm to `eval_static_init` in `src/ir/lowering/mod.rs`. (2) **Mutex[int] parser fix**: `looks_like_module_var_decl()` now includes `Keyword::Mutex` so `Mutex[int] counter = ...` parses correctly. (3) **map_ast_type_mut for globals**: `lower_static_decl` now uses `map_ast_type_mut` so generic types like `Mutex[int]` auto-register their C types. (4) **Mangled type name storage**: Pre-registration stores `"Mutex__int64_t"` (not bare `"Mutex"`) so method dispatch (`counter.lock()`) resolves correctly. (5) **CondVar full implementation**: Was missing from `gen_sync_module()`, C runtime, and method dispatch. Added `GorgetCondVar` struct + `gorget_condvar_new/notify_one/notify_all` to `SYNC_RUNTIME`; `gorget_condvar_wait_guard(void* cv_opaque, gorget_guard_t* g)` bridge using `void*` to avoid header ordering issues in `MUTEX_RUNTIME`; CondVar constructor + `notify_one`/`notify_all`/`wait` method dispatch in `exprs.rs`; `"CondVar" => Some("GorgetCondVar*")` C type override in `mod.rs`; fn_name_map entries for all three methods. (6) **c_suffix_to_type_id helper**: `Guard[bool].get()` was returning `int64_t` because `lookup_named("bool")` returns None. New helper maps C primitive type names ("bool", "int64_t", "double") to TypeIds; used in all Guard/ReadGuard/WriteGuard `.get()` dispatch. (7) **Dummy-span filter for stdlib StaticDecls**: `stderr`/`stdout` StaticDecl items use `Span::dummy()` (start==end==0) and were being lowered as user globals, generating `static void stderr = {0}` conflicts. Fixed by skipping items with dummy spans. (8) **4 new fixtures**: `thread_atomic.gg` (2 threads increment AtomicInt, expect `2`), `thread_mutex.gg` (2 threads increment Mutex[int], expect `2`), `thread_barrier.gg` (2-thread Barrier(2), expect `2`), `sync_condvar.gg` (producer/consumer with CondVar+Mutex, expect `true`). (9) **4 new integration tests**. (10) **Documentation**: Added `std.sync` and `std.thread` subsections to `docs/language-reference.md`; expanded `std.process` section with `process_spawn`, `getpid`, and `Process` methods. 350/350 integration tests pass.

- [2026-03-02] **std.process Phase 3 (Process lifecycle — fork/exec/pipes/wait/kill)**: Added `Process` type with full OS process lifecycle management. `process_spawn(program, args) -> Result[Process, str]` forks a child via `fork()`+`execvp()` with three anonymous pipes (stdin/stdout/stderr). `Process` methods: `wait() -> int` (waitpid, returns exit code), `kill() -> void` (SIGTERM), `pid() -> int` (child PID), `write_stdin(data: str)`, `close_stdin()`, `read_stdout() -> String`, `read_stderr() -> String`. Also added `getpid() -> int` free function. Key bugs fixed during implementation: (1) `gorget_process_spawn` signature changed from `Str program, GorgetArray args` to `const char* program, GorgetArray* args` to match C backend's natural emission. (2) `gorget_process_spawn_err()` returns `NULL` (not empty string pointer) when no error, so `TryCapture` `if (__err)` check works correctly. (3) Added `__gorget_spawn_errbuf[0] = '\0'` at start of spawn to clear stale errors from previous failed calls. (4) Added explicit `Process` method dispatch in `exprs.rs::lower_method_call` — `Process` type is `GorgetProcess*` (pointer), so passing `&recv` creates `GorgetProcess**` with default dispatch; explicit block passes receiver directly. (5) Fixed `GorgetString` construction in read_stdout/read_stderr: was `gs.a = *__gorget_current_alloc`, corrected to compound literal with `.alloc` field. (6) Fixed `GORGET_REALLOC` old-size tracking (was always passing 0). (7) Fixed `args.size` → `args.len` in spawn. 2 new fixtures (`process_spawn.gg`, `process_pipe.gg`), 2 new integration tests. 342/342 integration + unit tests pass.

- [2026-03-02] **std.sync Phase 1 (AtomicInt, AtomicBool, CondVar, Barrier, RWLock[T])**: Added all five sync primitives. AtomicInt: load/store/add/sub/compare_exchange using GCC `__atomic_*` builtins with `__ATOMIC_SEQ_CST`. AtomicBool: load/store/swap/compare_exchange. CondVar: wraps `pthread_cond_t`, `wait(guard)` takes a gorget_guard_t* to release/reacquire mutex literal with `.alloc` field. `GORGET_REALLOC(buf, 0, cap)` → correct old-size tracking. 4 new integration tests: sync_atomics, sync_barrier, sync_rwlock, thread_basic. 340/340 integration + 628/628 unit tests pass.

- [2026-03-02] **std.thread Phase 2 (Thread[T], thread_spawn, current_thread_id)**: Added bare thread primitive. Thread[T] is a generic Move type (join() consumes it). thread_spawn(fn_name) takes a bare function reference (closures deferred). C backend: `emit_thread_defs` generates per-type `__GorgetThread__T` structs and join/id methods; `emit_thread_helpers` generates `__gorget_thread_entry_fn` entry function + `__gorget_thread_spawn_fn` spawn helper using pthread_create. Key bugs fixed: `__thread` renamed to `_thr` (GCC reserved keyword for thread-local storage); `infer_method_return_type` guarded so `Thread__T__join` doesn't match the string `join` → GorgetString heuristic. 340/340 integration + 628/628 unit tests pass.

- [2026-03-02] **Meta M5 (Conditional Types) + M6 (Type Functions)**: Completed the "computed types" story from `meta.md`. **M5**: `meta type Map = Dict if feature("ordered") else HashMap` — new `MetaTypeRhs::Conditional` AST variant, parser intercepts `<type> if <cond> else <type>` after the `=`. **M6**: `meta type sized_int(int bits): ...` already parsed; evaluator now interprets the body — `eval_meta_type_body` walks `if/elif/else` + `return` stmts, `resolve_expr_as_type` maps identifier returns to types. `process_meta_item` registers `MetaTypeFunc` items into `type_func_env`, `resolve_meta_type_rhs` dispatches Plain/Conditional/Call. Two bonus fixes: (1) `substitute_type` now handles generic alias replacement (`Map[str, int]` where `Map = Dict` → `Dict[str, int]`); (2) `substitute_expr` now substitutes Named type-alias identifiers in expression position (constructor calls). `MetaTypeFunc` added to Phase 3 removal. 6 new unit tests + 2 integration fixtures. **646/646 unit + 340/340 integration + 295/296 sim PASS**.

- [2026-03-02] **Bug fixes & cleanup batch**: (1) **GorgetString→Str coercion in EnumInit** — Extended `coerce_constructor_arg()` in `src/backend/c/mod.rs` to emit `(Str){ .data = val.data, .len = val.len }` when a `GorgetString` (from string concat) is stored into a `Str` field. New fixture `tests/fixtures/result_str_concat.gg` + integration test. (2) **`looks_like_closure()` false positive** — Added early `return false` in `src/parser/expr.rs` when scanning closure-lookahead parens and encountering `and`/`or`/`not`/`==`/`!=`/`<=`/`>=`/`<`/`>`. New parser unit test `test_paren_expr_before_colon_not_closure`. (3) **Inline bounds cleanup** — Renamed `FunctionInfo.where_bounds` → `trait_bounds` in `resolve.rs` and `typecheck.rs`; removed dead `WhereBound::Trait` variant from `ast.rs`, dead match arm from `formatter/mod.rs`, and dead `WhereBound::Trait => None` arm from `resolve.rs`. 638/638 unit + 338/338 integration PASS.

- [2026-03-02] **Docs: rewrite "99% automatic" borrow inference claim + add Rust lifetime comparison**: Replaced vague "99%/~1%/almost never" tier labels in `docs/language-design.md` §3.4 with a concrete breakdown of what requires no annotation (owned types, single ref param, self methods, multi-param with body, transitive calls, local aliases) vs. what requires `live` (trait/FFI declarations, struct fields, multi-source precision, outlives). Added §3.5 "Comparison with Rust Lifetimes" with a side-by-side table and prose explaining body analysis vs. signature-only inference and the trade-offs. Updated Design Decision #1 table to remove "99% of code". Added "Lifetimes" row to §16 comparison table.

- [2026-03-02] **Bare Tuple Syntax**: Removed mandatory parentheses in three contexts: (1) function return types — `str, int f()` instead of `(str, int) f()`; (2) return statements — `return a, b` instead of `return (a, b)`; (3) `auto` destructuring — `auto a, b = f()` instead of `auto (a, b) = f()`; (4) `for` loop patterns — `for x, y in pairs` instead of `for (x, y) in pairs`. Parenthesized forms continue to work. Changes: `src/parser/mod.rs`: bare comma list after first return type wraps into `Type::Tuple`; `src/parser/stmt.rs`: `parse_return_stmt` collects comma-separated exprs into `Expr::TupleLiteral`; `parse_auto_var_decl` collects comma-separated binding patterns into `Pattern::Tuple`; `parse_for_stmt` calls `parse_bare_pattern` instead of `parse_pattern`; `src/parser/pattern.rs`: added `parse_bare_pattern` method; `src/formatter/mod.rs`: bare emission in function return-type position, return statements, `for` patterns, and `auto` tuple patterns; 9 new parser unit tests; `tests/fixtures/bare_tuples.gg` + integration test. 637/637 unit + integration tests pass.
