Works in VarDecl (`Color c = .Red()`), assignment, return, function arguments, and match patterns (`case .Blue(n):`). Implementation: (1) `src/parser/ast.rs`: Added `Expr::DotShorthand` and `Pattern::DotShorthand` AST nodes. (2) `src/parser/expr.rs`: `Token::Dot` prefix parsing; added to `is_expr_start()` and `contains_it()`. (3) `src/parser/pattern.rs`: `Token::Dot` prefix parsing for patterns. (4) All exhaustive matches updated across `visitor.rs`, `loader.rs`, `resolve.rs`, `typecheck.rs`, `borrow.rs`, `meta.rs`, `rewrite.rs`, `formatter/mod.rs`, `ir/lowering/exprs.rs`, `ir/lowering/stmts.rs`, `tests/integration.rs`. (8) `tests/fixtures/dot_shorthand.gg` (NEW): exercises VarDecl, assignment, function arg, return, and match patterns. 628/628 unit + 336/336 integration tests pass.

- [2026-03-02] **Enum Variant Namespace Separation (Phase 1)**: User-defined enum variants now require qualified access (`Color.Red()` instead of bare `Red()`). Built-in prelude variants (`Ok`, `Error`, `Some`, `None`) remain bare. Changes: (1) `src/semantic/scope.rs`: Added `alloc_def()` method that allocates a `DefId` without inserting the name into scope's `names` map — used for user-defined enum variant registration. (2) `src/semantic/resolve.rs`: `collect_item` for `Item::Enum` uses `alloc_def` for user-defined enums so variants don't pollute global scope; `collect_import` handles new `glob_types` field; two-pass approach in `collect_top_level` registers glob-imported variants after all enums are collected. (3) `src/ir/lowering/exprs.rs`: Qualified variant construction via `Expr::MethodCall` (static enum receiver) and `Expr::Path`; `lower_call` handles `Expr::Path` callee. (4) `src/ir/lowering/stmts.rs`: `lower_pattern_condition` and `emit_pattern_bindings` handle multi-segment paths for qualified patterns. (5) `src/loader.rs`: `qualify_variant_refs` performs pre-merge AST rewrite; `build_variant_map_from_all` builds a global variant-to-enum map excluding prelude names; global map applied to ALL modules (including entry). (6) `src/semantic/borrow.rs`: `is_enum_constructor` detection in `Expr::MethodCall` handler applies implicit-move logic for qualified variant constructors. (7) `src/semantic/derive.rs`: `variant_prefix` helper returns `"TypeName."` for non-generic enums, `""` for generic enums; all 6 enum derive generators updated. (8) `src/parser/ast.rs` + `src/parser/mod.rs`: Added `glob_types: Vec<Spanned<String>>` to `ImportStmt::From`; parser detects `Name.*` glob syntax. (9) All stdlib modules auto-qualified via global map. (10) `tests/fixtures/namespace_basic.gg` (NEW): exercises qualified variants alongside bare prelude variants; added to `tests/integration.rs`. (11) Docs updated: `language-reference.md` §5.3/§5.6, `language-design.md` §4.2/§8.3, `CLAUDE.md` syntax rules. 624/624 unit + 335/335 integration tests pass.

- [2026-03-02] **Inline Trait Bounds for Generic Parameters**: Moved trait bounds from trailing `where T is Trait` clauses into the generic parameter list using type-first syntax `[Printable T]`. Multiple bounds use `&` separator: `[A & B T]`. The `where` keyword is retained solely for `outlives` borrow-group ordering. Changes: (1) AST: `GenericParam::Type` changed from tuple to struct with `name` + `bounds` fields. (2) Parser: `parse_generic_params` speculatively tries `Trait [& Trait]* Name` before falling back to bare name; `parse_trait_bound_list` separator changed from `+` to `&`; `parse_where_clause` emits a clear error for `where T is Trait` guiding to inline syntax. (3) Semantic: `extract_where_bounds` replaced with `extract_generic_bounds` reading from inline param bounds. (4) Formatter: emits `Bound1 & Bound2 T` in generic param lists. (5) Tests + fixtures updated: `trait_bounds.gg`, 3 typecheck tests, 2 parser tests, `integration.rs`. (6) Documentation updated throughout `language-reference.md` and `language-design.md`. 600/600 unit + 328/328 integration tests pass.

- [2026-03-02] **`gg sim` Phase 7 — Backtrace & Source Location**: Four sub-phases implemented. P7a: Added `span_map: Vec<Option<Span>>` and `terminator_span: Option<Span>` to `BasicBlock`; `source_filename/source_code: Option<String>` to `Module`; `def_span: Option<Span>` to `Function` in `src/ir/mod.rs`. Updated `FunctionBuilder` in `builder.rs` with `current_span` field, `set_span()` method, and `emit()`/`set_terminator()` now record spans in parallel. P7b: `lower_stmt` calls `builder.set_span(stmt.span)` at entry; `lower_expr_inner` calls `builder.set_span(expr.span)` at entry; `lower_function` captures `func.span` and sets `def_span` after build. `main.rs` sim arm sets `gir_module.source_filename`/`source_code`. P7c: Added `StackFrame` struct (fn_name, display_name, call_span, def_span, current_span); added `call_stack: Vec<StackFrame>`, `current_instr_span`, `last_error_backtrace`, `last_error_span`, `backtrace_level` to `Interpreter`. Modified `call_function` to push frame on entry, update current_span per instruction, and use `sim_error_return!` macro for all error paths that captures backtrace (first time only), truncates call_stack to pre-call depth, and restores `prev_fn_name` via `std::mem::take`. All `?` in terminator arms converted to explicit matches. Added `capture_backtrace()` method. Added call_stack/error-state reset between test runs in `run_test_suite`. P7d: Created `src/sim/source_loc.rs` with `LineIndex` (binary-search line/col) and `format_sim_error` (error + source snippet + backtrace, respects `--backtrace=0|1|full`). Rewrote `run_main` to collect backtrace context and call `format_sim_error`. Backtrace frame location: innermost shows `current_span`, outer frames show the inner frame's `call_span` (where in that frame the callee was invoked). All regressions green: 283/283 sim PASS (1 SKIP), 6/6 UB PASS, 328/328 integration PASS, 600/600 unit PASS.

- [2026-03-02] **New Stdlib Modules (Batch 2)**: Added 6 new standard library modules: `gg.cli` (CLI arg parsing), `gg.log` (leveled logging), `gg.uuid` (UUID v4), `std.term` (terminal colors + TTY detection), `std.datetime` (calendar-aware dates with C-backed decompose), `std.heap` (generic binary min-heap). Also fixed 4 bugs discovered during implementation: (1) Parser `looks_like_closure()` false positive — `if condition and (expr):` was misidentified as a closure because `(...):`  looks like closure syntax; workaround: extract parenthesized sub-expression to local variable in cli.gg. (2) `LogLevel.Error` naming conflict with built-in `Error()` constructor for Result — renamed to `Err`. (3) `template_type_names()` in C backend didn't recognize `Heap__T` as a template (its only field is GorgetArray, not UNIT_TYPE) — added naming-convention check: types ending in `__X` (single uppercase letter) are treated as uninstantiated generics. (4) `register_equip_sigs_with_defaults()` used immutable type mapper, causing `Option[double]` to return UNIT_TYPE for `Heap[float]` methods registered before `Option[double]` was discovered — switched to mutable mapper (`substitute_and_map_mut`) so new types are auto-registered on first encounter. Also added a TODO for the GorgetString→Str coercion bug in Result compound literals. 624/624 unit + 334/334 integration tests pass.

(3) JSON dedup + String buffer: merged `json_stringify` + `json_pretty_internal` into `json_stringify_internal(Json, bool pretty, int depth)` with String buffer; eliminates O(n²) concatenation and ~90 lines of duplication; `json_stringify`/`json_pretty` are now thin wrappers. (4) XML String buffer: rewrote `xml_stringify_node`/`xml_stringify` to use String buffer instead of `str` concatenation in loop; eliminates O(n²) for large documents. (5) GFX null-check / error handling: changed `gfx_open` to return `Result[Canvas, str]`; added `sdl_create_window_try`, `sdl_create_renderer_try`, `sdl_window_is_null`, `sdl_renderer_is_null`, `sdl_get_error` to C runtime + `stdlib.rs` + `mod.rs` dispatch; updated examples. - [2026-03-02] **`gg sim` Phase 6 — Complete UB Detection (P6b + P6c)**: Wired heap-deallocation tracking through `run_drop_value` and added positive UB test fixtures. P6a (Dealloc emission from `drops.rs`) was deliberately skipped — it conflicts with P6b: both paths marking a slot dead on Drop → Dealloc would produce false-positive DoubleFree on every normal Box drop. P6b only: in `dispatch.rs` `run_drop_value` MutPtr/Ptr arm, added pre-check that raises `DoubleFree` if slot is already dead (catches double-drop via Custom Drop field-clone loop), then marks the slot dead after dropping inner value. Also changed Custom Drop's temporary heap copy from `heap_alloc` to `heap_alloc_ref_promoted` so the impl artifact is excluded from leak detection. P6c: Updated `tests/test_sim_ub.py` to support `.ub_expected` files (positive UB tests expect non-zero exit + stderr substring; clean tests use `--ignore-leaks`). Created 2 positive fixtures: `ub_double_free.gg` (Custom Drop moves Box field, field-clone loop tries second free → DoubleFree) and `ub_leak.gg` (extern `exit` bypasses RAII → allocation leak). Key limitations documented: `ub_use_after_free.gg` not implementable (borrow checker prevents accessing freed Box); `InvalidBoolValue` check is dead code (eval_cast always returns `Value::Bool(true/false)`). All regressions green: 283/283 sim PASS (1 SKIP = hot_reload_basic), 328/328 integration PASS, 600/600 unit PASS, 6/6 UB tests PASS.

- [2026-03-01] **`gg sim` GIR interpreter: 283/284 fixtures PASS** — Implemented full GIR interpreter (`src/sim/`) over multiple sessions. Key additions this session: (1) `Channel__T__poll_recv` for `select:` statement support — non-blocking try-recv used by the spin-wait loop generated by `lower_select`; (2) `regex` crate integration — added to Cargo.toml; implemented `try_regex_dispatch` with 25+ handlers for regex_compile/compile_with/escape, Regex methods (is_match, find, find_at, find_all, replace, replace_all, split, splitn, fullmatch, pattern_str, capture_count, group_names), Match accessors (text, start, end_pos, group_count, groups, group, group_by_name), and convenience free functions (regex_is_match, regex_find, regex_replace); (3) `MatchRaw` data extraction pattern to solve immutable/mutable borrow conflicts with `heap_alloc`; (4) `extract_caps` helper using `Regex::capture_names()` for named groups (avoiding non-existent `iter_names()`). - [2026-03-01] **Fix concurrency type registration: eager pre-scan via map_ast_type_mut** — Channel/Shared/Mutex/Guard/TaskGroup parameters in functions now resolve correctly. Root cause: `lower_function` uses the immutable `map_ast_type` (pure lookup) for parameter types; fn_sigs pre-scan calls `map_ast_type_mut`, which had no arms for concurrency types. Fix: added auto-registration arms to `map_ast_type_mut` in `types.rs` — Channel[T]/Shared[T]/Mutex[T] (Copy, no-drop), Guard[T] (Move, Trivial drop → `<mangled>__drop`), and non-generic TaskGroup (Move, Trivial drop → `gorget_task_group_free`). Also hardened Channel constructor intercept with lazy fallback registration (adds `ensure_channel_type_def`). The `ensure_*_type_def` functions and `gir_type_is_lazy_generic` in stmts.rs kept as defense-in-depth. New test: `concurrency_params.gg`. 328/328 integration + 600/600 unit tests.

- [2026-03-01] **Concurrency Enhancements: Shared[T], Mutex[T]+Guard[T], TaskGroup** — Implemented all three phases from the gorget-concurrency-enhancements plan. Phase 1 (Shared[T]): atomic ref-counted immutable wrapper; `clone()`, `get()`, field auto-deref; RAII release; SHARED_RUNTIME C implementation; `shared_basic` integration test. Phase 2 (Mutex[T]+Guard[T]): blocking pthread_mutex with RAII guard; `lock()` → Guard; `get()`/`set()` via void* interior; Guard drop calls `gorget_guard_release`; MUTEX_RUNTIME C implementation; `mutex_basic` integration test. Phase 3 (TaskGroup): structured concurrency — pthread_t array storing spawned thread handles; `spawn(task)` records thread; `join()` calls pthread_join on all; RAII drop joins + frees; TASK_GROUP_RUNTIME C implementation; `async_task_group` integration test. Key GIR fix: `gir_type_is_lazy_generic` condition extended to handle non-generic `TaskGroup` (no generic args), ensuring `needs_reinfer` fires for variable declarations. All constructor intercepts register TypeIds and TypeDefs lazily. 327/327 integration tests + 600/600 unit tests pass.

- [2026-03-01] **Retire legacy codegen (`src/codegen/`)** — Deleted all 14 files (~27,500 lines). GIR pipeline is now the sole code path for all commands. (2) **`src/lib.rs`**: Removed `pub mod codegen;`. (3) **`tests/str_runtime.rs`**: Updated import to `gorget::backend::c::c_runtime`. (4) **`src/backend/c/mod.rs`**: Fixed `emit_test_runner_main` to initialize trace (`__gorget_trace_init`) and emit `test_start`/`test_end` JSONL events when tracing is active — fixes GIR test-mode trace instrumentation that legacy codegen handled before. **Result**: 600/600 unit tests, 324/324 integration tests, 1/1 str_runtime test pass. Net removal: ~27,500 lines.

- [2026-03-01] **GIR Phase 5 complete** — Trace instrumentation and hot-reload fully implemented in the GIR pipeline; legacy codegen fallbacks removed. Changes: (1) **`src/ir/mod.rs`**: Added `trace_filename`, `hot_reload`, `hot_reload_state_type/hash/has_reload_fn` to `Module`; `display_name: Option<String>` to `Function`. (2) **`src/ir/builder.rs`**: `display_name: None` in `FunctionBuilder::build()`. - [2026-03-01] GIR Phase 4 complete — remove `--legacy-codegen` user-facing flag. Removed `legacy_codegen` variable from build/run command paths; removed `legacy` variable from shorthand-run path. `use_legacy` condition simplified to `source_has_hot_reload(&source) || trace`. Both `build` and `run` legacy branches now trigger only on `trace || source_has_trace(&source)`. - [2026-03-01] GIR Phase 4 — `gg test` wired to GIR pipeline, rich assert diagnostics, cleanup-before-longjmp. Two integration test fixes: (1) **`test_failure`**: Modified `lower_assert` in `src/ir/lowering/stmts.rs` to emit rich assertion diagnostics for primitive comparison conditions (`int`, `float`, `bool`). For `assert a == b` (where a, b are primitive), lowers LHS/RHS as GIR operands, branches on CmpOp, and in the fail block emits `gorget_panic(gorget_format("assertion failed: left == right\n  left:  %lld\n  right: %lld", a, b))` via InlineC. String/struct comparisons fall through to the simple path (they need `gorget_str_eq` etc.). Helper functions added: `comparison_op_info()`, `assert_printf_info()`, `operand_to_c_str()`, `is_primitive_type_for_assert()`. (2) **`test_cleanup`**: Root cause — `gorget_panic()` calls `longjmp()` which abandons the test function's stack frame; cleanup-stack entries pushed by the test function body hold pointers to now-dangling locals. Fix: run `__gorget_cleanup_run(__gorget_test_cleanup_mark)` BEFORE `longjmp` in `gorget_panic()` (while stack is still valid). Added `__gorget_test_cleanup_mark` variable set by test runner before each test call. Updated `PANIC_TEST` in `src/backend/c/c_runtime.rs` (moved cleanup stack defs before gorget_panic to avoid forward-reference) and `emit_test_runner_main` in `src/backend/c/mod.rs`. Also fixed unit test compilation: 18 `lower_module` call sites updated to pass `&LoweringOptions::default()` (new 3rd arg), and `is_test_fn: false` added to `Function` struct literals in `src/ir/validate.rs` and `src/ir/mod.rs`. Final: 671/671 unit tests, 324/324 integration tests, 279/280 GIR tests (1 SKIP = io_input pre-existing timeout).

- [2026-03-01] GIR test suite: 280/280 PASS, 0 SKIP. Two fixes: (1) **test_ir.py extended** to handle compile-error tests (if ref build fails with semantic error AND GIR also fails → PASS; if GIR accepts invalid code → MISMATCH) and runtime-crash tests (if ref crashes with no stdout AND GIR also crashes → PASS; if GIR doesn't crash → MISMATCH). Previously these 30 tests were SKIP because test_ir.py couldn't handle them. SKIP messages now printed in output. (2) **`exec_output`/`gorget_exec_output` return type fixed** in GIR C backend: `infer_runtime_return_type` was returning `"GorgetString"` instead of `"ExecResult"`, and `returns_cstr` was wrongly including `gorget_exec_output`. Fixed both — `exec_output` now correctly typed as `ExecResult` struct. This fixed `test_process.gg` which was failing to build via GIR with `ExecResult`/`GorgetString` type mismatch. 280/280 PASS, 324 integration tests pass.

- [2026-03-01] GIR Phase 4 Switchover complete — removed `source_has_async()` from pipeline selection in `main.rs`. All async programs now use GIR by default (`gg build`/`gg run` without `--legacy-codegen`). 324 integration tests pass (including all 17 async tests through GIR), 249/280 GIR tests pass, 31 SKIP (error/crash tests not testable by stdout comparison). `source_has_async` function deleted; all 3 call sites removed from `build`, `run` command branches.

- [2026-03-01] GIR Phase 4: Channel/Spawn/Await fixes — 246 PASS, 3 BUILD_FAIL → 249 PASS, 0 BUILD_FAIL, 0 MISMATCH. Fixed 6 issues: (1) **Channel constructor intercept in `lower_call`**: `Channel[int](5)` is parsed as `Expr::Call` (not `Expr::StructLiteral`), so the Channel intercept in `lower_struct_literal` was unreachable. Added matching intercept in `lower_call` with on-the-fly type registration. (2) **Task type registration in spawn lowering**: `ctx.type_mapper.lookup_named("Task__int64_t")` returned `None` (not yet registered) and fell back to `I64_TYPE`. Fixed by registering on-the-fly if absent. (3) **ASYNC_RUNTIME not emitted for spawn-only programs**: Added `|| module.has_spawn` to `needs_async_runtime` condition. (4) **`emit_channel_and_task_defs` not called without channels**: Separated channel runtime emission from Task typedef emission — now `emit_channel_and_task_defs` fires when `needs_channel || module.has_spawn`. (5) **Await dispatch in wrong code path**: `a.await()` is `Expr::Await { expr: a }` not `Expr::MethodCall` — the `Expr::Await` arm just pass-through'd. Added full spawn_result_locals dispatch to `Expr::Await`. (6) **await check gated on type_name**: Restructured `lower_method_call` to check spawn_result_locals FIRST before type_name (since declared type is `I64_TYPE` not `Task__int64_t`). All 249 PASS, 324 integration tests pass.

- [2026-03-01] Fix hot_reload_basic MISMATCH→PASS (246 PASS, 0 MISMATCH). When `gg build --ir <hot-reload.gg> -o <path>` is run, the hot-reload path was ignoring the `-o` flag — the host binary landed next to the source file, but test_ir.py tried to run it at the `-o` path (empty stdout → MISMATCH). Fix: after a successful hot-reload build, copy the host binary to the `-o` path if one was given. One-line copy in the build command's hot-reload branch. All non-async deferred tests now PASS. Only the 3 async BUILD_FAILs remain (async_channel, async_channel_waker, async_select — require GIR async state-machine lowering, a major separate effort).

- [2026-02-28] Meta M8 — Built-in meta functions: `platform()`, `arch()`, `arch_word_bits()`, `feature(str)`, `debug()`. Added `MetaContext<'a>` struct threading `features: &[String]` through meta evaluator. `eval_expr` now handles `Expr::Call` for built-in names: `platform()` → target OS string, `arch()` → target architecture string, `arch_word_bits()` → pointer size in bits (64 on 64-bit targets), `feature("name")` → true iff `--feature name` was passed on CLI, `debug()` → shorthand for `feature("debug")`. Added `parse_features(&[String]) -> Vec<String>` helper parsing `--feature name` and `--feature=name` CLI args. Added `"--feature"` to `flags_with_values` (filename detection). `analyze(module)` → `analyze(module, features)` — threaded through all 10 call sites (try_build, build, try_build_ir, check command, TUI REPL, codegen tests, borrow tests, typecheck tests, ir_lowering tests). New fixture `tests/fixtures/meta_builtins.gg` + integration test. 245 GIR PASS, 671 unit + 324 integration tests pass.

- [2026-02-28] GIR Phase 4: +10 PASS (234→244), Integration tests 321→323 PASS. Five fixes in this session: (1) **Two-pass type registration**: `mod.rs` pre-registers all non-generic type names (struct/enum/newtype/type-alias) before filling in their fields/variants — allows mutual forward references between type definitions without panics. (2) **Topological sort for C struct emission**: `c.rs` emits C struct body definitions in dependency order using reverse-post-order on a dependency graph, preventing forward-reference `error: field has incomplete type` in generated C. (3) **`deref_inner_type()` for Box dereference** (`context.rs`, `exprs.rs`): `Box[T]` types are `GirType::Named("Box__X")`, NOT `GirType::Ptr(T)`, so `pointee_type()` returned None for them. Added `deref_inner_type()` that first tries `pointee_type()`, then falls back to looking up `Box__X` TypeDef's first `_0` field. Used in `Expr::Deref` handler. Fixed `self_host_parser` which had `match (*cond).expr:` — the explicit deref of `Box[SpannedExpr]` was getting `I64_TYPE`, causing field access to fail and C compilation errors (`_387.tag` / `_387.data`). (4) **`is_terminated()` guard in `lower_match_stmt`** (`builder.rs`, `stmts.rs`): Match arm bodies with `return`/`break`/`continue` set block terminators; the unconditional `builder.jump(merge_bb)` after them was overwriting those terminators (both point to `set_terminator` which unconditionally replaces). This caused `merge_bb` fallthrough code (e.g., `return "?"`) to always execute. Added `is_terminated()` check — when true, use `pop_scope_no_emit()` instead of `pop_scope()` + jump. Fixed `self_host_lexer` returning `"?"` for all tokens. (5) **`source_has_trace()` for pipeline selection** (`main.rs`): Programs with `directive trace` must use legacy codegen (trace instrumentation). GIR is now default, so `gg build`/`gg run` only checked `--trace` CLI flag, not `directive trace` in source. Added `source_has_trace()` text check + `|| source_has_trace(&source)` condition. Fixed `trace_directive` integration test. **Score**: 244 PASS, 1 MISMATCH (hot_reload_basic — deferred, test infrastructure), 3 BUILD_FAIL (async — deferred), 31 SKIP. 671 unit + 323 integration tests pass.

- [2026-02-28] GIR Phase 4: +1 PASS (233→234), **regex_basic** BUILD_FAIL→PASS. Root causes and fixes: (1) **`Option[Match]` not registered during equip block pre-scan**: `map_ast_type` (read-only) at `mod.rs:519` returned `UNIT_TYPE` for unregistered generics; changed to `map_ast_type_mut` so `Option__Match`/`Option__Str` are registered during equip fn_sig collection. (2) **Regex.find() return type wrongly overridden to `Option__int64_t`**: The `matches!(method_name, "index_of" | "find")` override in `lower_method_call` applied to ALL "find" methods; added `&& !is_regex_receiver` guard. (3) **`infer_runtime_return_type` overriding correct GIR Option types**: `gorget_regex_find` returned `"GorgetRegexMatch"` overriding correctly-registered `Option__Match`; changed to `None` for find/fullmatch/match_group functions. (4) **Stale `_compiled` suffix in `map_stdlib_name`**: `Regex__find_all/replace/split/splitn` mapped to non-existent `_compiled` variants; fixed to correct names. (5) **`try_emit_higher_order_method` catching Regex__find**: Added exclusion guard for `Regex__`/`Match__` prefixes. (6) **`GorgetString.data` field access returning `Constant::Unit`**: TypeDef has empty fields; added special-case to return the GorgetString itself. (7) **String literal constants wrongly getting `.data` appended**: Fixed `is_str` check in Regex handler — `Constant::Str` literals are already `const char*` in C. (8) **Generic option wrapping using `>= 0` for `GorgetRegexMatch`**: Added `inner_is_match` check in generic stdlib option-wrapping path — uses `.start != -1` sentinel for `Option__Match`. (9) **Missing `-lpcre2-8` linker flag in IR build path**: `try_build_ir` checked `source.contains("std.regex")` but never called `add_regex_flags`; added the call. (10) **Missing C wrapper functions for free-function regex calls**: Added `gorget_regex_is_match_pat`/`gorget_regex_find_pat`/`gorget_regex_replace_pat` inline in `generate_c` after `REGEX_RUNTIME` (compile-use-free pattern). (11) **Wrong `GorgetRegex` field name**: Wrappers checked `_rx.re` — actual field is `.code`; fixed. **Score**: 234 PASS, 0 MISMATCH, 6 BUILD_FAIL, 39 SKIP (279 total). 6 BUILD_FAILs are all deferred (3 async, box_callable, closure_escape, hot_reload_basic).

- [2026-02-28] GIR Phase 4: +3 PASS (230→233). **Fixed 3 MISMATCH tests**: nested_generics, drop_collections, drop_struct_collection_fields. Root causes and fixes: (1) **Double-free from shallow element copies**: After `gorget_array_push(&arr, &elem)` / `EnumInit(Some(x))`, both the source local and the collection's element copy shared the same `.data` pointer → double-free when both were freed. Fixed by emitting `memset(&local, 0, sizeof(CType))` after consuming collection operations and EnumInit in C backend (`emit_collection_method_call`, `EnumInit` handler). (2) **Nested drop loop variable shadowing (C scoping bug)**: Inner loops for `Vector[Vector[T]]` reused `__di`/`__de` variable names. In C, `Container* __de = gorget_array_get(__de, __di)` — the inner `__de` shadows the outer `GorgetArray* __de` in its own initializer (C scoping: scope starts at point of declaration, covering the initializer). Fixed by adding `depth: usize` parameter to `emit_drop_for_type_via_ptr`, `emit_field_drops_via_ptr`, `emit_field_drops` — nested loops use `__di{depth}`/`__de{depth}` (e.g., `__di1`/`__de1` for depth=1). Callers within outer `__di`/`__de` loops pass `depth=1`; standalone callers pass `depth=0`. (3) **`emit_field_drops` not handling Vector fields**: `emit_field_drops` used `lookup_drop_strategy` which required TypeDef registry lookup. `Vector__int64_t` TypeDef lookup worked, but for struct-type fields nested in outer structs, the strategy resolved to None. Fixed by rewriting `emit_field_drops` to use name-based `needs_drop_by_name` + `emit_drop_for_type_via_ptr` (same approach as `emit_field_drops_via_ptr`). (4) **Unconditional Drop on synthetically-zeroed structs**: After memset-zeroing a local (post-push), the GIR-generated `Drop` (unconditional) fired and called `Container__drop` on the zeroed struct, printing spurious "drop  len=0". Added zero-check (`memcmp`) to `Drop` handler for `Custom`/`Recursive` struct types — mirrors `DropIfAlive`'s defense-in-depth. (5) **Wrong drop count for deep-cloned locals**: Variables declared as `auto w = wrappers.get(0).unwrap()` were not registered for Drop because `gir_type` was I64_TYPE (fallback for unknown return types) at drop-registration time. After type re-inference set the type to `Wrapper`, the DropElaborator entry wasn't updated. Fixed by adding `DropElaborator::update_or_register_type()` in `drops.rs` and calling it after re-inference in `stmts.rs::lower_var_decl`. **Score**: 233 PASS, 0 MISMATCH, 7 BUILD_FAIL, 39 SKIP (279 total). 671 unit tests pass.

- [2026-02-28] GIR Phase 4: +12 PASS (218→230). **MutableBorrow pointer forwarding**: Fixed `lower_call_arg()` in exprs.rs — when `&name` appears in a call argument and `name` is a MutableBorrow param (already a pointer in `mut_capture_locals`), forward the pointer directly instead of auto-deref→copy→re-borrow. Root cause: `Expr::Identifier` auto-derefs `mut_capture_locals` entries (`_4 = (*_1)`), then `MutableBorrow` takes address of copy (`_5 = &_4`), producing a stale pointer to a local copy instead of the original. Key insight: call arg ownership (`CallArg.ownership`) is separate from `Expr::MutableBorrow` — `parse_ownership_modifier()` consumes the `&` token before `parse_expr()`, so `lower_call_arg` (not `lower_expr_inner/MutableBorrow`) handles these. Same fix added to `Expr::MutableBorrow` handler for non-call-arg contexts. All 12 P2P tests MISMATCH→PASS: p2p_basic, p2p_dht, p2p_discovery, p2p_encrypted, p2p_encrypted_large, p2p_gossip, p2p_multiplex, p2p_nat, p2p_protocol_rpc, p2p_reliable_basic, p2p_reliable_bidir, p2p_reliable_large, p2p_stream_robust (also moved to PASS by this fix: p2p_stream_robust was already passing). **Files**: `src/ir/lowering/exprs.rs`. **Score**: 230 PASS, 3 MISMATCH, 7 BUILD_FAIL, 39 SKIP (279 total). 671 unit + 323 integration tests pass.

- [2026-02-28] GIR Phase 4: +4 PASS (214→218, session total). **Via delegation**: Added `emit_via_forwarding_function()` in traits.rs — generates GIR forwarding functions for `equip X with Trait via field:` that PtrCast→Borrow field→PtrCast→Call target impl method. via_delegation PASS. **Box drop semantics**: Fixed Box TypeDef metadata in 3 locations (exprs.rs `ensure_box_type_def`, types.rs `register_collection_alias`, generics.rs `monomorphize_struct`) to use `CopySemantics::Move` + `DropStrategy::Trivial("free")` instead of default Copy/None. Key fix: `monomorphize_struct` was overwriting Box metadata with defaults when `from std.collections import Box` triggered template-based monomorphization. **Trait-object Box drops**: C backend now distinguishes concrete Box (pointer, `free(place)`) from trait-object Box (struct, `free(place.data)`) using `registry.get_type_def("{inner}_TraitObj")` check. Prevents `free()` on struct values. Fixed 5 regressions (dynamic_dispatch, print_trait_object, serializable, deserializable, serialize_collections). **Crypto/P2P linker flags**: IR build path (`try_build_ir`) now detects `std.crypto`/`std.net.tls`/`std.p2p` imports and adds `-lssl -lcrypto`/`-lpthread` flags. crypto_hash, crypto_x25519 PASS, 11 p2p tests BUILD_FAIL→MISMATCH. **Regex type overrides**: Added ~20 `gorget_regex_*` functions to `is_cstr_param_fn()` and `infer_runtime_return_type()` for correct argument/return type handling (regex_basic still BUILD_FAIL due to callable/iterator issues). **Files**: `src/ir/lowering/traits.rs`, `src/backend/c.rs`, `src/ir/lowering/exprs.rs`, `src/ir/lowering/types.rs`, `src/ir/lowering/generics.rs`, `src/ir/lowering/drops.rs`, `src/main.rs`. **Score**: 218 PASS, 15 MISMATCH, 7 BUILD_FAIL, 39 SKIP (279 total). 671 unit + 323 integration tests pass.

- [2026-02-28] GIR: Fix pool_basic MISMATCH — allocator pointer types and `with` block lowering. **Root causes**: (1) Allocator types (PoolAllocator, TrackingAllocator, Arena) were never registered in the GIR type registry, so `lookup_named("PoolAllocator")` returned `None` and fell back to `I64_TYPE`. Method calls on these types got spurious `Borrow` instructions (`&pool` producing `T**` instead of `T*`). Fix: register allocator types as `Ptr(Named("GorgetT"))` in `mod.rs` during module initialization. (2) `infer_type_name_from_operand_full` only checked dereferenced type against named_types map, missing pointer-wrapped types. Fix: check both original `type_id` and dereferenced `effective_tid`. (3) `lower_with` did not emit `PushAllocator`/`PopAllocator` instructions, so the pool was never installed as the active thread-local allocator. Fix: detect allocator constructors and emit push/pop/destroy. **Files**: `src/ir/lowering/mod.rs`, `src/ir/lowering/exprs.rs`, `src/ir/lowering/stmts.rs`. GIR test results: 173→194 PASS, 22→13 MISMATCH, 45→33 BUILD_FAIL. 671 unit tests pass.

- [2026-02-25] GIR Phase 3 Deferred Items (P3.5.0–P3.5.7): String Interpolation, Collection Literals, Comprehensions, and More. Implemented all non-async deferred items from Phase 3. **P3.5.0 — String Interpolation**: `lower_string_interpolation()` builds format string + args, dispatches to `gorget_string_format` extern. Handles int (`%lld`), float (`%.17g`), bool (branch to `"true"`/`"false"` via `%s`), str/String (`%s`). **P3.5.1 — Array Literals**: `lower_array_literal()` creates `gorget_array_new(sizeof(elem_type))` + N `gorget_array_push()` calls. Empty arrays use `sizeof(int64_t)` default. New `Constant::SizeOf(TypeId)` variant for type-aware sizeof. **P3.5.2 — Dict Literals**: `lower_dict_literal()` infers key/value types from first pair, creates mangled `Dict__K__V__new()` + N `Dict__K__V__put()` calls. **P3.5.3 — List Comprehensions**: `lower_list_comprehension()` creates array + range-based for-loop (header/body/inc/exit blocks) + optional filter branch + push. **P3.5.4 — Dict/Set Comprehensions**: `lower_dict_comprehension()` creates dict + loop + put. `lower_set_comprehension()` creates array + loop + push (set-as-sorted-array). Both support optional filter conditions. **P3.5.5 — Implicit Closures**: Desugars `ImplicitClosure` to `Closure` with implicit `it` parameter, delegates to `ClosureLowering`. **P3.5.6 — Optional Chaining**: `lower_optional_chain()` emits null check (Cmp Ne) + Branch → field access on non-null path, Null on else path, merge block. **P3.5.7 — Range Expressions**: `lower_range_expr()` creates `GorgetRange` StructInit with start/end/inclusive fields. `GorgetRange` type registered in `mod.rs`. **Infrastructure**: `Constant::SizeOf(TypeId)` in `instructions.rs` + `c.rs` (`sizeof(C_type)`) + `printer.rs`. `TypeMapper.owned_string_type` for `GorgetString` format specifier matching. 10 new unit tests. **Remaining deferred**: only async constructs (Spawn, Await, Select) — these belong to resumable lowering, a separate effort. 671 unit + 323 integration tests pass, 0 regressions.

- [2026-02-25] GIR Phase 3: Complete Lowering (P3.0–P3.5). **P3.0 — Break, Continue, Loop**: `context.rs` — Added `LoopInfo` struct (`header_bb` for continue, `exit_bb` for break), `loop_stack: Vec<LoopInfo>` field, `push_loop()`/`pop_loop()`/`current_loop()` methods. `stmts.rs` — `lower_loop()` creates body_bb + exit_bb with back-edge, pushes Loop drop scope. `lower_break()` emits early-exit drops + jump to exit_bb + unreachable continuation block. `lower_continue()` emits early-exit drops + jump to header_bb + unreachable block. Updated `lower_while()`/`lower_for_range()` to push/pop loop info. 4 unit tests. **P3.1 — Match Statements**: `instructions.rs` — New `EnumFieldLoad { dst, base, variant, field }` instruction for C union variant access (`base.data.Variant._field`). `builder.rs` — `enum_field_load()` method. `printer.rs` — `EnumFieldLoad` text format. `validate.rs` — `EnumFieldLoad` validation. `c.rs` — `EnumFieldLoad` C emission. `stmts.rs` — `lower_match_stmt()` with Branch chain dispatch, `lower_pattern_condition()` (Wildcard→true, Literal→Cmp Eq, Binding→enum variant TagOf check or true, Constructor→TagOf+Cmp, Or→short-circuit chain, Tuple→true), `emit_pattern_bindings()` (Binding→register alias, Constructor→EnumFieldLoad per field, Tuple→FieldLoad per elem). `context.rs` — `resolve_variant_tag()` for enum tag lookup. 3 unit tests. **P3.2 — Match Expressions**: `exprs.rs` — `lower_match_expr()` identical to P3.1 but assigns each arm's result to shared `result_local`, returns `Copy(result_local)` from merge block. 1 unit test. **P3.3 — Error Handling**: `stmts.rs` — `lower_throw()` (CallExtern to gorget_throw + Unreachable), `lower_assert()` (Branch on condition, fail path: fprintf+exit+Unreachable). `exprs.rs` — `lower_try_expr()` (TagOf + Branch: Ok→extract value via EnumFieldLoad, Error→propagate via early return), TryCapture simplified pass-through. 2 unit tests. **P3.4 — Miscellaneous Expressions**: `exprs.rs` — CharLiteral→I64, NoneLiteral→Null, SelfExpr→lookup "self", It→lookup "it", Block/Do→lower_block_expr (last Stmt::Expr = value), As→Cast, TupleLiteral→TupleInit, ArrayLiteral→placeholder, Deref→Projection::Deref, TupleFieldAccess→FieldLoad with index, Is→pattern condition + negation, NilCoalescing→tag check + branch, Path→enum variant or identifier. 7 unit tests. **P3.5 — Remaining Statements**: `stmts.rs` — `lower_with()` (push Block scope, bind values, lower body, pop scope), Unsafe→regular block, Item→no-op. **Deferred**: async (Select, Await, Spawn), comprehensions, ImplicitClosure, ArrayLiteral runtime. **Files**: `src/ir/instructions.rs`, `src/ir/builder.rs`, `src/ir/printer.rs`, `src/ir/validate.rs`, `src/ir/lowering/context.rs`, `src/ir/lowering/stmts.rs`, `src/ir/lowering/exprs.rs`, `src/backend/c.rs`. 660 unit + 323 integration tests pass, 0 regressions.

- [2026-02-25] GIR Phase 2.6: Drop & Move Elaboration. `drops.rs` (NEW, ~280 lines) — `DropElaborator` struct with scope stack (Function/Loop/Block scopes). `DropEntry` tracks owned locals with `maybe_moved` flag. `register_local()` only registers Move-type locals (checked via TypeMetadata). `pop_scope()` emits Drop/DropIfAlive in LIFO order. `emit_early_exit_drops()` for return/break/continue cleanup. `needs_drop()` checks CopySemantics and DropStrategy from TypeDef metadata. `context.rs` — Added `drops: DropElaborator` field to LoweringContext. `functions.rs` — Function scope push/pop in `lower_function`, `lower_equip_method`, `lower_generic_function`, `lower_generic_equip_methods`. Implicit returns emit scope drops before return. `stmts.rs` — `lower_var_decl` registers Move-type locals with drop elaborator. `lower_return` emits early-exit cleanup drops. `lower_while`/`lower_for_range` wrap body in Loop scope. `exprs.rs` — `Expr::Move` marks local as `maybe_moved` in drop elaborator (uses DropIfAlive instead of Drop at scope exit). 7 new unit tests (primitives don't need drop, Move types do, scope lifecycle, maybe-moved, nested scopes, early return, Copy types skipped). 643 unit + 323 integration tests pass, 0 regressions.

- [2026-02-25] GIR Phase 2.5: Trait & Vtable Lowering. `traits.rs` (NEW, ~545 lines) — `TraitVTableInfo` and `VTableMethod` structs for vtable layout. `register_trait_types()` creates `{Trait}_VTable` struct TypeDef (function pointer fields for each method) and `{Trait}_TraitObj` fat-pointer struct TypeDef (`{data: void*, vtable: const VTable*}`). `register_trait_equip_sigs()` registers mangled trait impl method signatures (`{Trait}_for_{Type}__{method}`) in fn_sigs. `lower_trait_equip_methods()` lowers each trait equip block as a GIR function taking `void* self_void` as first param, PtrCasting to concrete type pointer internally. `emit_vtable_globals()` creates static const Global with `GlobalInit::Struct` containing `GlobalInit::FnRef` entries for each method slot. `context.rs` — Added `pointee_type()` helper to resolve Ptr(T)/MutPtr(T) → T. `exprs.rs` — `lower_field_access()` now handles pointer types by adding Deref projection (generates `(*_N).field` in C). `infer_type_name_from_operand()` resolves through pointer types for method dispatch. `c.rs` — `format_field_decl()` helper emits correct C syntax for function pointer struct fields (`RetType (*name)(Params)` vs standalone `RetType(*)(Params)`). `resolve_field_name()` handles Deref projections when walking through pointer types. `mod.rs` — `pub mod traits;`, full pipeline integration: register_trait_types → register_trait_equip_sigs → lower_trait_equip_methods → emit_vtable_globals. 4 new integration tests (vtable types, equip methods, vtable globals, C output). 636 unit + 323 integration tests pass.

- [2026-02-25] GIR Phase 2.4: Closure Lowering. `closures.rs` (NEW, ~480 lines) — `ClosureLowering` struct with `lower_closure()` creates `__Closure_N` env struct TypeDef + registers `__Closure_N__call` signature. Free variable collection via `FreeVarCollector` AST visitor (skips nested closures, tracks local bindings). Mutation detection for ByMutRef capture mode. Creation-site StructInit emission (ByValue → Copy, ByMutRef → BorrowMut pointer). `emit_closure_call_function()` creates GIR Function with env pointer param, field loads for captures, body lowering. Return type inference from body expression. `context.rs` — Added `type_registry: TypeRegistry` (owned during lowering, moved from Module), `closures: ClosureLowering`, `closure_info` map for call dispatch. `register_ptr_type()`/`register_mut_ptr_type()` now use internal registry. `populate_struct_fields()` uses internal registry. Architectural change: TypeRegistry moved into LoweringContext during lowering phase, returned to Module before validation. `mod.rs` — `pub mod closures;`, closure emission after function lowering via `std::mem::take` pattern. `exprs.rs` — `Expr::Closure` handling with `std::mem::take`/put-back pattern for borrow safety. `types.rs` — `Default` impl for TypeRegistry. 630 unit + 323 integration tests pass.

- [2026-02-25] String Code Review — Fix & Cleanup. **Bug fixes**: (B1) Fixed `substring()` using legacy null-terminated `gorget_string_slice()` — replaced with Str-native `gorget_str_byte_slice()` + `gorget_string_from_str()`. (B2) Fixed `File.read_all()` type checker returning `str` instead of `Result[String, str]`. (B3) Added NULL checks after all `al->alloc()`/`al->realloc()` calls in Str-native functions (to_upper, to_lower, replace, repeat, pad_left, pad_right, join) and `vsnprintf` return checks in `gorget_string_format()`/`gorget_format()`. **Inconsistency fixes**: (I1) Added `byte_len`, `byte_slice` to `builtin_method_return_type()` in c_expr_print.rs for both GorgetString and Str receivers. (I2) Added `PrimitiveType::CStr` to type checker method dispatch, routing cstr methods through str method type checking. (I3) Added Socket/TlsSocket `read_line()` return type (`Result[String, str]`) in type checker. **New features**: Implemented `.bytes()` → `Vector[uint8]`, `.codepoints()` → `Vector[int]`, `.chars()` → `Vector[str]` string iterator methods: C runtime functions (`gorget_str_bytes`, `gorget_str_codepoints`, `gorget_str_chars`), codegen dispatch, type checker entries, integration test (`string_iterators.gg`). **Legacy cleanup**: Deleted ~230 lines of dead `const char*` string functions (gorget_string_starts_with/ends_with/trim/strip/lstrip/rstrip/to_upper/to_lower/replace/at/slice/index_of/count/removeprefix/removesuffix/pad_left/pad_right/repeat and gorget_char_in_set). Fixed outdated `strcmp` comment in c_expr.rs. **Performance**: Replaced linear scan with binary search for Unicode case mapping exception tables (~50 entries each). **Docs**: Added `.bytes()`, `.codepoints()`, `.chars()` to language-reference.md string methods table.

- [2026-02-25] Strings S5: Unicode-Aware String Operations. Upgraded all string methods to Unicode-aware and migrated C runtime functions to Str-native signatures, fixing several correctness issues. **Unicode case mapping** (`c_runtime.rs`): Added `gorget_unicode_toupper()`/`gorget_unicode_tolower()` with range-based offset tables + exception tables covering Latin (Basic + Extended-A/B, U+0000–024F), Greek (U+0370–03FF), and Cyrillic (U+0400–04FF). ~120 ranges/exceptions per direction. 1:1 simple mappings only (locale-dependent/one-to-many deferred). **Unicode whitespace** (`c_runtime.rs`): `gorget_is_unicode_whitespace()` recognizes all 25 Unicode whitespace codepoints (Zs category + control chars: HT, LF, VT, FF, CR, SP, NBSP, OGHAM, EN/EM spaces, etc.). **UTF-8 encode** (`c_runtime.rs`): `gorget_utf8_encode()` for case mapping output (codepoint → 1-4 UTF-8 bytes). **Str-native search** (`c_runtime.rs`): `gorget_memmem()` (portable memmem, no `_GNU_SOURCE`), `gorget_str_contains()`, `gorget_str_starts_with()`, `gorget_str_ends_with()`, `gorget_str_find()` — all length-bounded, safe on non-null-terminated views. **Group A — View returns** (no allocation, return Str): `gorget_str_trim()`, `gorget_str_lstrip_ws()`, `gorget_str_rstrip_ws()`, `gorget_str_strip()`, `gorget_str_lstrip()`, `gorget_str_rstrip()`, `gorget_str_removeprefix()`, `gorget_str_removesuffix()` — all Unicode-aware whitespace/codepoint matching, zero allocation. **Group B — Allocating returns** (return GorgetString): `gorget_str_to_upper()`, `gorget_str_to_lower()`, `gorget_str_replace()`, `gorget_str_repeat()`, `gorget_str_pad_left()`, `gorget_str_pad_right()` — all Str-native, proper allocator usage. **Group C — Non-string returns**: `gorget_str_index_of()` returns **codepoint index** (was byte offset), `gorget_str_count()`, `gorget_str_split()`, `gorget_str_join()` — all memmem-based, Str-native. **Str→String coercion fix** (`c_runtime.rs`): New `gorget_string_from_str(Str)` copies using `.len` instead of `strlen()` — fixes incorrect behavior with non-null-terminated Str views (from `byte_slice()`/`s[i..j]`/`trim()`). **Typecheck** (`typecheck.rs`): Split single string method return-type arm — view methods (`trim`/`strip`/`lstrip`/`rstrip`/`removeprefix`/`removesuffix`/`byte_slice`) return `string_id` (Str), allocating methods (`to_upper`/`to_lower`/`replace`/`substring`/`repeat`/`join`/`pad_left`/`pad_right`) return `owned_string_id` (GorgetString). **Codegen type inference** (`c_expr_print.rs`): Matching split in `builtin_method_return_type()` for both `GorgetString` and `Str` receivers. **Codegen dispatch** (`c_expr_methods.rs`): Rewrote `gen_string_method()` shared section — removed `recv_data`/`extract_cstr_data` pattern, added `str_recv` helper for Str coercion, all methods dispatch to new `gorget_str_*` functions. New helpers: `gen_str_arg_from_call_arg()`, `gen_str_from_expr()` for argument coercion. No more `gorget_string_adopt()` wrappers — new C functions return correct types directly. **Str→GorgetString safety** (`c_stmt.rs`, `c_expr_call.rs`): Replaced all `gorget_string_new({expr}.data)` patterns (6 sites) with `gorget_string_from_str({expr})` — safe for non-null-terminated Str views. **Test** (`unicode_strings.gg`): 16 tests covering Latin Extended case (`café` → `CAFÉ`), Greek (`Ελληνικά` → `ελληνικά`), Cyrillic (`МОСКВА` → `москва`), ASCII trim, Unicode whitespace trim (NBSP), codepoint index_of, non-null-terminated view contains, roundtrip to_upper→to_lower, view/owned type verification, replace with unicode, starts_with/ends_with on multibyte, count unicode needle, split preserving unicode views. **Docs** (`language-reference.md`): Updated str method table — view methods return `str`, allocating methods return `String`. Added Unicode support scope section documenting covered scripts, whitespace codepoints, codepoint-based index_of, non-null-terminated safety, and deferred features. **Files**: `src/codegen/c_runtime.rs`, `src/semantic/typecheck.rs`, `src/codegen/c_expr_methods.rs`, `src/codegen/c_expr_print.rs`, `src/codegen/c_stmt.rs`, `src/codegen/c_expr_call.rs`, `tests/fixtures/unicode_strings.gg` (new), `tests/integration.rs`, `docs/language-reference.md`. All 601 unit + 322 integration tests pass, 0 regressions.
- [2026-02-25] Strings S4: UTF-8 Validation at System Boundaries. Added UTF-8 validation at three user-facing boundaries where external bytes enter the `str`/`String` world. All three now return `Result` instead of raw strings. **`File.read_all()`** → `Result[String, str]`: validates file contents, returns `Error("invalid UTF-8 in file")` on failure, frees the GorgetString on error path. **`Socket.read_line()` / `TlsSocket.read_line()`** → `Result[String, str]`: validates network data, returns `Error("invalid UTF-8 from socket")` on failure. **`bytes_to_str()`** → `Result[str, str]`: validates byte buffers, returns `Error("invalid UTF-8 in byte buffer")` on failure, frees the allocated buffer on error path. **Runtime** (`c_runtime.rs`): Added `gorget_string_is_valid_utf8()` convenience wrapper around `gorget_utf8_validate()`. **Stdlib** (`stdlib.rs`): Changed return types for `File.read_all`, `Socket.read_line`, `TlsSocket.read_line` from `ty_string()`/`ty_string()` to `ty_result(ty_string(), ty_str())`. **Codegen** (`c_expr_call.rs`): Added 4 codegen interceptions bypassing ExternBinding dispatch — `File.read_all`, `Socket.read_line`, `TlsSocket.read_line` (instance method interceptions before extern dispatch), and `bytes_to_str` (free function interception). Each generates a GCC statement expression that calls the C function, validates with `gorget_utf8_validate()`, and constructs the appropriate Result variant. Error paths free allocated memory (GorgetString or raw buffer). **bytes.gg**: Renamed extern to `__bytes_to_str_raw`, added Gorget wrapper `bytes_to_str()` returning `Result[str, str]` for type system compatibility. **Caller migration**: Updated 33+ callers across stdlib (`http.gg` 9 calls, `ssh.gg` 3 calls, `p2p.gg` 5 calls) and 14 test fixture files (25 calls) to use `.unwrap()`. `http_single()` uses `?` for read_line in Result-returning context. `file_io.gg` uses `String` type for `read_all().unwrap()`. **Test**: New `utf8_validation.gg` fixture testing valid ASCII, invalid bytes (0xFF/0xFE), valid multibyte (café), and truncated multibyte sequences. **Docs** (`language-reference.md`): Added UTF-8 validation boundary table, updated `File.read_all` signature, `bytes_to_str` signature, Socket description, and code examples. **Files**: `src/codegen/c_runtime.rs`, `src/stdlib.rs`, `src/codegen/c_expr_call.rs`, `lib/std/bytes.gg`, `lib/std/http.gg`, `lib/std/ssh.gg`, `lib/std/p2p.gg`, `tests/fixtures/file_io.gg`, `tests/fixtures/utf8_validation.gg` (new), `tests/integration.rs`, `docs/language-reference.md`, 14 test fixture files. All 601 unit + 321 integration tests pass, 0 regressions.
- [2026-02-25] Strings S3b-2: Flip `s[i]`, `s[i..j]`, `for ch in s:` to codepoint semantics. Changed string subscript/slice/iteration operators from byte-based to codepoint-based (Python-like). `s[i]` returns `str` (Str view of i-th codepoint) via `gorget_str_index()`, `s[i..j]` returns `str` (codepoint range) via `gorget_str_slice()`, `for ch in s:` yields `str` per-codepoint via UTF-8 walk. All non-allocating O(n) operations. Negative indexing supported. **Typecheck** (`typecheck.rs`): `s[i]` return type `char_id` → `string_id`, for-loop element type `char_id` → `string_id`. **Codegen** (`c_expr.rs`): Index branch passes full `Str` struct (coercing GorgetString/cstr) to `gorget_str_index()`/`gorget_str_slice()` instead of extracting `.data` for `gorget_string_at()`/`gorget_string_slice()`. Open-ended `s[i..]` uses `gorget_str_codepoint_count()`. `resolve_expr_type_id` for `Expr::Index` on strings returns `string_id` (was `char_id`). **Codegen** (`c_stmt.rs`): String for-loop changed from byte iteration (`char ch = data[idx++]`) to UTF-8 codepoint walk (`Str ch = view; byte_pos += cplen`). Added `Expr::Index` case to `infer_c_type_from_expr` returning `"Str"` for string indexing. **Async codegen** (`c_item.rs`): Async string for-loop state struct uses `Str` type (was `char`) + new `__for_cplen_N` local for codepoint length. Emission uses codepoint walk matching sync version. **Fixture migration**: `self_host_lexer/format.gg` last remaining `char c = s[i]` → `s.char_at(i)`. Rewrote `string_indexing.gg` for codepoint semantics (multi-byte UTF-8, negative indexing). New `str_codepoint_index.gg` with CJK, café, mixed-width UTF-8 codepoint indexing/slicing/iteration. **Docs** (`language-reference.md`): Added string indexing/slicing/iteration semantics table documenting codepoint-based operations and byte-level escape hatches. **Files**: `src/semantic/typecheck.rs`, `src/codegen/c_expr.rs`, `src/codegen/c_stmt.rs`, `src/codegen/c_item.rs`, `tests/fixtures/string_indexing.gg`, `tests/fixtures/str_codepoint_index.gg`, `tests/fixtures/self_host_lexer/format.gg`, `tests/integration.rs`, `docs/language-reference.md`. All 601 unit + 320 integration tests pass, 0 regressions.
- [2026-02-25] Strings S3b-1: Add `byte_slice()` + Migrate Parsers to `char_at()`/`byte_slice()`. Purely additive phase preparing for S3b-2 (codepoint-based indexing). **Runtime** (`c_runtime.rs`): Added `gorget_str_byte_slice(Str, start, end)` — O(1) non-allocating Str view, bounds-checked against byte length. Added `gorget_str_byte_at(Str, index)` — byte-level char access using Str length (replaces legacy `gorget_string_at` which used `strlen()`). Added `gorget_str_cat(Str, Str)` — length-aware string concatenation (replaces `gorget_string_from_concat` which used `strlen()`). Added `gorget_string_append_str(GorgetString*, Str)` — length-aware String append. Added `__gorget_hash_str_len(const char*, size_t)` — length-aware FNV-1a hash for Dict/HashMap keys. **Semantic** (`typecheck.rs`): Added `"byte_slice"` to str/String method return-type table. **Codegen** (`c_expr_methods.rs`): Added `byte_slice` arm with is_owned/needs_temp/normal branches. Updated `char_at` to use `gorget_str_byte_at` with Str. Updated `String.push(Str)` to use `gorget_string_append_str`. Updated `.hash()` to use `__gorget_hash_str_len`. **Print** (`c_types.rs`, `c_expr_print.rs`): Changed `PrimitiveType::Str` printf format from `"%s"` to `"%.*s"` with `(int)len, data` args — critical for non-null-terminated Str views from `byte_slice()`. **Concat** (`c_expr.rs`, `c_stmt.rs`): `str + str` now uses `gorget_str_cat` with `coerce_to_str` helper. `str += str` uses same. **C interop** (`c_stmt.rs`): `extract_cstr_data` for Str changed from `.data` to `gorget_str_to_cstr()` — creates null-terminated copy for C library calls. **Dict/HashMap** (`c_item.rs`): Str key hashing changed from `__gorget_hash_str(var.data)` (walks to null) to `__gorget_hash_str_len(var.data, var.len)` — fixes Dict lookup failures with non-null-terminated Str views. **Stdlib migration**: ~340 lines across 11 files: `json.gg` (27), `toml.gg` (54), `yaml.gg` (118+3 manual), `xml.gg` (24), `csv.gg` (3), `http.gg` (12), `encoding.gg` (41), `p2p.gg` (8), `bytes.gg` (7+9 manual), `self_host_lexer/lexer.gg` (24). All `s[i]` → `s.char_at(i)` for string indexing, all `s[a..b]` → `s.byte_slice(a, b)`. **Test fixtures**: Migrated `string_indexing.gg`, `string_index_oob.gg`, `char_method_on_index.gg`. New `str_byte_slice.gg` with multi-byte UTF-8 test cases. Updated `string_index_oob` expected error message. **Docs** (`language-reference.md`): Added `byte_slice(start, end)` to str method table. **Files**: `src/codegen/c_runtime.rs`, `src/semantic/typecheck.rs`, `src/codegen/c_expr_methods.rs`, `src/codegen/c_types.rs`, `src/codegen/c_expr_print.rs`, `src/codegen/c_expr.rs`, `src/codegen/c_stmt.rs`, `src/codegen/c_item.rs`, `lib/std/json.gg`, `lib/std/toml.gg`, `lib/std/yaml.gg`, `lib/std/xml.gg`, `lib/std/csv.gg`, `lib/std/http.gg`, `lib/std/encoding.gg`, `lib/std/p2p.gg`, `lib/std/bytes.gg`, `tests/fixtures/self_host_lexer/lexer.gg`, `tests/fixtures/string_indexing.gg`, `tests/fixtures/string_index_oob.gg`, `tests/fixtures/char_method_on_index.gg`, `tests/fixtures/str_byte_slice.gg`, `tests/integration.rs`, `docs/language-reference.md`. - [2026-02-25] Strings S3a: Switch `s.len()` to Codepoint Count, Add `byte_len()`. Changed the semantics of `str.len()` and `String.len()` from byte count to Unicode codepoint count (Python semantics), and added `byte_len()` for O(1) byte length access. **Semantic** (`typecheck.rs`): Added `"byte_len"` to str/String method return-type table. **Codegen** (`c_expr_methods.rs`): `len` arm now calls `gorget_str_codepoint_count()` (O(n) UTF-8 walk, already in runtime from S1). New `byte_len` arm reads `Str.len`/`GorgetString.len` directly (O(1)). **Bug fix** (`c_expr.rs`): `compute_escaped_byte_len()` was counting characters instead of UTF-8 bytes for multi-byte codepoints — fixed to use `c.len_utf8()` for non-escape characters. **Stdlib migration**: All byte-cursor-based `.len()` calls changed to `.byte_len()` across 11 files: `json.gg` (27), `toml.gg` (52), `yaml.gg` (84), `xml.gg` (33), `encoding.gg` (13), `csv.gg` (3), `http.gg` (10), `p2p.gg` (6), `bytes.gg` (1), `self_host_lexer/lexer.gg` (45), `self_host_lexer/format.gg` (1). Non-string `.len()` calls (Vector, Dict, etc.) left unchanged. **Test**: New `str_codepoint_len.gg` fixture verifying `len()` vs `byte_len()` with ASCII, multi-byte (café), and CJK (你好) strings. **Docs** (`language-reference.md`): Updated str method table — `len()` → codepoint count (O(n)), added `byte_len()` → byte count (O(1)). Updated `trim_prefix` example to use `byte_len()`. **Invariants**: `is_empty()` unaffected (zero bytes ⟺ zero codepoints). Indexing/slicing stay byte-based (deferred to S3b). For-loop over str stays byte-based (deferred to S3b). ASCII strings unaffected (byte count == codepoint count). **Files**: `src/semantic/typecheck.rs`, `src/codegen/c_expr_methods.rs`, `src/codegen/c_expr.rs`, `lib/std/json.gg`, `lib/std/toml.gg`, `lib/std/yaml.gg`, `lib/std/xml.gg`, `lib/std/encoding.gg`, `lib/std/csv.gg`, `lib/std/http.gg`, `lib/std/p2p.gg`, `lib/std/bytes.gg`, `tests/fixtures/self_host_lexer/lexer.gg`, `tests/fixtures/self_host_lexer/format.gg`, `tests/fixtures/str_codepoint_len.gg`, `tests/integration.rs`, `docs/language-reference.md`. All 601 unit + 318 integration tests pass, 0 regressions.
- [2026-02-25] Strings S2: Switch `str` from `const char*` to `Str` fat pointer. Changed the C representation of `str` from bare `const char*` to `Str` struct `{ const char* data, size_t len }`. This is the **breaking change** that enables O(1) `.len()`, length-prefixed comparison via `gorget_str_eq()`, and eliminates `strlen()` from hot paths. **Type mapping** (`c_types.rs`): `PrimitiveType::Str => "Str"`. **String literals** (`c_expr.rs`): `"hello"` → `gorget_str_from_literal("hello", 5)` with compile-time byte length. **Comparison** (`c_expr.rs`): `strcmp()` → `gorget_str_eq()` for `==`/`!=`. **Concatenation** (`c_expr.rs`): extracts `.data` from `Str` operands for `gorget_string_from_concat()`. **Indexing/slicing** (`c_expr.rs`): `gorget_string_at(s.data, i)`, `gorget_string_slice(s.data, start, end)`, open-ended `s[i..]` uses `s.len` instead of `strlen()`. **Printf** (`c_expr_print.rs`): `Str` → `expr.data` for `%s` format. **Coercion** (`c_stmt.rs`): `GorgetString → Str` via statement-expr intentional leak, `Str → const char*` via `.data`, `cstr → Str` via `gorget_str_from_cstr()`. New `extract_cstr_data()` helper and `infer_spanned_string_c_type()` for span-based resolution of pattern-bound variables. **Method calls** (`c_expr_call.rs`, `c_expr_methods.rs`): `gen_str_arg()` handles `Str`, all ~20 string methods extract `.data` from receiver/args. Method dispatch removes pre-coercion. **Extern functions** (`c_expr_call.rs`, `mod.rs`): New `returns_str` flag on `ExternBinding` wraps C return values in `gorget_str_from_cstr()`. Extern args coerced via `extract_cstr_data()`. **For-loops** (`c_stmt.rs`, `c_item.rs`): iteration uses `.len`/`.data` instead of `strlen()`/`[]`. **Runtime** (`c_runtime.rs`): `gorget_readdir()`, `gorget_args()`, `gorget_string_split()`, `gorget_regex_split()`, `gorget_regex_match_groups()`, `gorget_regex_named_groups()` all store `Str` in arrays (was `const char*`). `ExecResult.output`/`.errors` changed to `Str`. `GorgetUdpAddr.host` changed to `Str`. **Result/Option** (`c_expr_call.rs`): monomorphized type names use `Str` (was `const char*`). - [2026-02-24] GIR Phase 1: AST → GIR Lowering + C Backend. Built the full AST-to-GIR lowering pipeline and GIR-to-C backend, connecting the Phase 0 data structures to the compiler. **8 new files, ~1,400 lines.** **Lowering** (`src/ir/lowering/`, 6 files): `types.rs` — `TypeMapper` mapping AST PrimitiveType → GIR TypeId (Int→I64, Str→Ptr(U8), etc.) with printf format specifiers. `context.rs` — `LoweringContext` tracking locals (name→LocalId+TypeId), fn_sigs, type mapper; supports auto type inference from literal expressions. `exprs.rs` (~300 lines) — lowers Expr to GIR Operand: literals, identifiers, BinOp (with short-circuit And/Or via basic blocks), UnaryOp, Call, print (extracts interpolation segments → printf CallExtern with format specifiers). `stmts.rs` (~300 lines) — lowers Stmt: VarDecl, Assign, CompoundAssign, Return, If/elif/else (basic block diamonds), While (header/body/exit loops), For range (header/body/incr/exit loops), Expr, Pass. `functions.rs` (~80 lines) — lowers FunctionDef: Block and Expression bodies, parameter registration, implicit return (0 for main, unit for void). `mod.rs` (~80 lines) — `lower_module()` driver: creates Module, registers printf extern, pre-scans fn_sigs, lowers all functions, validates result. **C Backend** (`src/backend/`, 2 files): `c.rs` (~450 lines) — `generate_c()` translates ir::Module → compilable C: minimal preamble (stdio/stdint/stdbool), forward declarations, goto-based CFG emission. C name mangling for reserved words (`double` → `gg_double`). Type mapping (I64→int64_t, F64→double, Ptr(U8)→const char*, Bool→bool). Instruction→C: Assign, BinOp (+,-,*,/,%), UnOp (-,!,~), Cmp (==,!=,<,<=,>,>=), Call, CallExtern, Nop. Terminator→C: Return, Jump (goto), Branch (if/else goto), Switch, Unreachable. Bool-to-string conversion for printf args via ternary. **CLI** (`src/main.rs`): `--ir` flag on `build`/`run` commands branches to `try_build_ir()` which runs the GIR pipeline (parse → analyze → lower_module → generate_c → cc). **Wiring**: `pub mod lowering` in `ir/mod.rs`, `pub mod backend` in `lib.rs`. **Tests**: 15 new unit tests: 3 in types.rs (map_primitives, map_int_variants, format_specifiers), 3 in exprs.rs (lower_literals, lower_binary_op, lower_print_interpolation), 3 in stmts.rs (lower_var_decl, lower_if_else, lower_while_loop), 3 in mod.rs (lower_hello_world, lower_function_with_params, lower_multiple_functions), 3 in backend/c.rs (emit_empty_main, emit_arithmetic, emit_basic_blocks). **Integration verification**: all 5 target fixtures pass via `--ir`: hello ("Hello, World!"), variables (30/20/gorget), expressions (15/-5/50), functions (7/10/120), control_flow (positive/0-2/0-4). **Regression**: 597 unit tests pass, 315 integration tests pass, 0 regressions. **Files created**: `src/ir/lowering/mod.rs`, `src/ir/lowering/context.rs`, `src/ir/lowering/types.rs`, `src/ir/lowering/functions.rs`, `src/ir/lowering/stmts.rs`, `src/ir/lowering/exprs.rs`, `src/backend/mod.rs`, `src/backend/c.rs`. **Files modified**: `src/ir/mod.rs`, `src/lib.rs`, `src/main.rs`.

Added `select` statement for waiting on multiple channel operations simultaneously. **Syntax**: `select: case Type var = ch.recv(): ... case ch.send(expr): ... else: ...` — no scrutinee, each case arm is a channel operation, optional else arm for non-blocking fallback. **Lexer**: Added `Select` keyword variant. **Parser**: `SelectOp` enum (Recv/Send), `SelectArm` struct, `Stmt::Select` variant. `parse_select_stmt` / `parse_select_op` with `try_parse` backtracking to distinguish recv from send. **Semantic analysis**: Name resolution defines recv variable bindings in scoped blocks. Rewrite, visitor, closure mutation walker all walk select arms. **Codegen**: Sync codegen panics (typecheck enforces async-only). All dispatch points updated: formatter, contains_it, free-var visitor, generic-call/tuple scanners, stmt_contains_await/channel_op (always true). **Async codegen**: `async_select_counter` on CodegenContext. Analysis lifts recv vars and `__sel_send_tmp_N` fields to state struct. Emission uses probe-then-register pattern: probe each arm with NULL waker, if one succeeds execute its body, else register real waker with all channels and return PENDING. **Runtime fix**: Added NULL waker guards to `gorget_channel_poll_send` and `gorget_channel_poll_recv` — when waker is NULL (probe-only mode), returns 0 without registering. **Test**: `async_select.gg` — two producers on separate channels, select multiplexes recv, sums to 36. **Files**: `src/lexer/token.rs`, `src/parser/ast.rs`, `src/parser/stmt.rs`, `src/parser/expr.rs`, `src/parser/visitor.rs`, `src/semantic/resolve.rs`, `src/semantic/typecheck.rs`, `src/semantic/borrow.rs`, `src/semantic/rewrite.rs`, `src/semantic/errors.rs`, `src/formatter/mod.rs`, `src/codegen/mod.rs`, `src/codegen/c_stmt.rs`, `src/codegen/c_item.rs`, `src/codegen/c_runtime.rs`, `src/codegen/c_expr_closure.rs`, `tests/fixtures/async_select.gg`, `tests/integration.rs`. Added compile-time borrow checker enforcement preventing arena-allocated objects from escaping their `with Arena` scope. **Mechanism**: `arena_depth` counter (mirrors `loop_depth` pattern) + `arena_scoped_vars: FxHashSet<DefId>` tracking non-Copy variables declared inside arena blocks. **Detection**: `is_arena_type()` helper checks `ResolvedType::Defined` against Arena name. `Stmt::With` handler increments/decrements arena_depth when binding is Arena type. `Stmt::VarDecl` registers non-Copy bindings when arena_depth > 0. **Escape checks**: `Stmt::Return` rejects returning arena-scoped variables. `Stmt::Assign` rejects assigning arena-scoped values to outer-scope targets (handles both bare identifiers and `!move` expressions). **Safety**: arena_depth saved/restored across closure/implicit-closure boundaries (closures define independent scopes). Constructor `gorget_arena_new(capacity)` captures current alloc as parent, panics on OOM. `gorget_arena_bytes_used` (walks block chain), `gorget_arena_reset` (frees overflow, resets primary), `gorget_arena_destroy` (full cleanup via parent), `gorget_arena_free` (drop wrapper). **Stdlib** (`stdlib.rs`): `std.alloc` module with Arena struct + equip block (bytes_used, reset, destroy methods). **Type system**: Arena→GorgetArena* mapping in `c_types.rs` (ast_type_to_c + def_name_to_c). Arena method dispatch via `gen_arena_method`. `AllocRestore` drop action variant in `mod.rs` — emits `__gorget_current_alloc = saved; gorget_arena_destroy(arena);` on scope exit. `with` statement codegen (`c_stmt.rs`) detects Arena bindings via `is_allocator_expr`, emits typed `GorgetArena*` declaration + save/push thread-local + registers `AllocRestore` RAII cleanup. Both normal and break-flag `With` variants updated. Arena drop support: `OpaqueFree` for standalone declarations, `GorgetArena*` in `drop_action_for_c_type` before pointer catch-all. **Typechecker fix**: `Stmt::With` bindings now get inferred types assigned to their DefInfo (was missing — caused method dispatch to fall through to equip pattern). **Docs**: Expanded section 6.4 with panic vs Result policy. **Test**: `arena_basic.gg` — Vector allocation inside arena, bytes_used assertion, reset, nested arenas. **Files**: `src/codegen/c_runtime.rs`, `src/stdlib.rs`, `src/codegen/c_types.rs`, `src/codegen/c_expr_call.rs`, `src/codegen/mod.rs`, `src/codegen/c_stmt.rs`, `src/semantic/borrow.rs`, `src/semantic/rewrite.rs`, `src/semantic/typecheck.rs`, `docs/language-design.md`, `tests/fixtures/arena_basic.gg`, `tests/integration.rs`. Added `gorget_channel_poll_send()` (returns 1 if sent, 0 if full + registers waker) and `gorget_channel_poll_recv()` (returns 1 if received, 0 if empty + registers waker). Updated `gorget_channel_close()` to wake all async waiters. Updated sync `gorget_channel_send/recv` for interop: wake one async waiter before signaling condvar. Updated `gorget_channel_free()` to free waker arrays. **Detection** (`c_item.rs`): Added `expr_is_channel_op`/`expr_contains_channel_op`/`stmt_contains_channel_op`/`block_contains_channel_op` AST-level walkers (conservative: any `.send()`/`.recv()` MethodCall matches). All `block_contains_await` routing guards updated to also check `block_contains_channel_op` — ensures while/for/if/loop/match bodies containing channel ops go through async codegen. **Emission**: `ch.send(expr)` emits value → state field → suspension point with `gorget_channel_poll_send`. `Type var = ch.recv()` polls directly into `&__self->var`. `var = ch.recv()` polls into target. Added `is_task: bool` field to `CollectedAwait`. Removed the `if matches!(inner.node, Expr::Call { .. })` gate in `collect_awaits_from_spanned` — now collects ALL awaits (task and future). Added `infer_task_await_result_c_type_readonly()` helper for task result type inference. Updated `emit_expr_position_awaits` with two branches: task-await emits non-blocking waker-based suspension (lock→check done→install waker→PENDING / extract result→cleanup), future-await unchanged. **Part B**: Async for/else now correctly skips the else block when `break` is executed. Emission wraps else-body with `if (!__self->__for_broke_N) { ... }`. `Stmt::Break` in both `emit_async_stmt` and `gen_stmt` sets the flag before `break;` — handles breaks in non-await control flow (e.g., `if` without await inside async for-loop). - [2026-02-23] Await in If/While Conditions and For-Loop Iterables. Extended async codegen's Duff's device state machine to handle `.await()` in **conditions** (`while check(i).await():`) and **iterables** (`for i in 0..get_limit().await():`). Previously these fell through to synchronous `gen_expr`, silently blocking the executor thread. **Detection fixes**: Added `Expr::Range` arms to `expr_contains_await` and `collect_awaits_from_spanned` — range sub-expressions were silently returning false/skipped. Updated `stmt_contains_await` to check conditions in `If`/`While` and iterables in `For` (previously only checked bodies). **Analysis**: Added `analyze_expr_awaits()` helper — allocates sub-future fields + `__await_tmp_N` locals for expression-position awaits in conditions/iterables. `If` analyzes condition + each elif condition. `While` split from `Loop`; analyzes condition and widens body-lifting guard when condition has await. `For` analyzes iterable and widens guard from body-only to body-or-iterable. **Emission**: `While` with condition-await transforms to `for(;;) { emit_awaits(cond); if (!cond) break; body }` — condition re-evaluated each iteration via Duff's device. `If` with condition-await pre-evaluates via `emit_expr_position_awaits` before the `if`; elif conditions with await nest inside `else { await; if (cond) { ... } }` with `extra_nesting` counter for correct brace closing. `For` with iterable-await pre-evaluates iterable awaits (range bounds) before loop header — `gen_expr` on bounds picks up replacement map, cleared after header emitted. **Tests**: `async_condition_await.gg` (while+if with await in conditions), `async_range_await.gg` (for-range with await in bounds). - [2026-02-23] Async/Await Phase 13 — Waker Protocol + Event-Driven Executor + Non-Blocking Task-Await. Added `GorgetWaker` type-erased callback (the universal async primitive), event-driven main loop (sleeps on condvar, woken by workers via waker), and non-blocking task-await (`.await()` on `Task[T]` in async context suspends instead of blocking the thread). **Runtime**: `GorgetWaker { void (*wake)(GorgetWaker*); void* data; }` typedef + `__gorget_noop_waker` in `ASYNC_RUNTIME`. `parent_waker` field added to `GorgetTask`. `MAIN_WAKER_RUNTIME` (condvar-based main-thread waker, only emitted when `has_spawn`). **Poll signature**: `int (*poll)(Future*, GorgetWaker*)` — waker threaded through all 6 sub-future poll sites, SpawnCtx workers pass noop waker, main loop passes `__gorget_main_waker` (or noop when no spawn). **SpawnCtx worker**: captures `parent_waker` under lock, calls it after unlock + condvar broadcast. **Main loop**: event-driven when `has_spawn` — first poll runs immediately (`__gorget_main_woken=1` initial), subsequent polls sleep until worker wakes. **Non-blocking task-await**: 4 new arms in `emit_async_stmt` (VarDecl, Assign, Return, Expr) — checks `done` under lock, installs waker if not done + returns PENDING, extracts result if done. Race-free: either parent sees `done=true` (fast path) or worker calls waker (wakes main loop). Sync-context task-await (condvar blocking in c_expr.rs) unchanged. **Files**: `src/codegen/c_runtime.rs` (GorgetWaker, parent_waker, MAIN_WAKER_RUNTIME), `src/codegen/mod.rs` (conditional emission), `src/codegen/c_item.rs` (poll sig, sub-future sites, worker, main loop, 4 task-await suspension arms), `src/codegen/c_expr.rs` (noop waker in sync-context). - [2026-02-23] Async/Await Phase 11 — Expression-Position Await. Enabled `await` anywhere within an expression (`await f() + 1`, `await f() + await g()`, `f(await g())`), not just as a direct statement-level RHS. **Architecture**: Desugar expression-position awaits into sequential statement-level temporaries. `collect_awaits_from_spanned()` depth-first-left-to-right walks the expression tree collecting `CollectedAwait { inner_expr, await_span_start }` — inner awaits collected before outer (handles nesting). `emit_expr_position_awaits()` emits each collected await's sub-future assignment → state transition → poll case → result extraction into `__await_tmp_N` state field, then inserts `async_await_replacements[span_start] = "__self->__await_tmp_N"`. A subsequent `gen_expr()` on the full expression transparently substitutes each `Await` node via the replacement map (3-line check at top of `Expr::Await` arm in c_expr.rs). Detection uses `is_direct_single` pattern: `matches!(&value.node, Expr::Await { expr: inner } if !Self::expr_contains_await(&inner.node))` — direct single awaits use existing path, everything else uses new expression-position path. **Parser change**: `await` binding power raised from 2 to 33 (same as unary ops), so `await f() + 1` parses as `(await f()) + 1` rather than `await (f() + 1)`. **Extended `expr_contains_await`** to recurse into BinaryOp, UnaryOp, FieldAccess, TupleFieldAccess, Index, MethodCall, As, Try, Move, MutableBorrow, Deref, TryCapture, TupleLiteral, ArrayLiteral, NilCoalescing. **Test**: `async_expr_await.gg` — `await double(5) + 1` (single await in binop = 11), `await double(3) + await double(4)` (two awaits in binop = 14), `await add(await double(2), await double(3))` (nested await in call args = 10), `-await double(5)` (unary negation of await = -10), `total = total + await double(10)` (assign with expression-position await = 20). **Files**: `src/parser/expr.rs` (~1 line), `src/codegen/mod.rs` (~3 lines), `src/codegen/c_expr.rs` (~3 lines), `src/codegen/c_item.rs` (~200 lines), `tests/fixtures/async_expr_await.gg`, `tests/integration.rs`. - [2026-02-23] Async/Await Phase 7 — RAII for Async State Structs. Non-Copy fields in async state structs are now properly dropped at every completion site, preventing memory leaks for String, Vector, Dict, user structs with Drop, etc. **Architecture**: `AsyncAnalysis` extended with `drop_entries: Vec<DropEntry>` computed at analysis time — params use `drop_action_for_type()` (full AST type available), locals use AST type when explicit or fall back to new `drop_action_for_c_type()` (C-string-based, buffer-level drops). `emit_async_cleanup()` emits drops in reverse order with `__self->` prefix, then `free(__self); __future->state = NULL;`. `async_return_skip_var()` prevents double-free when returning a non-Copy local (ownership transferred to `__future->result`). **4 cleanup sites updated**: (1) `Return(Some(expr))` with skip_var, (2) `Return(None)`, (3) implicit void return at end of block body, (4) expression-body async functions. `drop_entries` threaded through `emit_async_stmts`/`emit_async_stmt` for returns inside control flow. **New helper**: `drop_action_for_c_type()` in c_stmt.rs — determines drop action from mangled C type strings (GorgetString, GorgetArray, GorgetDict/GorgetMap, GorgetSet, pointer types, primitives, user structs). Visibility: `drop_action_for_type()` and `emit_drop_entry()` promoted to `pub(super)` for cross-file access. **Zero-init safety**: State structs are `malloc+memset(0)` — all drop functions handle zero data safely (gorget_string_free checks cap>0, gorget_array_free calls free(NULL), StructDrop uses memcmp zero-check). **Test**: `async_drop.gg` — `Tracked` struct with Drop, `compute` creates and drops local before returning int, `main` prints result and drops its own local. Only recurses into control flow bodies that contain await — variables in non-await bodies stay as normal C locals (not lifted to state struct). **Emission**: Extracted `emit_async_stmts`/`emit_async_stmt` recursive methods from flat `emit_async_poll_body` loop.
