Fixes UB when throwing in spawned tasks (each thread now gets its own error/cleanup state). **Part B**: Bounded MPMC channel for inter-task communication. `Channel[T]` maps to `GorgetChannel*` in C — type-erased ring buffer with mutex + two condvars (`not_full`/`not_empty`) + `closed` flag. Copy semantics (pointer copy = shared access). API: `Channel[int](cap)` constructor, `ch.send(val)` (blocks while full, panics if closed), `ch.recv()` (blocks while empty, panics if closed+empty), `ch.close()` (sets closed, broadcasts all waiters). **Runtime**: `CHANNEL_RUNTIME` constant (~75 lines C) with `gorget_channel_new/send/recv/close/free`. **Module**: `std.channel` registered as synthetic module in `stdlib.rs` with `gen_channel_module()` (generic struct + equip block with `decl_method` entries). **Type system**: `Channel` added to built-in generics in `resolve.rs`, `Channel` → `GorgetChannel*` in both `ast_type_to_c` and `type_id_to_c`, `Channel` marked Copy in `is_copy_type` via `ResolvedType::Generic` arm (added `scopes` parameter to `is_copy_type`). **Codegen**: constructor via `gen_generic_call` Channel arm, method dispatch via `gen_channel_method` (send/recv/close), `Channel` excluded from struct-call rewrite in `rewrite.rs` and generic struct template collection in `c_item.rs`, conditional `CHANNEL_RUNTIME` emission + `#include <pthread.h>` when no spawn, `needs_threads = has_spawn || has_channel`. **Integration test**: `async_channel.gg` — two producers each sending 3 values via bounded channel, consumer summing all 6 values, verifies deterministic sum (21). **Files**: `src/codegen/c_runtime.rs`, `src/stdlib.rs`, `src/semantic/resolve.rs`, `src/semantic/borrow.rs`, `src/semantic/rewrite.rs`, `src/codegen/c_types.rs`, `src/codegen/mod.rs`, `src/codegen/c_item.rs`, `src/codegen/c_expr_generic.rs`, `src/codegen/c_expr_call.rs`, `src/codegen/c_stmt.rs`, `tests/fixtures/async_channel.gg`, `tests/integration.rs`. All 525 unit + 290 integration tests pass.
- [2026-02-23] Async/Await Phase 4 — Runtime: Thread-Pool Executor. Implemented pthread-based thread-pool executor so `spawn` creates tasks that run concurrently on worker threads. **Architecture**: `GorgetExecutor` with N worker threads (default = CPU count via `sysconf(_SC_NPROCESSORS_ONLN)`), mutex-protected FIFO task queue, lazy-init on first spawn. Per-`Future[T]` type: `__SpawnCtx__T` (embeds `GorgetTask` base + `Future__T`), `__SpawnCtx__T__run` (polls future to completion, signals done via condvar), `Task__T` (user-facing handle with `GorgetTask*` pointer). **Spawn flow**: evaluate args → construct `Future[T]` → heap-alloc `SpawnCtx` via `calloc` → init mutex/condvar → submit to executor → return `Task[T]` handle. **Await on Task[T]**: blocking condvar wait (NOT a suspension point) — `pthread_mutex_lock` → `while (!done) pthread_cond_wait` → extract result from `SpawnCtx.future.result` → destroy mutex/condvar → free. **Await on Future[T]**: unchanged busy-poll (Phase 3 behavior). **Key design decisions**: (1) `Task[T]` is now a distinct C type (`Task__T`) separate from `Future__T` (was aliased in Phase 3). (2) Task-await in async poll body treated as regular blocking assignment, NOT a suspension point — uses `is_task_await_static` check to skip sub-future state machine logic. (3) `analyze_async_function` skips Task-await expressions when counting sub-futures. (4) Type checker accepts both `Future[T]` and `Task[T]` in `await` expressions. (5) Executor shutdown called after async main completes (conditioned on `has_spawn`). **Detection**: `SpawnDetector` visitor walks all function/equip bodies for `Expr::Spawn` to set `has_spawn` flag, which gates executor runtime emission, Task typedef emission, and `-lpthread` linking. **Files**: `src/codegen/c_runtime.rs` (+`EXECUTOR_RUNTIME` ~80 lines C), `src/codegen/mod.rs` (`needs_threads` in CodegenOutput, `has_spawn` in context, SpawnDetector visitor), `src/codegen/c_types.rs` (Task[T] → `Task__T`), `src/codegen/c_item.rs` (`emit_task_typedefs`, `is_task_await`/`is_task_await_static`, poll body Task-aware, main shutdown), `src/codegen/c_expr.rs` (Spawn → executor submit, Await → Task vs Future branch), `src/codegen/c_stmt.rs` (`infer_c_type_from_expr` Spawn/Await cases), `src/semantic/typecheck.rs` (Await accepts Task[T]), `src/main.rs` (`add_thread_flags` at 4 cc_cmd sites), `tests/fixtures/async_spawn.gg`, `tests/integration.rs`. **V1 limitations**: thread-pool deadlock possible; `__gorget_jmp_stack` global (not thread-local); spawned-but-never-awaited tasks leak; `await Task` only in async functions. All 525 unit + 289 integration tests pass, 0 regressions.
- [2026-02-23] Self-hosting resolver Phase 3 complete — 231/240 fixtures match (96.25%). Ported name resolution pass (~2,400 Rust LOC) to Gorget as `tests/fixtures/self_host_resolver/` (~1,800 lines across 9 .gg files). Two-pass architecture: `collect_top_level` registers built-in traits/types/variants + all user-defined items, `resolve_bodies` walks function/method bodies resolving identifier uses via scope chain lookup. **Files**: `ids.gg` (sentinel constants), `errors.gg` (error types), `scope.gg` (scope tree with flat parallel vectors — avoids codegen memset bug with non-Copy Dict in &self methods), `types.gg` (TypeTable + ResolvedType enum), `resolve.gg` (two-pass resolver with typed accessor/len/Option helpers as codegen workarounds), `format_resolve.gg` (canonical DEF/SCOPE/RES formatter), `driver.gg` (CLI entry), `main.gg` (standalone 10-case test). **Rust side**: `format_resolution_canonical()` + `resolver_comparison` test in `tests/integration.rs`, `scope_count()`/`scope_parent()` accessors added to `ScopeTable`. **Comparison normalizations**: DEF spans stripped (Gorget AST lacks name spans), SCOPE lines excluded (Rust `Expr::Block` creates extra scopes). **Key codegen bugs discovered/worked around**: (1) `&self` methods + non-Copy field → function call triggers destructive `memset` (fix: flat parallel vectors + inline `.get().unwrap()`), (2) Dict uses `.put()` not `.set()` (`.set()` silently generates no-op), (3) pattern-destructured vectors lose generic type info for `.len()` (typed helper functions), (4) pattern-destructured `Option[Box[T]]` generates wrong tag names (typed Option helpers). All 284 integration + 506 unit tests pass.
- [2026-02-23] Async/Await Phase 3 — C Codegen: State Machine Transformation. Each async function becomes three C artifacts: (1) `__AsyncState_funcname` struct holding state tag + params + locals + sub-future fields, (2) `funcname__poll` function with switch-based dispatch that resumes at correct state on re-entry, (3) constructor function that allocates state and returns `Future[T]`. `Future[T]` monomorphized per-T with `{poll, state, result}` fields. `await` in poll body: store sub-future → set next state → fall through → poll sub-future → extract result. `await` in sync context: busy-poll fallback (GCC statement expression). `spawn`: identity (Task[T] = Future[T] for Phase 3). Async main emitted as `gg__async_main` + C `main` wrapper that busy-polls to completion. Variable prefixing via `async_lifted_vars: Option<HashSet<String>>` — same pattern as existing `mutable_captures` / `pointer_params` prefixing in `gen_expr` for `Expr::Identifier`. `Future`/`Task` type mapping in `ast_type_to_c` and `type_id_to_c` (Task maps to Future). V1 restrictions: await only at statement level (VarDecl RHS, expr-stmt, return), no await inside if/for/while/match, no async closures, no RAII for owned state fields. New integration test: `async_basic.gg` (multi-level async with await). Updated 2 codegen unit tests from stub checks to real async output. Files: `src/codegen/c_runtime.rs` (ASYNC_RUNTIME constant), `src/codegen/c_types.rs` (Future/Task type mapping), `src/codegen/mod.rs` (context fields, future type discovery, emission ordering), `src/codegen/c_item.rs` (AsyncAnalysis, state struct, poll function, constructor, async main, future typedefs — ~350 lines), `src/codegen/c_expr.rs` (identifier prefix, await/spawn codegen), `tests/fixtures/async_basic.gg`, `tests/integration.rs`. All 525 unit + 287 integration tests pass, 0 regressions.
- [2026-02-23] Async/Await Phase 2 — Borrow Checker Suspension-Point Tracking. Enforces V1 design: ban borrows across `await` points. Added `BorrowAcrossAwait { name }` error variant. Added `current_function_is_async` and `await_invalidated: FxHashSet<DefId>` to BorrowChecker. At each `Expr::Await` in an async function, scans all live variables with non-static `BorrowOrigin` and adds them to `await_invalidated`. `check_use()` emits `BorrowAcrossAwait` when a flagged variable is used post-await (invalidation-set model — only fires on actual use, not mere liveness). Reassignment clears the entry. `await_invalidated` included in `BranchState` save/restore/merge (union merge for conservative branch handling). `current_function_is_async` saved/restored in closure bodies (closures are not async). `spawn` correctly not treated as suspension point. Updated language-reference.md section 7.25 with suspension-point safety rules, type classification, and examples. 8 new unit tests. Files: `src/semantic/errors.rs`, `src/semantic/borrow.rs`, `docs/language-reference.md`. All 525 unit + 286 integration tests pass.

- [2026-02-20] Implement P2P Phase 12 — Stream Protocol Tags + Request/Response RPC. Two features: **(A) Protocol Tags**: SYN carries an optional protocol string (flag bit `0x04`). Protocol appended after all existing SYN fields as `[2B proto_len][proto_bytes]`. Fully backward-compatible: old nodes ignore extra bytes; new nodes receiving old SYNs see no `0x04` bit → `protocol = ""`. STREAM_SYN event carries protocol in `topic` field. SYN retransmit preserves protocol tag. **New API**: `p2p_stream_open_protocol(node, peer_hex, protocol)`, `p2p_stream_open_encrypted_protocol(node, peer_hex, protocol)`, `p2p_stream_protocol(node, stream_id)`. **(B) Request/Response RPC**: Framed request/response with correlation IDs, built on Phase 11 framing. RPC frame format: `[1B rpc_type] [4B correlation_id] [payload...]` (rpc_type 1=REQUEST, 2=RESPONSE). 5 bytes overhead per RPC message. **New API**: `p2p_rpc_send_request(node, stream_id, data) -> Result[int, str]` (returns correlation_id), `p2p_rpc_send_response(node, stream_id, correlation_id, data)`, `p2p_rpc_recv(node, stream_id) -> RpcMessage`, `p2p_rpc_recv_request`, `p2p_rpc_recv_response`. **New struct**: `RpcMessage(msg_type, correlation_id, payload)`. **Struct changes**: `ReliableStream` gains `str protocol` and `int rpc_next_id` fields. **Wire protocol**: 3 new SYN encoders (plain+proto, enc+proto, auth+proto) with flags `0x04`/`0x05`/`0x07`. SYN parsing extracts protocol at computed base offset (9/41/137 depending on enc/auth flags). Files: `lib/std/p2p.gg` (+2 struct fields, +1 new struct, +3 SYN encoders, +8 new functions, modified 2 existing functions), `tests/fixtures/p2p_protocol_rpc.gg` (new: protocol tag + RPC echo test), `tests/integration.rs` (+p2p_protocol_rpc). All 281 integration tests pass.
- [2026-02-20] Implement P2P Phase 11 — Stream Maturity (Flow Control, Keepalive, Framing, Graceful Close). Four sub-features: **(A) Flow Control Enforcement**: `p2p_stream_flush_send` now caps `can_send` by `remote_recv_window / mss` (prevents receiver buffer overflow). Zero-window probe in tick sends 1-byte segment at RTO intervals when `remote_recv_window == 0` and data is queued. Renamed struct field `recv_window` → `remote_recv_window` for clarity. **(B) Keepalive & Dead Peer Detection**: New wire type `56 = RELIABLE_KEEPALIVE` (8-byte payload: receiver_sid + sender_sid). New struct fields: `keepalive_interval_ms` (30s default), `keepalive_timeout_ms` (120s default), `last_recv_ms`. Keepalive sent when established stream idle for interval, no queued data. Dead peer (state 7) declared when silence exceeds timeout; `p2p_poll` scans for state 7, emits STREAM_CLOSED, transitions to CLOSED. `last_recv_ms` updated on every received stream packet. **(C) Message Framing**: `p2p_stream_send_frame()` prepends 4-byte big-endian length header. `p2p_stream_recv_frame()` reassembles into `frame_buf`, returns one complete frame or empty vector. New struct field `frame_buf` for reassembly. **(D) Graceful Close**: New state `6 = FIN_PENDING`: `p2p_stream_close` transitions here when `send_queue` not empty. `flush_send`, `handle_data`, `handle_ack` all operate in state 6. Tick drains send_queue then sends FIN. When send_queue empty at close time, FIN sent immediately (existing behavior). **Bug fix**: `last_send_ms` initialized to `time_ms()` (was 0, causing spurious keepalive on first tick due to `now - 0` = epoch time). Files: `lib/std/p2p.gg` (+4 struct fields, +4 functions, modified 8 existing), `tests/fixtures/p2p_stream_robust.gg` (new: framing + graceful close test), `tests/integration.rs` (+p2p_stream_robust). All 487 unit + 280 integration tests pass.
- [2026-02-20] Fix borrow checker: track implicit moves in struct/variant/newtype constructor calls. Non-Copy variables passed to constructors are now implicitly consumed — subsequent uses are flagged as UseAfterMove, double-passing as DoubleMove. Two code paths: (1) `Expr::StructLiteral` (parsed-as-struct constructors like `Wrapper(s)`) — inline move check in expression handler, (2) `Expr::Call` with `DefKind::Variant | DefKind::Newtype` callees — integrated into the args loop. Copy-type args and function parameters are excluded. Loop-local guard: non-loop-local vars inside loops skip implicit move tracking to avoid false MoveInLoop for `return Variant(var)` patterns (common in parsers/serializers). Added 5 unit tests: struct_constructor_implicit_move, struct_constructor_copy_args_ok, struct_constructor_double_move, variant_constructor_implicit_move, struct_constructor_param_not_moved. Files: `src/semantic/borrow.rs`. All 492 unit + 279 integration tests pass.
- [2026-02-20] Parser comparison test: 234/237 fixtures match between Rust and self-hosting Gorget parsers. Phase 4 (mismatch fixing) resolved 14 of the original 16 mismatches from 221→234. Fixes: (1) `where` clause skipping in function defs, (2) `live(a)` lifetime group name skipping in params, (3) match guard skipping (`if cond` after case pattern), (4) test `with` clause skipping, (5) `&Type` ownership markers in function type params, (6) inline extern function → `IFunction` (not `IExternBlock`), (7) `equip via field:` delegation skipping + `pass` in equip body, (8) list comprehension `[expr for var in iter]`, (9) set/dict comprehension `{expr for ...}` / `{k: v for ...}` with raw strings for braces, (10) `ImplicitClosure` unwrapping in Rust formatter (avoids complex recursive `it`-detection in Gorget). New AST variants: `ESetComp`, `EDictComp`. 3 remaining mismatches are fundamental limitations: `chars.gg` (null byte C string truncation), `math_constants.gg` (C `%g` float formatting vs Rust Display), `name_first.gg` (`directive name-first` completely different parsing mode). Files: `parser.gg`, `ast.gg`, `format.gg`, `tests/integration.rs`. All 280 integration tests pass.
- [2026-02-20] Implement P2P Phase 10 — Multiplexed Streams (Dual-ID Disambiguation). Each stream now has independent local/remote ID spaces via `(local_id, remote_id)` pairs. Responder assigns its own `stream_id` (no longer reuses initiator's), stores initiator's as `remote_stream_id`. All post-SYN packets carry `(receiver_sid, sender_sid)` — receiver matches by its own local ID. **Wire protocol**: SYN-ACK gains 4B `initiator_sid`; DATA/ACK/FIN/FIN-ACK gain 4B `sender_sid` after `receiver_sid`. SYN unchanged. **Duplicate SYN detection**: same `(peer_hex, remote_stream_id)` pair silently dropped. **MSS adjusted**: 1196 plain, 1168 encrypted (4B less for dual-ID overhead). Resolves probabilistic `next_stream_id` bump hack. Files: `lib/std/p2p.gg` (+`remote_stream_id` field, updated all encode/decode/handle functions), `tests/fixtures/p2p_multiplex.gg` (new: two concurrent A→B streams with independent data), `tests/integration.rs` (+p2p_multiplex test).
- [2026-02-20] Implement self-hosting parser (Phases 1-9). Built a complete recursive-descent parser with Pratt expression parsing for the Gorget programming language, written in Gorget itself. **Files**: `tests/fixtures/self_host_parser/` — `ast.gg` (~168 lines, all AST node types: Span, SpannedExpr/Type/Pattern, Type enum 8 variants, Pattern enum 7, Expr enum 30, Stmt enum 17, Item enum 7, plus supporting structs), `parser.gg` (~2042 lines, 60 token tag constants, 88 keyword tag constants, token_tag/keyword_tag dispatch, operator helpers, Pratt binding power tables, Parser struct with ~40 equip methods: token management, type/pattern/expression/statement/item parsing, backtracking via save/restore), `format.gg` (~590 lines, canonical AST→string formatter with typed accessor helpers for pattern-destructured Vectors), `main.gg` (~32 lines, 11 test cases), `lexer.gg` (symlink to self_host_lexer). **Codegen fix**: Modified `collect_value_type_dep_names` in `src/codegen/c_item.rs` to skip recursion into pointer-based generic wrappers (Box, Rc, Arc, Weak, Vector, Dict, HashMap) for topological sort — prevents false circular dependencies when structs like SpannedExpr embed Expr via Box. **Workarounds applied**: (1) `extends` → `parent_traits` field rename (keyword collision), (2) `const int` for top-level constants, (3) `"" + name` for pattern-bound str returns (borrow checker false positive), (4) typed accessor helper functions for `.get(i).unwrap()` on pattern-destructured Vectors (codegen loses generic type), (5) `has_opt_boxed_expr`/`get_opt_boxed_expr` helpers for matching on pattern-destructured `Option[Box[T]]`, (6) raw strings `r"{"` for brace literals (string interpolation), (7) `sexpr_vec_len` helper for `.len()` on certain pattern positions. All 487 unit + 272 integration tests pass.
- [2026-02-20] Fix bitwise operator precedence — `&`, `|`, `^` now bind tighter than `==`/`!=`/`<`/`>` (Python/Rust order). Previously used C's historical precedence where `==` bound tighter than `&`, causing `flags & 1 == 1` to parse as `flags & (1 == 1)`. Swapped binding power blocks in Pratt parser (`src/parser/expr.rs`). Language reference already documented the correct order. All 487 unit + 277 integration tests pass.
- [2026-02-20] Implement P2P Phase 9 — Peer Authentication (Ed25519-Signed Handshake). Added Station-to-Station (STS) pattern authentication to encrypted streams. Each side signs their ephemeral X25519 public key with their Ed25519 identity key; the other side verifies the signature and checks `SHA256(ed25519_pubkey) == sender_id`. **Wire protocol**: flags byte extended — `0x01`=encrypted, `0x02`=authenticated, `0x03`=both (Phase 9 default). Authenticated SYN adds 32B Ed25519 pubkey + 64B signature (137B total). Authenticated SYN-ACK adds same (141B total). **Handshake**: `p2p_stream_open_encrypted` now sends flags=0x03 with signed ephemeral key; `p2p_handle_syn` verifies initiator's identity (SHA256 + Ed25519 verify) before accepting; `p2p_stream_accept` signs responder's ephemeral key; `p2p_handle_syn_ack` verifies responder's identity against intended peer_hex. Verification failure → silent reject (return no_event or close stream). **Backward compatible**: flags=0x01 (encrypted only, Phase 8) still works. **New API**: `p2p_stream_is_authenticated()`. **SYN retransmit**: re-signs on each retransmit (simple, no stored signature). Files: `lib/std/p2p.gg` (+`bool authenticated` field, +2 auth encode functions, modified handshake/accept/tick, +is_authenticated helper), `tests/fixtures/p2p_encrypted.gg` (auth check added), `tests/integration.rs` (updated expected output). All 487 unit + 277 integration tests pass.
- [2026-02-20] Implement P2P Phase 8 — Encrypted Reliable Streams. Added opt-in per-stream encryption to the P2P reliable stream layer via X25519 ECDH key exchange + HKDF-SHA256 key derivation + AES-256-GCM AEAD. **New crypto primitives** (C runtime + stdlib): `X25519KeyPair` opaque type with `keygen/public_key/private_key`, `crypto_x25519_shared_secret` (keypair + pub → shared secret), `crypto_x25519_dh` (raw priv bytes + pub → shared secret), `crypto_hkdf_sha256` (salt, ikm, info, len → derived key), `crypto_aes_gcm_encrypt/decrypt` (32-byte key, 12-byte nonce, AEAD with 16-byte tag). **Wire protocol extension**: SYN/SYN-ACK payloads now carry flags byte + optional 32-byte ephemeral X25519 pubkey (backward compatible — unencrypted streams send flags=0). **Key exchange**: initiator generates ephemeral keypair at `p2p_stream_open_encrypted`, stores pub+priv bytes in stream; responder generates own keypair on SYN receipt, computes shared secret immediately; initiator computes shared secret on SYN-ACK using stored private key bytes + responder's pubkey. **Key derivation**: HKDF-SHA256 with role-based key separation (64 bytes → initiator tx_key/rx_key, responder gets swapped halves). **Data encryption**: segments encrypted with AES-256-GCM before sending, decrypted on receipt; nonce = [8 zero bytes | 4-byte counter]; fresh nonce per packet including retransmissions; MSS reduced to 1172 for encrypted streams (28-byte AEAD overhead). **New API**: `p2p_stream_open_encrypted()`, `p2p_stream_is_encrypted()`. Files: `src/codegen/c_runtime.rs` (+X25519/HKDF/AES-GCM, +openssl/kdf.h), `src/stdlib.rs` (+X25519KeyPair, 7 new functions), `src/codegen/c_expr_call.rs` (+6 dispatch arms), `src/codegen/c_types.rs` (+X25519KeyPair mapping), `lib/std/p2p.gg` (8 new ReliableStream fields, 4 new functions, modified handshake/flush/receive/tick), 3 new test fixtures (`crypto_x25519.gg`, `p2p_encrypted.gg`, `p2p_encrypted_large.gg`). All 487 unit + 277 integration tests pass.
- [2026-02-20] Implement ownership in Callable signatures + generic Callable params. Two features: (1) Generic Callable params — `T` in Callable signatures like `Callable[T(int)]` now monomorphize correctly via `substitute_type` handling `Type::Function` recursively and `Callable`/`MutCallable`/`ConsumeCallable` named types. (2) Ownership in Callable sigs — `&Type`/`!Type` prefixes in function type params (e.g., `Callable[int(&Counter)]`) parsed, threaded through AST (`Type::Function.param_ownerships`), semantic (`ResolvedType::Function.param_ownerships`), codegen, and formatter. Key changes: parser parses `&`/`!` before params in function types; codegen applies ownership to adapter closures, vtable signatures, fn_type_signatures, GorgetClosure dispatch arg wrapping (`addr_of` for MutableBorrow), monomorphized function signatures and body contexts (pointer_params, closure_vars); generic function calls wrap named function args in GorgetClosure adapters when template param is Callable-typed. Files: `src/parser/ast.rs`, `src/parser/types.rs`, `src/semantic/types.rs`, `src/semantic/typecheck.rs`, `src/codegen/c_item.rs`, `src/codegen/c_expr_call.rs`, `src/codegen/c_expr_generic.rs`, `src/codegen/c_types.rs`, `src/codegen/c_stmt.rs`, `src/codegen/mod.rs`, `src/formatter/mod.rs`, 3 new test fixtures. All 487 unit + 271 integration tests pass.
- [2026-02-20] Implement P2P Phase 7 — Reliable UDP Layer. Added reliable data transfer over the existing P2P module: sequence numbers, selective ACKs (SACK), retransmission with Jacobson/Karels RTT estimation, AIMD congestion control, and a `ReliableStream` abstraction. **New structs**: `SACKBlock`, `SendSegment`, `RecvSegment`, `ReliableStream` (23 fields). **Wire protocol**: msg types 50-55 (RELIABLE_DATA/ACK/SYN/SYN_ACK/FIN/FIN_ACK), MSS=1200B. **Public API**: `p2p_stream_open`, `p2p_stream_accept`, `p2p_stream_close`, `p2p_stream_send`, `p2p_stream_recv`, `p2p_stream_tick`. **Connection lifecycle**: 3-way handshake (SYN→SYN_ACK→ESTABLISHED), segmented data transfer with congestion window, FIN/FIN_ACK close. **Congestion control**: slow start + congestion avoidance (AIMD), fast retransmit on 3 dup ACKs, timeout retransmit with exponential backoff, SACK for out-of-order segments. **Compiler fixes required**: (1) borrow checker divergence tracking — branches ending in return/break/continue/throw excluded from merge; (2) loop-local move safety — variables declared within loop body can be moved; (3) span-based DefId lookup for pattern bindings — `lookup_def_by_span` replaces `find_def_by_name` to avoid wrong DefId for same-named variables; (4) `ArrayDeepClone` codegen — `.get()` on Vector of structs with nested vectors now deep-clones element fields; (5) `p2p_stream_tick` iteration fix — always remove index 0 for correct rotation through all streams; (6) stream_id collision prevention — `p2p_handle_syn` bumps `next_stream_id` past remote ID. **Key bug found**: struct constructor moves zero the source variable via `memset`, but borrow checker doesn't track this — `bytes_slice(chunk, ...)` after `SendSegment(..., chunk, ...)` reads zeroed data. Fix: reorder to create copies before struct construction. Files: `lib/std/p2p.gg` (~1470 lines total, ~660 new), `src/semantic/borrow.rs`, `src/codegen/mod.rs`, `src/codegen/c_stmt.rs`, 3 test fixtures, `tests/integration.rs`. All 487 unit + 270 integration tests pass.

- [2026-02-20] Implement P2P standard library module (Phases 1-6). Built a complete peer-to-peer networking stack over UDP. **Phase 1**: UDP socket primitives (`std.net.udp`) — synthetic module with `UdpSocket`, `UdpAddr`, `UdpPacket`, bind/sendto/recvfrom/poll/multicast ops, C runtime via POSIX sockets. **Phase 2**: P2P core (`std.p2p`) — file-based module with Ed25519 identity (crypto_ed25519_keygen/sign/verify via OpenSSL EVP), binary wire protocol (GG01 header + msg_type dispatch), peer management, poll-based event model. **Phase 3**: Multicast LAN discovery — ANNOUNCE messages via multicast group 239.42.0.1:5354, bootstrap node support, stale peer eviction, drain loop for disc_socket. **Phase 4**: DHT (Kademlia-style) — XOR distance, STORE/FIND_VALUE/FIND_VALUE_REPLY messages, local key-value store with bytes_slice copy to prevent use-after-free from shallow copies. **Phase 5**: NAT hole punching — ADDR_PROBE/REPLY for external address discovery, HOLE_PUNCH_REQ/NOTIFY via rendezvous peer, RELAY_REQUEST/DATA for relay fallback. **Phase 6**: Gossip/PubSub — subscribe/publish/unsubscribe on named topics, epidemic broadcast with TTL-7 and SHA-256 message ID dedup, forwarding to all peers except sender. Key lessons: (1) file-based modules need `bytes_slice(v, 0, v.len())` copies when storing Vector values to prevent use-after-free from codegen drop code, (2) poll-based tests need manual protocol driving (poll each node separately), (3) multicast needs drain loop for own-announce skipping. Files: `src/codegen/c_runtime.rs` (UDP_SOCKET_RUNTIME + Ed25519 in CRYPTO_RUNTIME), `src/stdlib.rs` (gen_udp_socket_module, crypto extensions, std.p2p registration), `src/codegen/mod.rs` (has_udp), `src/codegen/c_types.rs` (type mappings), `src/codegen/c_expr_call.rs` (udp_bind/sendto/recvfrom/join_multicast + crypto dispatch), `lib/std/p2p.gg` (~770 lines), 6 test fixtures (udp_echo, p2p_basic, p2p_discovery, p2p_dht, p2p_nat, p2p_gossip). All 487 unit + 264 integration tests pass.

- [2026-02-20] Implement pattern destructuring in VarDecl, for-loop, and list comprehension. Parser and semantic analysis already supported recursive patterns, but codegen only handled `Pattern::Binding` in three sites: VarDecl (`gen_var_decl` fallback), for-loop (element name extraction), and list comprehension (variable name extraction). Fix: (1) **VarDecl** — emit value into a fresh temp, then call `emit_pattern_bindings()` or `emit_typed_pattern_bindings()` (new) to recursively declare each binding with proper C types. For explicit types, `emit_typed_pattern_bindings` emits concrete C types and registers TypeIds in `pattern_var_types`. For `auto`, resolves value's TypeId via `resolve_expr_type_id` and extracts tuple field TypeIds from `ResolvedType::Tuple`. (2) **For-loop** — added `var_name_holds_element` flag tracking which iterable branches (Range/String/Map) already handle patterns; remaining branches (Array/Set/Iterator/C-array) emit `emit_pattern_bindings()` after element assignment. (3) **List comprehension** — uses `pattern_bindings_inline()` for inline destructuring in GCC statement expressions. Also added `pattern_var_types: FxHashMap<String, TypeId>` to CodegenContext as a supplementary type resolution map for pattern-bound variables, consulted by `resolve_expr_type_id` as a third fallback after `resolution_map` and `scoped_lookup`. This fixes `print(s)` format inference for pattern-bound `str` variables. Files: `c_stmt.rs`, `c_expr_pattern.rs`, `c_expr.rs`, `mod.rs`, `pattern_destructure.gg` (new), `pattern_destructure_loop.gg` (new), `integration.rs`. All 487 unit + 260 integration tests pass.
- [2026-02-20] Extract `try_parse()` backtracking helper. Added `try_parse(&mut self, f: FnOnce -> Option<T>) -> Option<T>` to Parser — saves position, runs closure, restores on `None`. Replaced all 5 manual `saved_pos` sites: (1) generic method vs field+index in `parse_postfix` (2 restore points → 1 `try_parse`), (2) generic call vs index in `parse_postfix` (1 restore point), (3) typed vs untyped closure params in `parse_closure` (2 restore points), (4) declaration vs expression in `parse_decl_or_expr_stmt` (3 restore points), (5) name-first declaration in `parse_name_first_decl_or_expr_stmt` (1 restore point). Key design: only speculative prefix goes inside `try_parse`; committed parsing (after `=` or `(`) stays outside so real errors propagate. - [2026-02-20] Fix Option[T] as struct field codegen. Three bugs fixed: (1) **Constructor mangling** — `Foo(Some("hi"))` emitted `Option__Some()` instead of `Option__const_char_ptr__Some()` because `decl_type_hint` was set to `Foo` (the struct) not `Option[str]` (the field type). Fixed by saving/setting/restoring `decl_type_hint` per field in both `StructLiteral` (`c_expr.rs`) and `gen_call` struct path (`c_expr_call.rs`), using `field_type_names` to look up each field's AST type. Fixed by making it recurse into generic type args for transitive deps, and adding `collect_field_generic_enum_deps()` to detect non-pointer-safe generic enum instances in struct fields. `emit_type_definitions()` now interleaves these generic enum emissions into the topological sort, with `emitted_in_type_defs` tracking to skip in phase2. (3) **Match pattern tag resolution** — `match g.message` (FieldAccess on generic enum field) emitted `Option_TAG_Some` instead of `Option__const_char_ptr_TAG_Some` because `resolve_enum_c_type_for_scrutinee()` couldn't resolve the monomorphized name from a TypeId. - [2026-02-19] Fix `elif expr is Pattern:` codegen bug. `elif ... is Pattern(binding):` followed by `else:` generated invalid C (`} else { if (...) { ... } } else {`) because `gen_elif_is_binding()` closed the inner if and dedented from the wrapper, leaving the wrapper open for the next `} else {` to close (orphaning the else). Wrapped elifs (is-binding and string-temp) now leave the inner if open — the next elif/else/final `}` naturally closes it. After all branches, wrappers are closed in reverse order (free temps, dedent, `}`). Same fix applied to string-temp elif path which had the identical latent bug. Updated `option_box_enum.gg` to use `elif else_val is Some(ev):` instead of `match` workaround. Added 4 test cases to `is_bindings.gg`: elif-is + else, multiple elif-is, mixed plain/is chain, elif-is hitting else. Files: `c_stmt.rs`, `is_bindings.gg`, `option_box_enum.gg`, `integration.rs`. All 487 unit + 255 integration tests pass.
- [2026-02-19] Document synthetic vs file-based module split in `stdlib.rs`. Expanded the module-level doc comment to explain both strategies (synthetic for C-runtime glue, file-based for Gorget logic), when to choose each, and step-by-step instructions for adding a new module (including variable prefixing for `lookup_by_name_anywhere` safety). (`stdlib.rs:1-51`)
- [2026-02-19] Fix `Option[Box[UserEnum]]` codegen for self-hosting. Three bugs fixed: (1) **Discovery gap** — `Option[Box[Expr]]` in enum variant fields was never registered as a generic instance because variant field types are stored as AST `Type` nodes, not in the TypeTable. Added `discover_generic_from_ast_type()` to scan AST enum/struct variant fields during generic discovery. (2) **Ordering gap** — generic enums with pointer-safe type args (like `Option__Expr_ptr`) were always emitted in Phase 2 (after user types), but they only contain pointers and can safely go in Phase 1. Added `is_pointer_safe_type_arg()` classifier; Phase 1 now emits generic enums whose args are all pointers/primitives. (3) **Variant constructor type resolution** — `resolve_expr_type_id` and `box_inner_c_type` didn't handle `DefKind::Variant` calls, causing `Box(Num(1))` to emit `int64_t*` instead of `Expr*` and `Some(Box(...))` to use `Option__Some` instead of `Option__Expr_ptr__Some`. Added variant→parent enum lookup in `resolve_expr_type_id` via `try_defined_id()`, added `resolve_variant_parent_enum_c_type()` helper for Box inner type, and rewrote variant constructor codegen to propagate per-field type hints from `variant_field_types`. Also added `Box` case to `substitute_type()` so generic templates with `Box[T]` fields produce `T*`. Discovered pre-existing bug: `elif expr is Pattern:` generates invalid C (tracked in TODO). Files: `c_item.rs`, `c_expr.rs`, `c_expr_call.rs`, `c_expr_methods.rs`, `mod.rs`, `types.rs`, `option_box_enum.gg` (new), `integration.rs`. All 487 unit + 255 integration tests pass.
- [2026-02-19] Implement `std.csv` module — pure Gorget RFC 4180 CSV parser and serializer. File-based module (`lib/std/csv.gg`, ~230 lines) providing: CSV parsing (`csv_parse`, `csv_parse_delim` for custom delimiters), table parsing with headers (`csv_parse_table`, `csv_parse_table_delim`), CSV serialization (`csv_stringify`, `csv_stringify_delim`, `csv_stringify_table`, `csv_stringify_table_delim`). `CsvParser` struct handles quoted fields (with `""` escape for literal quotes, embedded newlines/delimiters), CRLF/LF line endings, and first-error-wins error reporting. `CsvTable` struct provides header-based access: `row_count`, `col_count`, `get`, `get_named`, `has_column`, `column_index`. All variables prefixed `csv_` to avoid cross-module name collisions. Files: `lib/std/csv.gg` (new), `src/stdlib.rs` (3 registration edits + 4 unit tests), `tests/fixtures/csv_basic.gg` (new), `tests/integration.rs`, `docs/language-reference.md`. All 487 unit + 254 integration tests pass.
- [2026-02-19] Fix cross-type `map`/`map_err` type inference + implement compound `is` conditions. **Part 1 — map_err inference**: Added `("Result", "map_err")` case to `infer_closure_method_type()` in typecheck.rs — inspects closure return type to correctly infer `Result[T, F]` when the error type changes (e.g., `Result[int, str].map_err((str e): e.len())` → `Result[int, int]`). Added cross-type map tests: `Option[int].map((int x): "val")` → `Option[str]`, `Result[int, str].map((int x): "yes")` → `Result[str, str]`, `Result[int, str].map_err(...)` → `Result[int, int]`. **Part 2 — compound `is` conditions**: `if a is Some(x) and x > 0:` now works. Four-layer implementation: (1) resolve.rs: new `resolve_is_condition()` walks `BinaryOp::And` chains left-to-right, defining pattern bindings after each `is` so subsequent guards can reference them; `has_is_patterns()` replaces `extract_is_pattern()`; scope push moved before condition resolution for all if/while/elif; (2) typecheck.rs: `assign_compound_is_types()` recursively assigns pattern types through `And` chains; (3) borrow.rs: `mark_compound_is_origins()` recursively marks borrow origins through `And` chains; (4) c_stmt.rs: `gen_compound_is_condition()` shared helper — evaluates scrutinee temps, builds combined condition with tag checks + guards (guards wrapped in GCC statement expressions with inline bindings for variable access), emits single flat `if` preserving elif/else chain; `has_compound_is_bindings()` detects compound conditions at all 3 detection sites; `flatten_and_chain()` decomposes `And` trees. Multiple `is` patterns also supported: `if a is Some(x) and b is Ok(y) and x + y == 15:`. Files: `typecheck.rs`, `resolve.rs`, `borrow.rs`, `c_stmt.rs`, `option_map.gg`, `result_map.gg`, `is_bindings.gg`, `integration.rs`. All 483 unit + 253 integration tests pass.
- [2026-02-19] Collection field support in `@derive(Serializable, Deserializable)`. `Vector[T]`, `Dict[str, V]`, and `HashMap[str, V]` fields now generate proper serialization/deserialization code instead of falling through to `.serialize(ser)`. Vector fields emit `begin_seq`/`elem`/`end_seq` loops with recursive element handling. Dict/HashMap fields emit `begin_struct`/`field`/`end_struct` with key iteration via new `keys()` method on `Deserializer` trait and `JsonDeserializer`. Option[T] fields generate `match Some/None` serialization and `is_null()` deserialization (codegen blocked by pre-existing Option-as-struct-field issue, tracked separately). Two infrastructure fixes: (1) derive-generated items now use unique span offsets via `Parser::new_with_offset()` with a global `DERIVE_SPAN_OFFSET` atomic counter (100K spacing), preventing `method_resolutions` span collisions that broke vtable dispatch with 3+ derived structs; (2) temp variable names use global `DERIVE_VAR_COUNTER` atomic counter, preventing `lookup_by_name_anywhere` type confusion across equip blocks. New helpers: `CollectionKind` enum, `parse_collection_type()`, `field_write_lines()`, `field_read_lines()` (both recursive for nested collections). Files: `src/semantic/derive.rs`, `lib/std/json.gg` (+keys() method), `tests/fixtures/serialize_collections.gg` (new), `tests/integration.rs`. All 483 unit + 254 integration tests pass.
- [2026-02-19] Implement `std.encoding` module — pure Gorget text encoding/decoding utilities. File-based module (`lib/std/encoding.gg`, ~280 lines) providing: URL encoding/decoding (RFC 3986 percent-encoding, `url_encode`, `url_decode`, `form_encode`, `form_decode`), HTML entity escaping (`html_escape` for `& < > " '`, `html_unescape` for named + decimal + hex numeric entities + 17 common HTML entities like `&copy;`, `&euro;`, `&hellip;`), UTF-8 utilities (`utf8_len`, `utf8_codepoints`, `utf8_is_valid`, `utf8_char_at` — all handle multi-byte sequences via bitwise decoding), Latin-1 codec (`latin1_encode`, `latin1_decode`). Key design decision: added `enc_byte_val()` helper (`ord(ch) & 255`) to handle signed char — `ord()` returns negative values for bytes > 127 since C `char` is signed by default, breaking UTF-8 byte range checks and percent-encoding. All variables prefixed `enc_` to avoid cross-module name collisions. Files: `lib/std/encoding.gg` (new), `src/stdlib.rs` (3 registration edits + 4 unit tests), `tests/fixtures/encoding_basic.gg` (new), `tests/integration.rs`, `docs/language-reference.md`. All 483 unit + 253 integration tests pass.
- [2026-02-19] Phase 27: Nested element drops for collection pre-drop. `compute_predrop_code()` now produces fully recursive drop trees for collection-typed elements, fixing a leak where overwriting e.g. a `Vector[Container]` inside `Vector[Vector[Container]]` via `.set()` freed only the inner Vector's buffer but leaked the Container elements inside it. Added `drop_action_for_type_id()` — parallel to `drop_action_for_type()` but operates on resolved TypeIds instead of AST Types, covering all collection/struct/Box types recursively. `compute_predrop_code()` now accepts an optional `elem_type_id` parameter; when present, it delegates to `drop_action_for_type_id()` → `gen_inline_drop_code()` for full recursive drops on collection types instead of buffer-only cleanup. Element TypeIds are extracted from the receiver expression at both call sites: `Vector.set()` (extracts element TypeId from `Generic(_, args)[0]`) and `Dict/HashMap.put()` (extracts value TypeId from `Generic(_, args)[1]`). Files: `c_stmt.rs` (`drop_action_for_type_id`, `compute_predrop_code`), `c_expr_methods.rs` (TypeId threading), `drop_struct_collection_fields.gg`, `integration.rs`. All 479 unit + 252 integration tests pass.

- [2026-02-19] Implement Parseable trait + primitive type static method calls. Parser now allows primitive type keywords (`int`, `float`, `bool`, `char`, `str`, etc.) in expression position, producing `Expr::Identifier`. - [2026-02-19] Phase 26: Recursive StructClone for Nested Struct Deep-Cloning. Added `StructClone(Vec<(String, CloneAction)>)` variant to `CloneAction` enum. `clone_action_for_type()` now recurses into non-collection struct types: looks up the struct's DefId, calls `compute_clone_fields()` (which itself calls `clone_action_for_type()` per field), and returns `StructClone` if any fields need cloning. This is naturally recursive — a struct containing a struct containing a Vector produces `StructClone([(inner, StructClone([(data, ArrayClone)]))])`. No cycle-safety needed since Gorget structs are value types (cyclic definitions are infinite-size and rejected earlier). Added `gen_clone_code_for_action()` helper that recursively generates clone code for any `CloneAction`, eliminating the manual per-variant match in `compute_push_clone_code()`. Now `.get()` on `Vector[Wrapper]` (where Wrapper contains a Container with a Vector[int] field) returns a deep-cloned copy with independent buffers, triggering `deep_cloned_expr` and full drop registration. Files: `src/codegen/mod.rs` (StructClone variant), `src/codegen/c_stmt.rs` (struct branch in `clone_action_for_type`, `gen_clone_code_for_action` helper), `tests/integration.rs` (updated expected output). All 479 unit + 251 integration tests pass.
- [2026-02-19] Phase 25: Field-Move-Zeroing for Function Call Arguments. Added `queue_call_arg_field_move_zeros()` in `c_expr_call.rs` — when a non-Copy struct field is passed by value to a function call, the source field is now zeroed via `memset` after the call, preventing double-free when the callee stores the shallow copy in a long-lived structure. Applied at the general function call path in `gen_call()` (constructor and method calls already had equivalent handling). Safety guards inherited from `queue_field_move_zero` → `resolve_field_zero_target`: parent must have StructDrop without user Drop, field type must be non-Copy, parent must not be a pointer parameter. Simplified the `deep_cloned_expr` VarDecl branch in `c_stmt.rs`: deep-cloned `.get()` results now register full drops for ALL types (including StructDrop with field drops), not just non-struct types. Previously, StructDrop cases only registered UserDrop (or nothing for structs without user Drop) because function call args could create aliased buffers — now safe because field-move-zeroing prevents the aliasing. This fixes a memory leak where deep-cloned struct results' collection-field buffers leaked at scope exit. Added Wrapper test case (struct WITHOUT Drop, Container field WITH Drop) to `drop_struct_collection_fields.gg`. Known limitation: `clone_action_for_type()` doesn't handle nested struct types (struct containing struct with Drop), so `.get()` on `Vector[Wrapper]` returns a shallow copy — tracked in TODO. Files: `c_expr_call.rs`, `c_stmt.rs`, `drop_struct_collection_fields.gg`, `integration.rs`. All 479 unit + 251 integration tests pass.
- [2026-02-19] Make interpolated strings return `String` (owned). Interpolated string literals (`"{x}"`) and `format()` now produce `GorgetString` (via `gorget_string_format()`) instead of `const char*` (via `gorget_format()`), fixing a memory leak (malloc'd strings never freed) and a double allocation (`String s = "{x}"` did `gorget_string_new(gorget_format(...))` — alloc + copy). Updated all three inference levels: type checker (`infer_expr`), codegen type inference (`infer_c_type_from_expr`, `resolve_expr_type_id`), and codegen expression generation (`gen_gorget_format_from_string_lit`, `gen_format_call`). Plain string literals remain `str`. `print("{x}")` unchanged (uses `printf()` directly). Existing `String ↔ str` coercion handles all downstream contexts: `str s = "{x}"` coerces via `.data`, `auto s = "{x}"` resolves to `String`, function args coerce via `coerce_arg_to_str`. Also fixed a pre-existing bug: `emit_monomorphized_equip_method` did not set `current_function_return_c_type`, preventing return coercion in monomorphized generic equip methods (e.g., derive-generated `display()` returning `format(...)` in a `str`-returning method). Made `coerce_return_value` `pub(super)` and added expression-body coercion in monomorphized methods. Files: `c_expr_print.rs`, `c_expr.rs`, `c_stmt.rs`, `typecheck.rs`, `c_item.rs`, `string_format.gg`, `integration.rs`. All 479 unit + 251 integration tests pass.
- [2026-02-19] Phase 24: Full drops for deep-cloned `.get()` non-struct types. The `deep_cloned_expr` VarDecl branch now registers full drops (ArrayFree, MapFree, SetFree, StringFree) for non-struct deep-cloned `.get()` results. Previously, ALL deep-cloned values got only UserDrop (or nothing), causing direct collection types from nested `.get()` (e.g., `Vector[Vector[int]].get(0).unwrap()`) to leak their buffers. StructDrop cases retain the existing UserDrop-only behavior — full field drops remain unsafe due to field aliasing via by-value function arguments (TOML parser's `toml_assemble` pattern: `sec.sec_path` passed to `toml_place_aot` creates aliased buffers that would double-free). Also fixed a pre-existing bug: `try_queue_field_move_zero_for_value` in VarDecl queued field-move-zeros AFTER the initial `flush_move_zeros()`, causing deferred zeros to emit inside the next conditional branch instead of unconditionally after the declaration. Added `self.flush_move_zeros(emitter)` in the field-move-zero VarDecl branch to flush immediately. Remaining struct field drop issue tracked in TODO. Files: `c_stmt.rs`, `drop_struct_collection_fields.gg`, `integration.rs`. All 479 unit + 250 integration tests pass.
- [2026-02-19] Fix Dict/HashMap struct field codegen. `ast_type_to_type_id()` in `c_expr_print.rs` now handles generic Named types (e.g., `Dict[str, int]`, `HashMap[str, int]`, `Vector[T]`) by looking up the DefId and recursively resolving type arguments, then finding the matching `ResolvedType::Generic` entry via a new `TypeTable::find_generic()` method. Previously, the function rejected all generic Named types (returned `None`), causing every downstream function (`infer_map_kv_types`, `is_gorget_map_expr`, `is_ordered_map_expr`, subscript codegen) to fall back to wrong defaults. This one-function fix unblocks Dict/HashMap as struct fields: `.put()`, `.get()`, `[key]` subscript, `.len()`, `.keys()`, `.contains()` all now generate correct C types. Files: `src/codegen/c_expr_print.rs` (generic Named branch in `ast_type_to_type_id`), `src/semantic/types.rs` (`find_generic` method), `tests/fixtures/dict_struct_field.gg` (new), `tests/integration.rs`. All 479 unit + 251 integration tests pass.

Fixed 3 bugs: (1) leading underscore in numbers (`_1000` was accepted as integer; now rejected in `yaml_is_int()` and `yaml_is_float()` for all bases — decimal, hex, octal, binary), (2) unknown escape sequences in double-quoted strings (`\q` preserved backslash; now errors with "unknown escape sequence"), (3) division-by-zero crash for `.inf`/`.nan` special floats (replaced `1.0/0.0` with `INFINITY`/`NAN` constants from `std.math`). Fixed `parse_block_value()` dispatch ordering: moved `looks_like_mapping()` check before quoted scalar handling so quoted keys (`"key": value`, `'key': value`) are recognized as mappings at the block level. Added 30+ new test cases covering: hex/octal/binary/underscore/negative/scientific numeric formats, positive sign (`+42`), special floats (.inf/-.inf/.nan), leading underscore rejection, unknown escape error, known escape sequences (`\n`, `\\`, `\"`), error paths (unterminated strings, unterminated flow collections, undefined alias), empty values, quoted keys, empty collections, plain scalar `#` handling, anchor redefinition, null/bool variants, nested flow collections, deeply nested blocks, multi-key mappings, and stringify of special values. - [2026-02-19] Implement `is`-pattern bindings in `if`/`elif`/`while` conditions. `if result is Ok(value):` now binds `value` in the then-body, `elif result is Error(e):` binds `e` in the elif-body, and `while iter is Some(item):` binds `item` each iteration. Reuses all existing match infrastructure: `define_pattern_bindings()` (resolve), `assign_pattern_types()` (typecheck), `mark_pattern_origins()` (borrow checker), `pattern_to_condition()` + `emit_pattern_bindings()` (codegen). Codegen for if-is evaluates scrutinee into a `__typeof__` temp, generates condition via `pattern_to_condition()`, and emits bindings inside the body. While-is generates `while(1) { tmp = scrutinee; if (!cond) break; bindings; body; }`. Elif-is wraps in `} else { tmp; if (cond) { bindings; body } }` following the same nesting pattern as string-temp elif. Negated `is not` never binds. `has_pattern_bindings()` helper distinguishes binding vs non-binding patterns. Files: `resolve.rs` (extract_is_pattern + define bindings in if/elif/while body scopes), `typecheck.rs` (assign_pattern_types for is-conditions), `borrow.rs` (mark_pattern_origins for is-conditions), `c_stmt.rs` (gen_if_is_binding, gen_elif_is_binding, gen_while_is_binding, has_pattern_bindings), `is_bindings.gg` + `integration.rs`. All 479 unit + 250 integration tests pass.
- [2026-02-19] std.yaml Phase 4 — Anchors, aliases, and merge keys. Added `&anchor` definitions, `*alias` references, and `<<` merge key support to the YAML parser. Anchors stored via parallel `Vector[str]`/`Vector[Yaml]` fields on `YamlParser` (Dict struct field avoided due to codegen limitation — method calls on Dict struct fields don't resolve types correctly, tracked in TODO). Added `parse_anchor_name()` and `parse_alias_name()` methods for reading anchor/alias identifiers (alphanumeric, `-`, `_`). Added `anchor_put()`, `anchor_get()`, `anchor_has()`, `anchor_clear()` helper methods. Anchor/alias dispatch in all three value parsers: `parse_block_value()`, `parse_inline_value()`, `parse_flow_value()`. Key design: inline anchor handler detects end-of-line after anchor name and delegates to `parse_block_value()` for block-level anchored values (e.g., `key: &anchor\n  nested: val`). Added `yaml_merge_into()` free function implementing YAML merge key spec — existing keys take precedence, supports both single mapping and sequence-of-mappings merge sources. Merge key dispatch in both `parse_block_mapping()` (3 sites) and `parse_flow_mapping()` (1 site). Anchors cleared per document via `anchor_clear()` in `parse_document()`. Integration tests cover: scalar anchor+alias, sequence anchor+alias, mapping anchor with merge key, existing-key precedence over merge, sequence-of-mappings merge, and flow-context anchor+alias. Files: `lib/std/yaml.gg`, `tests/fixtures/yaml_parse.gg`, `tests/integration.rs`, `TODO.md`, `DONE.md`. All 479 unit + 249 integration tests pass.

- [2026-02-19] Fix nested generic enum constructor resolution. `Some(Some(100))` and `Some(None())` for `Option[Option[int]]` now resolve each nesting level's mangled name correctly. Root cause: `resolve_unit_variant_from_type_hint()` used the outer `decl_type_hint` for all nested constructors. Fix: before generating field expressions for a single-type-param generic enum variant constructor, peel one layer off `decl_type_hint` (e.g., `Option[Option[int]]` → `Option[int]`), then restore after. Works recursively for arbitrary nesting depth. Multi-param generics (like `Result[T, E]`) are unaffected — the `generic_args.len() == 1` guard skips them. - [2026-02-19] std.yaml Phase 2 — Multi-line Block Scalars. Added `parse_block_scalar()` method to `equip YamlParser` supporting literal (`|`) and folded (`>`) block scalars with full chomping indicators (clip/strip `-`/keep `+`) and optional explicit indent width (`1`-`9`). Literal mode preserves newlines exactly; folded mode joins consecutive non-blank content-indent lines with spaces, preserves blank lines as paragraph breaks, and preserves more-indented lines literally. Extended `parse_plain_scalar()` for multi-line continuation — continuation lines (indented deeper than `min_indent`, not mapping keys or sequence items) are folded with spaces; multi-line plain scalars always resolve as strings (skip `yaml_resolve_scalar()`). Added `|`/`>` dispatch in both `parse_block_value()` and `parse_inline_value()`. Updated `yaml_pretty_internal()` to emit multi-line strings as literal block scalars (`|`) with proper indentation. Added `yaml_str_contains_newline()` helper. Integration test covers: literal block scalar, folded block scalar, strip/keep chomping, multi-line plain scalar, block scalar in sequence, folded with more-indented lines, and pretty-print round-trip with block scalars. Files: `lib/std/yaml.gg`, `tests/fixtures/yaml_parse.gg`, `tests/integration.rs`, `TODO.md`, `DONE.md`. All 479 unit + 249 integration tests pass.
- [2026-02-19] Implement `.enumerate()` for for-loops. Syntactic sugar that exposes a sequential index via tuple destructuring: `for (i, elem) in items.enumerate():`. Zero-cost universal counter approach — detects `.enumerate()` MethodCall in `gen_for_loop()`, unwraps to inner iterable, emits a `size_t` counter before the loop, and binds `int64_t idx = (int64_t)counter++` after element binding. Works for all iterable types (Vector, str, Set, Iterable trait, etc.) because the counter increments only when a valid element is reached (map/set `continue` for deleted entries happens before binding). Type checker extended to assign proper types to enumerate tuple pattern bindings (int for index, element type for value) — also fixes the broader issue that for-loop variables over Vector/Set didn't have type_id assigned, which would cause wrong printf format specifiers in string interpolation. Files: `typecheck.rs` (enumerate in `builtin_method_type()` + pattern type assignment in `Stmt::For`), `c_stmt.rs` (enumerate detection + counter in `gen_for_loop()`), `enumerate.gg` + `integration.rs`. All 475 unit + 248 integration tests pass.
- [2026-02-19] std.yaml Phase 1 — Core YAML 1.2 parser. Pure Gorget implementation (`lib/std/yaml.gg`, ~700 lines) following the same file-based module pattern as json/toml/xml. `enum Yaml: Null, Bool(bool), Int(int), Float(float), Str(str), Seq(Vector[Yaml]), Map(Dict[str, Yaml])`. Recursive-descent parser with indentation-tracked block collections (mapping/sequence), flow collections (`{}`/`[]`), double/single-quoted strings, plain scalars with auto-resolution (null/~/true/false, decimal/hex/octal/binary integers, floats including .inf/.nan), `#` comments (full-line and inline), `---`/`...` document markers. Public API: `yaml_parse(str) -> Result[Yaml, str]`, `yaml_stringify(Yaml) -> str` (compact flow-style), `yaml_pretty(Yaml) -> str` (block-style indent=2). Full accessor method suite matching `equip Json`: `get`, `at`, `has`, `has_index`, `as_str`, `as_int`, `as_float`, `as_bool`, `is_null`, `is_object`, `is_array`, `is_string`, `is_number`, `is_bool`, `len`, `keys`. All variable names use `yaml_` prefix to avoid `lookup_by_name_anywhere` cross-module collisions. Registered in `stdlib.rs` (3 insertion points: `is_stdlib_module`, `generate_stdlib_module`, `stdlib_module_source`). 4 new unit tests (is_stdlib_yaml, generate_yaml_returns_none, yaml_module_source_exists, yaml_source_parses). Integration test `yaml_parse.gg` covering block/flow collections, nested structures, quoted scalars, scalar resolution, comments, document markers, stringify, pretty print, all accessors, and error cases. Files: `lib/std/yaml.gg` (new), `src/stdlib.rs`, `tests/fixtures/yaml_parse.gg` (new), `tests/integration.rs`. All 479 unit + 248 integration tests pass.
- [2026-02-19] Add `Option.flatten()` method. `Option[Option[T]].flatten() → Option[T]` — unwraps one nesting layer. Type checker (`builtin_method_type()`) inspects `elem_type()` for nested `ResolvedType::Generic(Option_DefId, _)` and returns the inner Option TypeId directly. Codegen (`gen_option_method()`) uses `infer_generic_type_args(receiver)` to get the inner Option mangled name and emits: if outer is Some, return inner value; if outer is None, construct inner None. Type inference (`c_expr_generic.rs`) adds `flatten` to Option-returning method list for method chain support. Note: nested generic enum constructors like `Some(Some(100))` have a pre-existing bug where inner constructors resolve using the outer `decl_type_hint` — workaround is constructing inner Options as separate variables. Files: `typecheck.rs`, `c_expr_methods.rs`, `c_expr_generic.rs`, `option_result_combinators.gg`, `integration.rs`, `language-reference.md`. All 475 unit + 247 integration tests pass.
- [2026-02-19] Phase 23: Pre-Drop Old Values on Collection `set()`/`put()` Overwrite. When `Vector.set(i, val)` or `Dict/HashMap.put(key, val)` overwrites an existing element, the old value's non-Copy fields (String, Vector, Dict, Set, structs containing them) are now properly dropped before overwriting. Added `gen_inline_drop_code()` — a string-based counterpart to `emit_drop_entry()` that generates inline C drop statements without CEmitter, handling all DropAction variants recursively with `__pdi{depth}`/`__pde{depth}` loop variables. Added `compute_predrop_code()` parallel to `compute_push_clone_code()` — dispatches by C type name: direct collection types get buffer-only frees, struct types get full StructDrop with user drop + recursive field drops via `drop_action_for_struct_name()`. Vector `set()`: extracts old element with bounds check (`(size_t)__set_idx < recv.len`) before `gorget_array_set()`. Dict/HashMap `put()`: uses existing `__get_ptr()` to locate old value slot before `__put()`. Known limitation: nested element drops for direct collection values (e.g., `Vector[Vector[String]]`) only free outer buffer, not inner elements — tracked in TODO. Files: `codegen/c_stmt.rs`, `codegen/c_expr_methods.rs`, `tests/fixtures/drop_struct_collection_fields.gg`, `tests/integration.rs`. All 475 unit + 247 integration tests pass.
- [2026-02-19] Option[T] standardization batch 2: `Vector.pop()`, `Vector.remove()` → `Option[T]`; `Vector.index_of()`, `str.index_of()` → `Option[int]`. Same four-layer pattern as batch 1 (.get()): semantic return types in `builtin_method_type()`, codegen with `register_generic("Option", ...)` and bounds/sentinel checks, type inference in `builtin_method_return_type()` and `infer_generic_enum_mangled_name()`, receiver type mapping in `c_expr_generic.rs`. Pop codegen saves element before decrementing len (GORGET_ARRAY_AT bounds check). Remove codegen validates index in-range before extracting element. Index_of wraps the existing `gorget_array_index_of`/`gorget_string_index_of` sentinel (-1) in Option. Updated ~20 caller sites across stdlib (json.gg, ecs.gg, http.gg) and examples (md2html.gg, ecs/entity.gg) — value-capturing calls add `.unwrap()`, sentinel checks rewritten to `Option[int]` + `.is_some()`/`.unwrap()`. Void-context pop/remove calls (agentic_loop, snake, sparse_set) unchanged. Files: `typecheck.rs`, `c_expr_methods.rs`, `c_expr_print.rs`, `c_expr_generic.rs`, `language-reference.md`, 6 test fixtures, 3 stdlib .gg files, 2 example .gg files, `integration.rs`. All 475 unit + 246 integration tests pass.
- [2026-02-19] Phase 22: Deep-Clone on Collection Write/Read + Full Element Drops. Removed the `safe_element_drop()` safety filter that stripped collection-field drops from struct elements stored in collections (e.g., `Vector[TomlSection]` where `TomlSection` has Dict/Vector fields). Elements now get full drops including nested collection cleanup. To prevent double-frees from shallow copies, deep-clone infrastructure ensures collection elements independently own their buffers. **Write-side (push/set/put):** `source_is_safe_for_push()` checks whether the source expression will be move-zeroed (Identifier with drops, FieldAccess with zero target) or is a fresh allocation (function return, constructor call). Unsafe sources get deep-cloned via `compute_push_clone_code()` before insertion. **Read-side (.get()):** `.get()` deep-clones non-Copy elements, returning an independently-owned copy. The `deep_cloned_expr` flag signals VarDecl to register user Drop only (not field drops) to avoid double-frees when the deep-cloned result's fields are shallow-copied elsewhere (the TOML parser's get→modify→set pattern). **Clone infrastructure:** `gorget_string_clone()`, `gorget_set_clone()` in c_runtime.rs; `gorget_array_clone()` already existed; `{mangled}__clone()` for each monomorphized Dict/HashMap type in c_item.rs. `CloneAction` enum (`ArrayClone`, `StringClone`, `MapClone(String)`, `SetClone`) drives `compute_clone_fields()` which inspects struct field types to determine per-field clone actions. Known limitations: deep-cloned `.get()` results' collection-field buffers leak if not consumed (tracked in TODO); `set()`/`put()` doesn't pre-drop old values (tracked in TODO). Files: `codegen/mod.rs`, `codegen/c_runtime.rs`, `codegen/c_item.rs`, `codegen/c_stmt.rs`, `codegen/c_expr_methods.rs`, `tests/fixtures/drop_struct_collection_fields.gg`, `tests/integration.rs`. All 475 unit + 246 integration tests pass.
- [2026-02-19] Complete Option/Result method suite. Type checker: registered `map`, `and_then`, `or_else`, `filter`, `or`, `unwrap_or_else` for Option and `map`, `and_then`, `or_else`, `or`, `map_err`, `unwrap_or_else`, `unwrap_err` for Result in `builtin_method_type()`. Codegen: added `unwrap_or_else` (calls closure on None/Error), `filter` (turns Some to None if predicate fails), `or` (returns self if present, else alternative) for Option; `unwrap_or_else` (calls closure with error value on Error), `unwrap_err` (extracts error, panics on Ok), `map_err` (transforms error type), `or` (returns self if Ok, else alternative) for Result. Bonus fixes: (1) `infer_receiver_type` in `c_expr_generic.rs` now recognizes Option/Result combinator methods as returning the same type, enabling method chaining like `.or(x).unwrap()`. (2) Fixed `is_some`/`is_none`/`is_ok`/`is_err` operator precedence bug — non-temp comparison expressions now wrapped in parens to prevent cast misapplication in print interpolation. Deferred: `Option.flatten()` (needs nested generic type extraction), cross-type `map`/`map_err` type inference (needs closure body type in type checker). Files: `typecheck.rs`, `c_expr_methods.rs`, `c_expr_generic.rs`, `option_result_combinators.gg`, `integration.rs`. All 475 unit + 247 integration tests pass.
- [2026-02-19] Phase 21: Extended Field-Level Move-Zeroing (self.field + chained access). Replaced the hard-coded `Identifier`-only logic in `queue_field_move_zero()` with a recursive `resolve_field_zero_target()` helper that handles three cases: (1) `Identifier` — preserves identical behavior from Phase 20; (2) `SelfExpr` — enables `self.field` zeroing in mutable equip methods (`&self` / `!self`), generating `memset(&self->field, 0, ...)` to prevent double-free when extracting fields; (3) chained `FieldAccess` — resolves `obj.a.b` chains recursively, building the full C access path and type-checking at each level. Added `self_is_mutable: bool` field to `CodegenContext`, set from self parameter ownership in both `emit_function_def` (regular methods) and `emit_monomorphized_equip_method` (generic instantiations), with proper save/restore alongside `pointer_params`. Safety gates preserved: pointer params rejected, structs with user Drop rejected, only non-Copy fields zeroed. Also fixed pre-existing ownership violation in `LinkedList` and `SparseSet[T]` iterators — both created aliased non-Copy `Vector[int]` copies (shallow copy sharing the same buffer). Replaced with collect-into-fresh-vector pattern: `iter()` now traverses the data structure and populates a new `Vector[int]` with copied element values, giving the iterator sole ownership of its buffer. Extended `drop_field_move_zero.gg` with two new test cases: self.field extraction via `take_payload(&self)` method, and chained access `o.nested.items`. Files: `codegen/mod.rs`, `codegen/c_item.rs`, `codegen/c_stmt.rs`, `tests/fixtures/drop_field_move_zero.gg`, `tests/integration.rs`, `tests/fixtures/linked_list.gg`, `examples/linked_list.gg`, `lib/std/ecs.gg`, `examples/ecs/sparse_set.gg`. All 475 unit + 245 integration tests pass.
- [2026-02-19] Extend `String` with polymorphic `push()` and `push_line()`, plus capacity constructor `String(int)`. `push()` dispatches at codegen time based on argument type: `str`/`String` → `gorget_string_append`, `int` → `gorget_string_push_int`, `float` → `gorget_string_push_float`, `char` → `gorget_string_push_char`, `bool` → `gorget_string_push_bool`. `push_line()` mirrors the same dispatch with `\n` appended. No type-specific method names needed — one method handles all primitive types. C runtime: `gorget_string_with_capacity`, `gorget_string_push_int`, `gorget_string_push_float`, `gorget_string_push_bool`, `gorget_string_push_line` + typed variants. Constructor overload in `c_expr_call.rs` detects integer arg for capacity. Files: `c_runtime.rs`, `c_expr_call.rs`, `c_expr_methods.rs`, `c_expr_print.rs`, `string_builder.gg`, `integration.rs`. All 475 unit + 246 integration tests pass.
- [2026-02-18] Phase 20: Field-Level Move-Zeroing. When a non-Copy struct field is consumed (e.g., `items.push(h.data)` or `Vector[int] v = h.data`), the source field is now zeroed via `memset(&obj.field, 0, sizeof(obj.field))`, making the copy the sole owner. Previously, both the copy and the struct's scope-exit drop would free the same buffer → double-free. Implementation reuses existing `pending_move_zeros` + `flush_move_zeros()` infrastructure — pushing `"h.data"` generates valid C `memset(&h.data, 0, ...)`. Added `queue_field_move_zero()` helper with safety gates: only fires for simple Identifier objects, non-pointer-params, structs without user Drop (partial zeroing + user Drop = UB), and non-Copy field types. Extended all 4 consuming-site queue functions in `c_expr_call.rs` with `FieldAccess` branches alongside existing `Identifier` checks. Extended `Expr::Move` handler, Return handler (direct field access returns), and VarDecl drop registration (field-access RHS now gets full drops when source field is zeroed, instead of being skipped as "aliased buffer"). New integration test `drop_field_move_zero.gg` validates: push-field-into-collection (was double-free) and assign-field-to-variable (was leak). Files: `codegen/c_stmt.rs`, `codegen/c_expr_call.rs`, `codegen/c_expr.rs`, `drop_field_move_zero.gg`, `integration.rs`. All 475 unit + 245 integration tests pass.
- [2026-02-18] Interactive slash-command menu for TUI. Replaced cooked-mode `BufRead::read_line()` with raw-mode line editor using `crossterm` for key events. New `src/tui.rs` module with `read_line(prompt, show_menu) -> ReadLineResult` — enters raw mode with RAII `RawModeGuard` (Drop-based cleanup), handles full line editing (cursor movement, insert at cursor, backspace, delete, Home/End), and a box-drawn slash-command menu that appears above the prompt when `/` is typed at position 0. Menu filters as the user types, arrow keys navigate (with wrapping), Enter selects and executes, Tab fills without submitting, Escape dismisses. Ctrl+D = EOF on empty line, Ctrl+C = clear line. Menu rendered via full-redraw approach with `prev_render_lines` tracking and `ensure_menu_space()` for terminal scroll management. Six commands: `/run`, `/check`, `/show`, `/reset`, `/help`, `/quit`. Continuation prompt (`"... "`) uses `show_menu: false` for line editing only. Refactored `run_tui()` in main.rs to use `tui::read_line()`, removed `stdin.lock()` / `BufRead` import. Files: `Cargo.toml` (+crossterm 0.28), `src/tui.rs` (new), `src/main.rs`. All 475 unit + 244 integration tests pass.

Semantic layer: `builtin_method_type()` in typecheck.rs returns `Option[elem_type]` with `expr_types` insertion for codegen. Updated ~120 `.get()` call sites across stdlib (bytes, json, toml, xml, ssh, http, ecs), test fixtures, and examples with `.unwrap()`. Rewrote `bounds_check.gg` to test Option behavior. - [2026-02-18] Complete String→str coercion in all intercepted stdlib calls. Changed `gen_expr()` to `gen_str_arg()` for every `str`-typed parameter across all intercepted stdlib function calls in `c_expr_call.rs`. Previously only `parse_time` and `format_time` had this fix; now covers 35+ calls: filesystem ops (`read_file`, `write_file`, `append_file`, `file_exists`, `delete_file`, `mkdir`, `rmdir`, `rename`, `copy_file`, `file_size`, `is_dir`), path ops (`path_parent`, `path_basename`, `path_extension`, `path_stem`, `path_join`), OS ops (`getenv`, `setenv`, `readdir`), conversions (`parse_int`, `parse_float`), I/O (`input`, `exec`, `exec_output`), SDL string args (`sdl_create_window` title, `sdl_load_texture` path, `sdl_load_font` path, `sdl_render_text`/`sdl_draw_text` text, `sdl_text_width`/`sdl_text_height` text), crypto (`crypto_hmac` algo), networking (`socket_connect`/`tls_connect` host), and `File.open`/`File.create` path (both call sites). `gen_str_arg()` checks if the argument expression is `GorgetString` and coerces via `.data`; otherwise passes through unchanged. This prevents C compiler errors when users pass `String` values to intercepted stdlib calls. File: `c_expr_call.rs`. All 475 unit + 243 integration tests pass.
- [2026-02-18] Add math constants to `std.math`: `PI`, `E`, `TAU`, `INFINITY`, `NAN`. Constants are declared as `ConstDecl` items in the synthetic module (alongside existing function declarations) and mapped to `GORGET_*` C runtime constants via the existing `is_stdlib_const()` → `GORGET_{name}` codegen path. `PI`, `E`, `TAU` use `static const double`; `INFINITY` and `NAN` use `#define` to delegate to `<math.h>` macros. Refactored `gen_math_module()` to build items directly (mixed `ConstDecl` + `Function` items) instead of using `make_module()` which only accepts `FunctionDef`. New integration test `math_constants.gg` verifying values, arithmetic with constants, and trigonometric identity.
